### [46] [Extending Deep Event Visual Odometry with Sparse Point-Cloud Export](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22890)
*Alireza Safdari,Sajad Ashraf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过为DEVO视觉里程计系统集成稀疏点云导出流水线，在保持高效定位的同时，实现了实时3D场景几何的输出与可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DEVO虽然在事件相机视觉里程计（VO）方面表现卓越，但其输出主要局限于位姿轨迹，缺乏显式的3D场景几何输出，限制了其在可视化及后续空间感知任务中的应用。因此，需要扩展其功能以导出点云。

Method: 在现有的DEVO（Deep Event Visual Odometry）框架基础上，增加了一个稀疏点云导出流水线。该方法利用DEVO内部估计的3D结构信息，引入了一套数据导出、格式转换及点云清洗的实用工作流，从而生成显式的3D点云表示。

Result: 在BOARD SLOW序列上的实验表明，导出的稀疏点云在5厘米阈值下具有很高的精度，局部与EMVS重建结果一致；同时，系统也证实了在处理高密度、全局完整性以及长期位姿累积误差方面仍存在一定技术局限。

Conclusion: 本研究成功在不改变原有视觉里程计核心架构的基础上，为DEVO添加了稀疏点云导出功能，使其能够输出实时的3D几何场景，同时保持了原有的高性能定位能力。实验验证了其局部一致性与高精度，同时也指出了在密度、完整性及累积误差影响方面的局限。

Abstract: Event cameras are well suited for visual odometry under high-speed motion and challenging lighting conditions due to their low latency, high temporal resolution, and high dynamic range. Deep Event Visual Odometry (DEVO) demonstrated that monocular event-only odometry can achieve strong performance by combining sparse patch tracking, learned patch selection, recurrent correspondence refinement, and differentiable bundle adjustment. In this project, we extend DEVO with a sparse point-cloud export pipeline. Rather than modifying the core odometry formulation, our approach exposes the internal 3D structure already estimated by DEVO and converts it into an explicit point-cloud representation for visualization and further processing. In addition, we implement a practical workflow for data export, format conversion, and point-cloud cleanup. The resulting system preserves the original visual odometry pipeline while enabling sparse geometric scene output. Experiments on the BOARD SLOW sequence show that the exported sparse cloud is locally consistent with EMVS reconstructions, achieving high precision at a 5 cm threshold, while also highlighting the expected limitations in density, completeness, and sensitivity to accumulated odometry noise.

</details>


### [47] [Agentic-VLA: Efficient Online Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22896)
*Ruofan Jin,Zaixi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Agentic-VLA 通过引入自适应奖励合成、语言引导探索和经验记忆机制，显著提升了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操作中的在线适应能力、训练效率及泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在机器人操作中存在泛化能力差、样本效率低以及训练难度大等问题，亟需一种能够在线高效适应的训练框架。

Method: 提出了三种核心创新机制：1. 自适应奖励合成（Adaptive Reward Synthesis）：通过分解任务和课程学习进行奖励动态生成；2. 语言引导探索（Language-Guided Exploration）：利用判别器实现结构化探索；3. 经验记忆（Experience Memory）：存储和检索策略权重以支持快速冷启动。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准测试中，长视距任务提升 12.3%，单样本（1-shot）学习提升 28.5%，实现跨任务迁移性能从 0% 提升至 31.2%，收敛速度比现有方法快 2.4 倍；同时在 RoboTwin 2.0 双臂设置中表现优异。

Conclusion: Agentic-VLA 提供了一种高效的在线适应框架，实现了 VLA 模型在无需特定任务演示的情况下进行跨任务迁移和持续学习，是迈向通用机器人智能的重要进展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation by leveraging pre-trained vision-language representations. However, current VLA training methods suffer from two critical limitations: poor generalization to novel environments and low training efficiency requiring extensive demonstrations. We introduce Agentic-VLA, an agentic training framework that enables VLAs to efficiently adapt online through three key innovations: (1) Adaptive Reward Synthesis, which dynamically generates and adjusts reward functions based on the VLA's current capabilities and task complexity, decomposing complex tasks into learnable sub-goals for curriculum learning; (2) Language-Guided Exploration, where a critic model provides structured guidance for systematic exploration rather than random sampling; and (3) Experience Memory,which stores and retrieves task-relevant policy weights for warm-starting adaptation to similar tasks. We evaluate Agentic-VLA on the LIBERO benchmark, achieving substantial improvements: +12.3% on long-horizon tasks, +28.5% in 1-shot learning, and enabling cross-task transfer from 0% to 31.2% without task-specific demonstrations. Our framework also demonstrates 2.4x faster convergence compared to existing online adaptation methods. Beyond LIBERO, Agentic-VLA retains its advantage on the dual-arm RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark, including under its randomized Hard setting. These results establish Agentic-VLA as a significant step toward truly adaptive VLA systems capable of continuous learning in deployment.

</details>


### [48] [Robots That Know What to Ask: Recovering Misaligned Rewards through Targeted Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22986)
*Helena Merker,Nick Walker,Andreea Bobu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对从人类演示中学习奖励函数时的“特征欠指定”难题，研究提出了一种通过分析演示方差来定位薄弱环节，并以自然语言交互主动请求针对性演示的框架，有效提升了机器人的学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人为因素（认知负荷、操作难度）或数据集覆盖不足，从演示中学习奖励函数时，关键特征往往被“欠指定”（underspecified），导致学到的奖励函数存在歧义，最终引发部署时的行为偏差。

Method: 提出了一种检测欠指定特征的框架：通过分析多项演示中各特征的方差，识别出被演示者忽视或表达不明确的特征；通过自然语言向用户解释不确定性，并针对性地请求补充演示。

Result: 在模拟桌面操作环境和真实Franka机器人用户研究中，该方法证明了其能够有效识别并弥补特征缺失，在降低奖励函数歧义方面远超随机查询和被动学习方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了从不完美演示中学习奖励函数时的特征欠指定问题。利用统计信号主动查询特定特征的演示，显著提升了奖励函数的恢复精度，并增强了学习过程的样本效率，优于随机查询和被动数据采集。

Abstract: Learning reward functions from demonstrations assumes that demonstrations provide adequate supervision over all features -- or task-relevant aspects of behavior. In practice, demonstrations are often imperfect: humans may under-emphasize certain features due to cognitive load or physical difficulty, or the training regime may fail to sufficiently cover all relevant situations. In either case, important features may be underspecified, leading to ambiguity in the learned reward function and misaligned behavior at deployment. We propose a framework that detects such underspecified features and actively solicits targeted corrective demonstrations. Our key insight is that demonstrations implicitly reveal which features are well specified: features that are consistently optimized show little variation across demonstrations, while features that are underspecified vary widely. We leverage this statistical signal to infer which features may have been insufficiently demonstrated. The robot then explains which features it is uncertain about in natural language and queries for demonstrations that explicitly address the identified gaps. We evaluate our approach in a simulated tabletop manipulation domain and in a user study with a real Franka robot. Targeted, explanation-guided queries significantly improve reward recovery compared to random querying and passive data collection, reducing ambiguity that would otherwise persist in learning from imperfect demonstrations.

</details>


### [49] [Verified Task-Space Motion Planning Under Joint-Space Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22991)
*Hanjiang Hu,Changliu Liu,Yebin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人路径规划中的关节极限违规问题，该论文提出了一种基于二阶近似和S-procedure的自适应步长验证方法，使Bug2规划器能根据局部运动学条件动态调整步长，从而在复杂约束下实现安全且高效的运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统反应式任务空间规划器（如Bug2）使用固定步长且忽略关节角度限制，导致在雅可比矩阵病态时，规划出的步长常引发关节越限（Clipping），进而导致跟踪漂移甚至无法到达目标点。

Method: 1. 利用二阶多项式近似逆运动学；2. 使用S-procedure方法将约束转化为半定规划（SDP）；3. 提出一种基于二分法的快速求解流程，实现亚毫秒级的证书计算；4. 将该计算出的动态步长上限（λ*）集成到Bug2路径规划器中。

Result: 在94个对抗性场景测试中，该方法实现了0%的关节限制违规率及100%的目标达成率；相比之下，标准Bug2规划器在6-11%的步骤中出现违规，且在高达18%的场景中失败。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的基于SOS（平方和）验证的自适应路径规划器，通过实时计算可达笛卡尔超矩形，成功解决了传统Bug2算法在关节极限限制下的跟踪漂移和无法到达目标的问题，在保证零违规的前提下实现了高效的目标达成。

Abstract: Reactive task-space planners such as Bug2 operate with fixed Cartesian step sizes and are unaware of the manipulator's joint-angle limits. When the Jacobian is poorly conditioned, even small Cartesian steps can demand joint changes that exceed admissible bounds; clipping the joints to their limits causes tracking drift and can prevent goal reaching entirely. We address this by computing, at each planning step, the largest Cartesian hyperrectangle that is \emph{certifiably reachable} under joint displacement bounds. Using a second-order polynomial approximation of the inverse kinematics and the S-procedure, we formulate a small semidefinite program whose solution yields the certified half-width~$λ^\star$. An equivalent bisection procedure exploiting the quadratic structure solves the certification in sub-millisecond time. Integrating this certificate with Bug2 yields a planner whose step size adapts to local kinematic conditioning. In a statistical evaluation over 94 adversarial scenarios spanning six joint-limit settings, the SOS-verified planner achieves \emph{zero} joint-limit violations with a 100\% goal-reaching rate, whereas a standard Bug2 planner violates joint limits in 6--11\% of steps and fails to reach the goal in up to 18\% of scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [UfM*: Uncertainty from Motion* for DNN Depth Estimation Using Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23098)
*Soumya Sudhakar,Sertac Karaman,Vivienne Sze*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 UfM* 的高效不确定性估计算法，通过利用多视角下的高斯混合模型测量不一致性，在保持较低计算开销的同时，显著提升了机器人单目深度估计的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的不确定性估计方法（如集成学习或采样法）在单目深度估计任务中计算与存储开销巨大；且单帧预测无法衡量同一区域在不同视角下的预测不一致性，难以满足安全关键型机器人的实时性与资源受限需求。

Method: 提出 Uncertainty from Motion* (UfM*) 算法。该方法仅需单次 DNN 推理，通过比较当前视角与先前视图形成的紧凑高斯混合模型（Gaussian mixtures）来计算多视角间的不一致性（multiview disagreement），并结合偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty）进行估计。

Result: 在 ScanNet 数据集上，相比集成学习方法，UfM* 的期望校准误差（ECE）降低了 24-28%，能耗仅为前者的 3%，内存占用仅为 0.02%。在资源受限机器人的 Arm CPU 上实现了 30 FPS 的实时推理，功耗仅 63 mJ/图像。

Conclusion: UfM* 证明了通过使用高斯混合模型来度量多视角间的不一致性，是在资源受限的机器人系统上实现高效、可靠的不确定性估计的有效方案。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty estimation is critical for deploying monocular depth deep neural networks (DNNs) in safety-critical robotic systems. Conventional uncertainty methods such as ensembles and sampling-based approaches require multiple inferences per image, incurring substantial compute and memory overhead. Moreover, uncertainty predicted from a single image misses out on measuring disagreement between predictions across views of the same region. We propose Uncertainty from Motion* (UfM*), an uncertainty estimation algorithm that measures multiview disagreement efficiently by comparing previous and current views using a compact Gaussian mixture, requiring only a single DNN inference per image. Using Gaussians to compute multiview disagreement is not only more compute- and memory-efficient than a prior approach using a point cloud, but also improves uncertainty by measuring disagreement across regions of 3D space. UfM* paired with aleatoric uncertainty improves expected calibration error by 24-28% compared to an ensemble, while requiring only 3% of the energy and 0.02% of the memory on 100 out-of-distribution ScanNet sequences. We demonstrate UfM* consumes only 63 mJ per 224x224 image while running real-time at 30 FPS on an Arm Cortex-A76 CPU onboard a miniature energy-constrained robot, highlighting that measuring multiview disagreement using Gaussians enables efficient uncertainty for resource-constrained robotic systems.

</details>


### [51] [Four Simple Proprioceptive Estimators for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23100)
*Frank Dellaert,Chiyun Noh,Varun Agrawal,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对消费级IMU在足式机器人上的漂移问题，本文提出了从EKF到因子图及固定滞后平滑器的四种递进式状态估计方案，并开源了基于GTSAM和ROS2的实现以供研究使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人内置的消费级IMU由于噪声会导致状态估计漂移，需要利用足端与环境的间歇性接触约束来修正这种漂移。

Method: 开发了四种递进式状态估计器：1. 基于接触辅助的恒定不变扩展卡尔曼滤波 (Invariant EKF)；2. 将EKF测量更新替换为因子图(Factor Graph)；3. 采用固定滞后平滑器 (Fixed-lag Smoother) 结合接触周期落脚点；4. 引入随时间演变的IMU偏置的平滑器。

Result: 构建了一系列具有不同表达能力的状态估计变体，并验证了利用接触信息有效抑制惯性导航漂移的可行性，同时完成了开源代码适配。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一系列基于接触辅助的足式机器人状态估计器，通过在GTSAM框架与ROS2中开源实现，为运动学里程计的研究提供了可复现的基准工具。

Abstract: Legged robots carry an IMU, but the inertial solution drifts because consumer-grade IMUs are noisy. However, the feet create intermittent contacts with the environment that can be used to mitigate that drift. This report develops a sequence of increasingly expressive legged robot state estimators that leverage this. In all cases, the floating-base state comprises attitude, position, velocity, and IMU biases. To model foot contacts, we start from the contact-aided invariant EKF of Hartley et al., albeit at a reduced contact update rate. This is then augmented by replacing the measurement update by a small factor graph. Finally, we turn the same factors into a fixed-lag smoother with contact-episode footholds, with and without an evolving IMU bias. To facilitate reproducibility and further research in proprioceptive legged odometry, all four variants are available in GTSAM (Dellaert et. al), and we additionally provide a ROS2-compatible implementation.

</details>


### [52] [$π_0$-EqM: Equilibrium Matching for Closed-Loop Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23128)
*Huanming Liu,Congsheng Xu,Jianmin Ji,Yao Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对现有 VLA 模型动作解码器中采样步长固定导致计算效率低的问题，本文提出了 $\pi_0$-EqM，利用平衡匹配（EqM）替代流匹配，显著提升了机器人在复杂任务中的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 VLA 模型的生成式流匹配动作解码器通常采用固定的采样步长（sampling horizons），导致无法根据状态调整计算资源消耗，且难以在控制周期内进行时间重用，限制了机器人操作的泛化能力。

Method: 提出 $π_0$-EqM 模型，将原 $π_0$ 模型中的流匹配专家（flow-matching expert）替换为平衡匹配（Equilibrium Matching, EqM）解码器，同时保持上游 VLA 架构不变，以实现计算资源的动态分配和时间重用。

Result: 在 300 步预算下，RoboTwin 任务的平均成功率由 40.4% 提升至 50.2%（19 个任务）；在 LIBERO 基准测试中保持竞争力，其中 LIBERO-10 的成功率提高至 87.0%。

Conclusion: 推理深度在迭代式 VLA 控制中属于策略设计的一部分；引入了能量驱动的 VLA 视角，为跨任务和跨本体的组合动作生成提供了理论支持。揭示了推理预算（计算时间）与任务完成质量之间存在“平稳性-可执行性差距”（stationarity--executability gap）。

Abstract: Currently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become the most adopted paradigm for robotic manipulation for its great potential for task generalization. While most generative flow-matching action decoders for VLA control are often deployed with fixed sampling horizons, limiting state-dependent compute and temporal reuse across control cycles. We present $π_0$-EqM, which replaces the flow-matching expert in $π_0$ with an Equilibrium Matching (EqM) decoder while leaving the upstream VLA stack unchanged. Under a matched 300-step budget, $π_0$-EqM improves RoboTwin average success from 40.4% to 50.2% across 19 tasks and remains competitive on LIBERO, with its clearest gain on LIBERO-10 (87.0%). Two threshold scans reveal a task-dependent non-monotonic relation between residual and success, which we term the stationarity--executability gap. The results suggest that inference depth in iterative VLA control is part of policy design and introduce an energy-based VLA perspective that may inform future work on composable action generation across tasks and embodiments.

</details>


### [53] [Semantic-Aware Guided Drone Exploration for Language-Conditioned 3D Indoor Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23160)
*Nitin Vegesna,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAGE 是一种语义感知指导的 3D 探索系统，通过将 CLIP 语义信息无缝集成到几何探索代价函数中，在保持高空间覆盖效率的前提下，实现了对目标物体的高效探索与发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决开放词汇（open-vocabulary）环境下，自主移动机器人在未知 3D 室内空间中进行探索时，如何在保证覆盖效率的同时，实现针对特定感兴趣语义目标的快速定位与发现。

Method: 构建了 SAGE 系统，基于 FALCON 探索器，整合了 CLIP 模型。其核心机制包括：1. 以物体为中心的嵌入存储；2. 将近期观测投影至边界的临时缓存；3. 针对高相似度检测的“目标边界”；4. 引入统一的语义-几何路径规划代价函数，平衡了覆盖广度与语义优先级。

Result: 在 Matterport3D 仿真中，SAGE 在目标发现任务上优于 FALCON 和纯语义基准，且探索完成速度比 FTU 快 13.7 倍。真实环境飞行实验证明其在保持高覆盖率的同时，显著优于单纯的几何探索策略。

Conclusion: SAGE 成功地将语义引导与环境覆盖（exploration）相结合，在实现全覆盖任务的同时，极大地提升了特定目标物体的发现效率，并在仿真和真实无人机平台上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: We present Semantic-Aware Guided Exploration, SAGE, a system for open-vocabulary exploration in unknown 3D indoor environments that preserves coverage-oriented behavior while allowing semantic cues to reprioritize frontier selection. Building on the FALCON volumetric explorer, SAGE integrates Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) via four key components: object-centric embedding storage, a temporal cache that projects recent observations onto the free-unknown boundary, object frontiers for high-similarity detections, and a unified semantic-geometric planning cost. This cost function bounds semantic reweighting influence, ensuring frontiers are prioritized without sacrificing total coverage. In Matterport3D-based simulations, SAGE outperforms FALCON and a semantic-only ablation in object discovery across map-query pairs. Compared to Finding Things in the Unknown (FTU), SAGE completes exploration 9.0 to 25.9 times faster across the nine shared map-query pairs, achieving a mean speedup of 13.7. Furthermore, SAGE achieves substantially higher volumetric throughput than FTU. Finally, we deploy SAGE in five real-world flights in two environments on a Modal AI Starling 2 quadrotor with onboard sensing and planning, and offboard CLIP inference. Comparing SAGE and FALCON, we find that while FALCON results in faster exploration and shorter mapping trajectories, SAGE outperforms FALCON in terms of object discovery.

</details>


### [54] [Autonomous Frontier-Based Exploration with VLM Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23165)
*Aarush Aitha,Avideh Zakhor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用视觉语言模型（VLM）进行自主探索的轻量化流水线，通过语义决策替代几何启发式搜索，显著提升了机器人探索室内环境的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自主探索依赖几何启发式算法，缺乏对环境场景的上下文语义理解，难以高效地在未知与危险环境中进行高层战略规划。

Method: 提出了一种新型免训练的探索流水线：机器人将当前地图与各潜在探索路径（frontiers）的视觉信息打包为多模态提示（Multimodal Prompt），输入VLM进行高层次的战略决策，从而替代传统的几何启发式（Geometric Heuristics）搜索算法，指导底层机器人控制系统。

Result: 在六个室内模拟环境中的验证显示，该方法在地图覆盖率方面比现有基准方法提升了最高达 24%。

Conclusion: 本研究通过引入VLM实现了基于语义感知的自主机器人探索，证明了结合逻辑推理能力的决策方法能显著提升复杂环境下的覆盖效率，且具有极高的部署灵活性。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic exploration of unknown and hazardous environments, a long-standing challenge, can be significantly improved by leveraging the advanced reasoning of Vision-Language Models (VLMs). We introduce a novel exploration pipeline where a VLM performs high-level strategic decision-making, guiding a conventional low-level robotics control stack. At decision points, the robot generates a multimodal prompt with its current map and visual imagery of potential paths, or frontiers. The VLM analyzes this prompt to select the most promising frontier, replacing simple geometric heuristics with contextual spatial reasoning. This approach, validated in simulation across six indoor environments, improves map coverage by up to 24\% over existing methods. Our pipeline is lightweight, training-free, and easily transferable to any robot with standard sensors and an internet connection.

</details>


### [55] [Signal Temporal Logic Motion Planning via Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23240)
*Yu Chen,Ancheng Hou,Mingyang Feng,Xiao Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合定时自动机与凸集合图（GCS）的框架，将STL运动规划问题转化为凸优化求解，实现了高层逻辑约束下的平滑机器人轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在满足复杂的信号时序逻辑（STL）规范下，生成既满足高层逻辑/计时要求，又符合低层运动约束（如平滑度、速度限制）的机器人轨迹这一难题。

Method: 1. 将STL规范表示为定时自动机（Timed Automata）；2. 将自动机与配置空间的凸分解相结合，构建联合转换系统；3. 将运动规划问题重新表述为GCS上的最短路径问题，通过凸松弛求解；4. 生成满足高层逻辑和底层运动学约束的平滑Bézier样条轨迹。

Result: 证明了该方法在固定自动机和凸分解后的凸松弛复杂度随配置空间维度和Bézier阶数呈多项式增长；实验涵盖了从低维到30自由度类人机器人及UR-3机械臂，验证了方法的高效性和可行性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种将STL运动规划转化为凸集合图（GCS）上的最短路径问题的高效框架。该方法在理论上具有稳健性，在实践中通过实验验证了其在处理复杂逻辑任务和高维机器人系统生成平滑轨迹方面的有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates continuous-time motion planning under Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications. The goal is to generate smooth robot trajectories that satisfy high-level logical and timing requirements while respecting low-level motion constraints. To this end, we propose an efficient framework that combines timed-automata reasoning with graphs of convex sets (GCS). An STL specification is first represented by a timed automaton, which is then coupled with a convex decomposition of the configuration space to form a joint transition system encoding both task progress and region occupancy. Based on this joint transition system, the STL motion-planning problem is reformulated as a shortest-path problem over a GCS, whose solution induces a smooth Bézier-spline trajectory satisfying the STL specification, smoothness requirements, and velocity bounds. We establish the soundness of the proposed formulation and analyze its computational complexity, showing that, once the timed automaton and convex decomposition are fixed, the convex relaxation scales polynomially with the configuration-space dimension and the Bézier degree. We further develop a compact timed-automaton construction for an expressive STL fragment using dedicated templates and Boolean composition. Numerical experiments on low-dimensional benchmarks, a $3$-D quadrotor, a $30$-DoF humanoid, and a hardware experiment on a UR-3 robot arm demonstrate that the proposed method efficiently solves complex STL motion-planning problems and produces smooth executable trajectories.

</details>


### [56] [Turning Adaptation into Assets: Cross-Domain Bridging for Online Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23257)
*Zixuan Hu,Xuantuo Huang,Yancheng Li,Yichun Hu,Shengyong Xu,Ling-Yu Duan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 面对非平稳导航环境，IDEA 框架通过构建包含历史经验的“动态资产库”和“跨域桥梁”，将即时适应转化为资产积累与组合过程，有效解决了 VLN 场景下的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言导航（VLN）即时适应方法往往将在线适应视为瞬时、孤立的过程，导致模型在非平稳环境转换中出现灾难性遗忘和负迁移，难以适应复杂的现实部署环境。

Method: 提出了 IDEA（Inter-Domain BridgE with Historical Assets）框架：1. 利用 Fisher 信息量指导优化软提示（soft prompts）以提取可迁移知识；2. 构建包含“域坐标”的动态资产库；3. 通过将目标域投影到历史知识的凸包（convex hull）上构建跨域桥梁，形成资产优化与桥梁构建的互补循环。

Result: 在 REVERIE、R2R 及 R2R-CE 等基准测试中，IDEA 均优于现有先进方法，证明了其无需再训练即可实现跨域适应有效性的能力。

Conclusion: IDEA 通过“资产共享”机制实现了高效的训练即时适应（Test-Time Adaptation），克服了持续学习中的灾难性遗忘与负迁移问题，在多个VLN基准测试中表现卓越。

Abstract: Navigating under non-stationary environment shifts poses a critical challenge for a Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) agent deployed in the wild. Yet, existing Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) methods for VLN largely treat online adaptation as transient, isolated updates, leading to catastrophic forgetting and negative transfer. To overcome these issues, we propose Inter-Domain BridgE with Historical Assets (IDEA), a novel TTA framework that transforms adaptation into the accumulation and composition of assets. Specifically, IDEA introduces soft prompts optimized via a Fisher-guided weighting scheme to capture the transferable knowledge. These optimized prompts are then augmented with domain coordinates to form a dynamic asset library. Leveraging this library, IDEA constructs a cross-domain bridge by projecting the target domain onto the convex hull of historical knowledge. These designs form a complementary loop: the evolving library underpins bridge construction, while the bridge provides superior initialization to accelerate asset optimization. Extensive experiments across REVERIE, R2R, and R2R-CE benchmarks demonstrate the consistent superiority of IDEA over existing methods, showcasing its ability to enable training-free adaptation via asset sharing.

</details>


### [57] [Sparse Compositional Flow Matching by geometric assembly from motion primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23341)
*Yan Tang,Yuanbo Tang,Tingyu Cao,Shaolun Huang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种直接在物理轨迹空间中利用可复用“运动基元”进行组合生成的新框架，有效提升了具身智能体的运动轨迹生成精度与结构化水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前生成模型将轨迹视为单一密集信号导致的数据采样效率低和潜在结构缺失问题，提出通过组合式结构（即复用运动基元）来解决任务分解与轨迹建模的困难。

Method: 提出了一种结合“运动基元字典学习（Motion-Primitive Dictionary Learning）”和“结构化稀疏流匹配（Structural Sparse Flow Matching with Geometric Constraints）”的框架。该方法直接在物理轨迹空间中通过可学习的长度掩码和二进制放置矩阵进行原子化组合，而非传统的隐空间解码。

Result: 在Open X-Embodiment和3DMoTraj数据集上表现出SOTA水平，ADE/FDE性能分别提升了19.2%和21.0%，且FDE/ADE比率从1.8优化至1.07。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一个直接在物理轨迹空间进行组合的生成框架，证明了利用显式的学习运动基元和结构化稀疏流匹配能够显著提升具身轨迹生成的质量与解耦能力。

Abstract: Embodied trajectories, such as the executable motion sequences of robotic manipulators, underwater vehicles, and mobile robots, are a fundamental output of embodied AI. Modern generative models often treat them as a dense, monolithic signal generated point by point, fitting an intricate high-dimensional posterior while leaving the data's latent structure unmodeled, the same sample inefficiency long identified by the structured generative model literature. We argue that a compositional latent structure is a natural choice: many embodied tasks share recurring motion fragments that can be made explicit as a finite repertoire of reusable motion primitives, and compositional units naturally align with subtask boundaries to support task decomposition. Existing compositional generators, however, compose in a latent space and rely on post-hoc decoding to relate sampled units to actual trajectory segments. We instead compose directly in the physical trajectory space through a flow-matching framework with two coupled designs. Motion-Primitive Dictionary Learning equips each atom with a learnable length mask and binary starting indicators so the atom itself is the primitive, reused verbatim wherever it is placed. Structural Sparse Flow Matching with Geometric Constraints then generates a binary placement matrix using duration-aware tokenization and a differentiable geometric loss that enforces spatial continuity and temporal contiguity where adjacent primitives meet. On Open X-Embodiment and 3DMoTraj, the framework attains state-of-the-art accuracy and reduces the FDE/ADE ratio from 1.8 to 1.07, improving ADE by 19.2% and FDE by 21.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [58] [Multi-Floor Exploration for Ground Robots via an Incremental Reachable Graph and Structural Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23350)
*Zhiwen Zhu,Jiaqi Chen,Xiangyi Huang,Meiqi Hu,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于增量可达图的多楼层自主探索框架，通过整合先验信息与实时观测，实现了机器人对复杂多楼层环境的高效导航与建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统2D/2.5D地图无法有效表示楼梯、坡道等重叠的可通行区域，导致地面机器人难以处理多楼层环境下的自主探索任务。

Method: 提出一种基于增量可达图（Incremental Reachable Graph）的探索框架，通过在可达支撑表面上构建稀疏图来表示多楼层连通性；利用任务区域先验（Task-Zone Priors）在目标楼层初始化假设图，并通过分层规划器对确认结构与假设结构进行联合推理。

Result: 在仿真实验中显著提升了探索效率和地图完整性；通过实机部署验证了算法的实时性能与实际可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了基于增量可达图的框架能够有效解决多楼层环境的自动探索问题，其提出的分层规划策略在复杂环境下的探索效率和地图完整性方面均优于现有基准方法。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration of multi-floor buildings remains challenging for ground robots because conventional 2D and 2.5D maps cannot represent overlapping traversable surfaces such as stairs, ramps, and multiple reachable elevations. This letter presents a multi-floor exploration framework based on an incremental reachable graph. Built as a sparse graph over reachable support surfaces, the graph preserves potentially valid connectivity through tentative graph elements under sparse observations and enables stable, physically reachable frontier detection. To guide exploration beyond the currently mapped floor, we project task-zone priors from an explored floor to initialize a hypothetical graph on the target floor and reconcile it incrementally with incoming observations. A hierarchical planner then jointly reasons over confirmed and hypothetical structures for global guidance. In simulation, the proposed method demonstrates improved exploration efficiency and mapping completeness compared to evaluated baselines. Furthermore, onboard real-world experiments validate its practical feasibility and real-time performance.

</details>


### [59] [Droneulator: A Portable UAV Simulator for Agricultural Workflows with RotorPy and Godot 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23386)
*Jacob Swindell,Michael Lowen,Marija Popovic,Riccardo Polvara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Droneulator，一个基于 Godot 4 和 RotorPy 的高保真、可移植无人机模拟架构，支持农业场景下的图像采集、路径规划及强化学习，并完成了从仿真到 ROS 2 环境的无缝集成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业无人机研究亟需一个既具备高保真度（动力学与视觉），又具备轻量化部署特性，且能兼容主流机器人开发工具的统一模拟器平台。

Method: 提出 Droneulator 框架，结合 RotorPy（多旋翼动力学）与 Godot 4（高性能 3D 渲染及传感器生成）；通过 Zenoh 实现 ROS 2 兼容的流水线，支持 PX4 控制与轻量级 WebSocket 通信。

Result: 通过树木影像重构任务、 canopy 障碍物局部路径规划（EGO-Planner）以及基于深度感受的强化学习策略训练，验证了系统在低延迟传感、环境交互及闭环策略训练方面的有效性。

Conclusion: Droneulator 提供了一个高效、可移植且功能全面的农业无人机模拟栈，成功将多旋翼动力学、3D渲染及机器人中间件集成在一起，能够满足农业场景中视觉监测、路径规划与强化学习的多样化研发需求。

Abstract: Agricultural UAV research requires simulators that integrate realistic 3D scenes, high-fidelity vehicle dynamics, and robotics middleware, while remaining practical to deploy across heterogeneous development machines. We present Droneulator, a portable UAV simulator architecture that combines RotorPy for multirotor dynamics with Godot 4 for rendering and sensor generation. Droneulator exposes both PX4-based control and a lightweight WebSocket command path, and publishes synchronised visual and state streams through a Zenoh-based ROS~2-compatible pipeline. This integration enables a single stack to support inspection-oriented data capture, ROS~2/PX4 local planning, and reinforcement learning experiments without modifying the simulator infrastructure. We present quantified validation of the current system across three agricultural UAV workflows: tree-scale image collection for 3D reconstruction with COLMAP, local planning around canopy obstacles using EGO-Planner, and closed-loop reinforcement learning through a custom Gymnasium environment. In the reported setup, the results show that the simulator can sustain low-latency sensing, support reconstruction-oriented data collection under varying capture density, execute collision-free local planning around canopy obstacles, and support stable depth-sensing-based policy training for obstacle-aware navigation. Together, these results show the potential of Droneulator for agricultural UAV inspection, planning, and learning within one deployable stack.

</details>


### [60] [Vision-Based Agile Landing on Turbulent Waters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23717)
*Dimosthenis Angelis,Leonard Bauersfeld,Davide Scaramuzza,Evangelos Boukas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种无需显式获取船舶运动状态、仅依赖机载测量与视觉特征的无人机海上着陆强化学习方法，实现了复杂海况下的稳定自主着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在海上运动平台着陆受到平台与飞行器耦合运动的影响，传统的基于平台状态估计的方法在复杂海况下难度大且鲁棒性受限，研究旨在摆脱对显式平台状态信息的依赖，提高着陆的稳定性和适应性。

Method: 提出了一种基于强化学习（RL）的控制策略，通过无人机自身状态测量与基于视觉的关键点/描述符特征来直接预测姿态与推力指令，并利用模拟环境中的合成关键点训练，实现了针对不同特征提取器的零样本（zero-shot）迁移。

Result: 在模拟仿真中超过了最先进的MPC基准（即使在“非常恶劣”的海况下）；在真实场景中，通过两种不同的视觉特征提取器成功验证了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种无需显式平台状态信息的强化学习着陆方法，在极度恶劣的海况下实现了无人机在运动船舶上的自主着陆，在模拟与真实实验中均优于现有的模型预测控制（MPC）基准。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles on maritime vessels is challenging due to the coupled motion of the vehicle and landing platform in open-sea conditions. This paper presents a reinforcement-learning-based approach for autonomous multirotor landing on moving maritime platforms without requiring explicit platform-state information. The proposed method uses multirotor state measurements together with local visual features, consisting of keypoints and associated descriptors extracted from the landing surface, to predict attitude and thrust commands. These commands are tracked by a conventional low-level controller. The policy is trained in simulation using synthetic keypoints with randomly generated normalized descriptors, enabling zero-shot deployment with different local feature extractors onboard the UAV. We evaluate the method in a realistic simulator and show that it outperforms a state-of-the-art Model Predictive Control baseline under platform motions corresponding to ``Very Rough'' sea conditions. Finally, we perform extensive real-world experiments, demonstrating autonomous onboard landing using two different local feature extractors. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first approach for agile multirotor landing on maritime platforms in turbulent waters that does not rely on an explicit platform-state representation.

</details>


### [61] [Direct Dynamic Retargeting for Humanoid Imitation Learning from Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23762)
*Constant Roux,Ludovic De Matteïs,Armand Jordana,Valentin Guillet,Nicolas Mansard,Olivier Stasse,Philippe Souères*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决人形机器人模仿学习中的形态差异和几何偏差问题，本文提出了Direct Dynamic Retargeting (DDR)，通过物理仿真和MPC直接从视频生成动态可行轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习方法（如几何或间接动态重定向）存在形态差异带来的几何偏差，限制了搜索空间，导致人形机器人动作表现次优。

Method: 提出一种名为“直接动态重定向（DDR）”的单阶段框架，绕过几何重定向，直接在任务空间利用基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）求解器，在物理仿真器中优化复杂接触序列。

Result: 实验表明，DDR在演示追踪准确性上超越现有最先进方案，并能显著加快强化学习训练收敛速度，提升动作执行性能。

Conclusion: DDR框架通过消除几何偏差，提高了演示追踪精度，并为强化学习提供了更好的参考轨迹，从而提升了人形机器人敏捷和平衡行为的训练效率与执行效果。

Abstract: Imitation Learning from monocular video demonstrations provides a scalable approach for teaching complex skills to humanoid robots. However, translating human motion to humanoids requires overcoming significant morphological mismatches. Standard approaches rely on Geometric Retargeting or Indirect Dynamic Retargeting pipelines. We identify that these intermediate kinematic projections introduce a geometric bias, restricting the search space and yielding suboptimal dynamic behaviors. In this paper, we propose Direct Dynamic Retargeting (DDR), a novel single-stage framework that generates high-fidelity, dynamically feasible trajectories directly from expert videos. By formulating the problem in the task space and leveraging a sampling-based Model Predictive Control solver within a physics simulator, DDR natively optimizes over complex contact sequences while mitigating input drift. Our experiments demonstrate that bypassing the geometric bias allows DDR to outperform state-of-the-art baselines in demonstration tracking accuracy. Furthermore, we establish that providing such physically viable references to RL agents accelerates training convergence and enhances the final execution of agile and balancing behaviors. Source code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [62] [Instrumentation for Imitation Learning: Enhancing Training Datasets for Clothes Hanger Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23847)
*Remko Proesmans,Thomas Lips,Francis wyffels*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过在物体中集成传感器（Instrumentation）来补充机器人操作任务中的状态信息，有效提升了仿生学习的效率和表现，并证明了视觉基模型可以通过训练数据增强达到类似传感器的控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大行为模型在机器人操作领域面临的数据需求过高问题，提出利用传感器集成（Instrumentation）来降低数据依赖并提升学习效率。

Method: 通过在衣架中集成传感器获取物体的状态信息；使用扩散策略（Diffusion Policies）对比“仅视觉（vision-only）”与“传感器辅助”两种方案；利用传感器数据扩展遥控演示数据集，并通过知识蒸馏训练视觉学生模型。

Result: 传感器辅助的策略在衣架插入任务中表现优于视觉模型（提升14-25个百分点）；黑盒仿生策略能够自动优先处理传感器信号；结合传感器专家策略的辅助训练，视觉学生模型的性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 实验证明传感器集成（Instrumentation）是提升机器人仿生学习效率的有效策略。通过引入物体传感器数据，视觉导向的策略能够实现性能超越，并展示了通过蒸馏技术（利用传感器数据作为专家信号训练视觉模型）来提高数据效率的潜力。

Abstract: Large behaviour models have transformed the field of robotic manipulation, but prohibitive data requirements have thus far prevented a revolution similar to vision language models. We believe that instrumentation, i.e. sensor integration in objects, can provide invaluable state information and enable efficient learning for robotic manipulation. In this paper, we present instrumented imitation learning of clothes hanger insertion. Using 180 teleoperated demonstrations, we train diffusion policies with and without access to instrumentation data. Results show that policies leveraging instrumentation outperform vision-only counterparts by 14-25 %pt and exhibit greater task awareness. Crucially, a black-box imitation learning policy learns to prioritise instrumentation signals without explicit guidance. In addition, enhancing the teleoperation dataset with rollouts from an instrumented expert policy, enables a vision-only student policy to achieve performance comparable to the instrumented expert, thereby surpassing the original vision-only policy. These findings establish instrumentation as a promising strategy to enhance imitation learning for robotic manipulation. Datasets are available on Zenodo.

</details>


### [63] [Point Tracking Improves World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23856)
*Jiarui Guan,Wenshuai Zhao,Yue Pei,Ziliang Chen,Arno Solin,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JOPAT 是一种新型世界-动作模型，通过结合像素预测与 2D 点轨迹追踪，有效解决了纯像素模型对视觉干扰敏感的问题，显著增强了机器人处理复杂动态任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的像素级预测模型容易受到光照、纹理等与任务无关的干扰因素影响（噪声），导致视觉表征在处理长跨度、遮挡或物体交互任务时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 提出了 JOPAT（JOint Pixel-And-Track World-Action Model），这是一种基于去噪扩散 Transformer 的模型。它不仅预测潜在视觉表征，还显式地预测 2D 点轨迹及其可见性，从而解耦了动态信息与无关的视觉扰动。

Result: 在 LIBERO 和 LeRobot 真实世界任务中，JOPAT 的表现均优于传统的纯像素级基准模型，特别是在长跨度、遮挡和物体动态交互场景下优势显著。

Conclusion: 通过引入“轨迹（Tracks）”作为对像素预测的补充，JOPAT 有效提升了机器人策略在长跨度、遮挡以及复杂物体交互任务中的鲁棒性与表现。

Abstract: Robot policy learning benefits from world-action models that capture environment dynamics, but pixel-level prediction entangles dynamics with nuisance factors such as lighting and texture, making learned representations vulnerable to task-irrelevant visual variation. We propose JOPAT, a JOint Pixel-And-Track World-Action Model that predicts latent visual observations, 2D point tracks with visibility, and actions in a single denoising diffusion transformer. The key insight is that tracks provide an explicit representation of motion that captures long-horizon dynamics and remains robust under occlusion or partial out-of-frame motion, offering greater utility than modeling pixel appearance alone. On LIBERO and real-world LeRobot tasks, JOPAT improves over pixel-based baselines, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks involving occlusion, object interaction, and off-screen motion.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [64] [KPI2KVI: A Multi Agent Workflow for Calculating Key Value Indicators from Service Descriptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22825)
*Masoud Shokrnezhad,Tarik Taleb,Yan Chen,Qize Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KPI2KVI是一个基于大模型的多智能体工具，旨在自动化实现从非结构化服务描述到可评估、可解释的关键价值指标（KVI）的转换过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际应用中，手动根据非结构化文档定义和计算KVI不仅耗时，而且由于缺乏统一标准，导致结果存在不一致性和难以追溯的问题。

Method: 提出了一款名为KPI2KVI的工具，利用大模型（LLM）驱动的确定性多智能体工作流，包括：(1)补充服务上下文；(2)从分类体系中提取并确定KVI类目；(3)生成具体的KPI指标及其定义；(4)通过交互式对话收集或预测KPI值；(5)计算并生成具备可追溯解释的区间型KVI输出。

Result: 模拟实验表明，KPI2KVI能够始终如一地生成从服务描述到KVI区间的完整映射，并提供透明的计算叙述，支持后续审计和交互式咨询。

Conclusion: KPI2KVI通过大模型驱动的多智能体工作流，实现了从非结构化服务文档到关键价值指标（KVI）的自动化转化，有效解决了手动计算KVI时效率低下和不一致的问题。

Abstract: Key Value Indicators (KVIs) provide a decision oriented view of a service by summarizing how operational performance translates into stakeholder value, risk, and outcomes. However, in many domains KVIs are difficult to compute in practice because they require selecting relevant KVI categories, defining measurable Key Performance Indicators (KPIs), collecting KPI values, and applying consistent calculation logic, all of which is typically performed manually and inconsistently from unstructured service documentation. This paper presents KPI2KVI, a tool that transforms a natural language service description into computed KVI estimates by orchestrating a deterministic multi agent workflow powered by Large Language Models (LLMs) that (i) elicits missing service context, (ii) extracts and finalizes relevant KVI categories from a taxonomy, (iii) generates service specific KPIs with units and descriptions, (iv) collects KPI values through an interactive dialogue and also supports intelligent estimation for KPI values that are unavailable, and (v) computes interval valued KVI outputs (minimum, exact, maximum) with traceable explanations for each KVI code. Simulations with representative service descriptions demonstrate that KPI2KVI consistently produces a complete end to end mapping from description to KVI intervals and provides transparent calculation narratives that support post hoc auditing and interactive advisory queries.

</details>


### [65] [An AI-Driven Framework for Energy-Efficient Environmental Monitoring in Smart Cities Using Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22824)
*Yichen Liu,Imam Akintomiwa Akinlade,Xiaochong Jiang,Wenting Yang,Shiqi Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于边缘智能和TinyML的动态感知框架，通过环境上下文感知动态激活传感器，有效解决了大规模智慧城市环境监测中的高能耗与传感器短寿命问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模智能传感器部署面临能耗过高、数据冗余以及传感器寿命受限等问题，影响了环境监测的可持续性。

Method: 提出一种基于TinyML和边缘智能的分层架构，利用上下文感知和效用函数（考虑实时环境、位置、剩余电量）进行动态传感器激活决策。

Result: 基于城市规模仿真实验表明，该机制在保持高监测覆盖率的前提下，相比静态、周期性及UCB动态感知策略，显著降低了能耗并延长了传感器寿命。

Conclusion: 通过边缘智能和上下文感知决策，AI驱动的框架能够显著降低智能城市环境监测系统的能耗并延长传感器寿命，是构建可持续智慧城市监控系统的有效途径。

Abstract: Environmental monitoring is a crucial component of the smart city infrastructure. It enables informed decision making which enhances sustainability, public health and urban planning. However, the large-scale deployments of the smart sensors have raised concerns on excessive energy consumption and redundant data collection as well as limited sensor lifespan. To resolve these issues, we present an AI-driven framework for energy-efficient environmental monitoring in smart cities utilizing edge intelligence. Our proposed framework leverages TinyML-enabled edge devices and context-aware adaptive decision-making in order to dynamically activate the sensors based on the spatiotemporal conditions, environmental statistics and energy constraints. The sensors will be dynamically activated based on a utility function that takes in factors such as real-time environmental conditions, sensor location, and remaining battery lifespan. Our framework will reduce unnecessary sensing and communication while maintaining high coverage for monitoring. We introduce a hierarchical Edge Intelligence architecture to support deployments in city-wide scales. We conducted evaluation using a city-scale simulation driven by real multi-sensor environmental traces, which demonstrates that the proposed mechanism significantly reduces energy consumption and extends sensor lifespan when compared to static, periodic, and UCB-based adaptive sensing strategies. The results highlight the potential of edge intelligence and adaptive AI techniques for building sustainable and efficient smart city monitoring systems.

</details>


### [66] [Monte Cimone v3: Where RISC-V Stands in High-Performance Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22831)
*Emanuele Venieri,Simone Manoni,Giacomo Madella,Federico Proverbio,Federico Ficarelli,Luca Benini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了基于SOPHGO SG2044处理器的RISC-V HPC集群MCv3，评估显示其能效比已达到主流服务器水平，且在向量维度归一化后具备与主流x86和Arm高性能芯片竞争的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估第三代Monte Cimone RISC-V HPC集群（MCv3）的性能与能效，验证新一代SOPHGO SG2044处理器在高性能计算场景下的实际表现。

Method: 使用HPL（高性能Linpack）和STREAM基准测试对Monte Cimone v3（基于SOPHGO SG2044处理器）进行评估，并结合功耗测量；将结果与Intel Xeon Platinum 8480+（Sapphire Rapids）和NVIDIA Grace CPU Superchip进行对比。

Result: 1. SG2044单核性能较前代SG2042翻倍且可扩展性更好；2. MCv3能效比达3.08 GFLOPs/W，较MCv1提升10倍，达到主流x86-64/Arm服务器水平；3. 在归一化SIMD/向量长度后，MCv3（16核峰值效率点）性能达到Intel Sapphire Rapids服务器的46%，NVIDIA Grace的91%。

Conclusion: MCv3证明了RISC-V架构在高性能计算领域的可行性与竞争力。尽管在绝对性能上与顶尖服务器芯片仍有差距，但其能效比已经追平了主流x86-64和Arm服务器水平，标志着RISC-V向高性能计算迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: The Monte Cimone project provides a RISC-V testbed for High-Performacne Computing cluster. This paper presents Monte Cimone v3 (MCv3), the third iteration of the Monte Cimone RISC-V HPC cluster, integrating the SOPHGO Sophon SG2044 processor, an evolution of the SG2042 used in MCv2. We characterize MCv3 using HPL and STREAM benchmarks coupled with power measurements, and compare it against two reference platforms: the Intel Xeon Platinum 8480+(Sapphire Rapids) and the NVIDIA Grace CPU Superchip. Our results show that the SG2044 more than doubles single-core performance and improves scalability compared to SG2042. MCv3 achieves an energy efficiency of 3.08GFLOPs/W which improves of 10x w.r.t. MCv1 and is in the range of x86-64 and Arm servers. On pure performance when normalized on the SIMD/Vector length MCv3 on its peak efficiency point (16 cores) achieves 46% performance of Intel Sapphire Rapids server and 91% performance of NVIDIA Grace CPU superchip.

</details>


### [67] [ObjectCache: Layerwise Object-Storage Retrieval for KV Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22850)
*Yu Zhu,Aditya Dhakal,Yunming Xiao,Dejan Milojicic,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ObjectCache 是一项将 LLM 前缀 KV 缓存卸载至对象存储的方案，通过协同调度和计算-传输重叠技术，在几乎不增加 TTFT 延迟的情况下，解决了大模型内存容量瓶颈并降低了 Serving 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 LLM 推理上下文长度增加，共享前缀的 KV 缓存占用内存过大，现有的将 KV 缓存存储在远程 DRAM 中的做法导致Serving集群规模大、成本高。为打破容量瓶颈，研究者探索将 KV 缓存卸载至成本更低、容量更大的 S3 对象存储中。

Method: 构建了 ObjectCache 系统，核心在于协同设计存储协议与传输调度（Transfer Schedule）。该机制确保存储服务器按 GPU 消费顺序传输 KV 缓存，并利用并发请求实现计算与数据传输的重叠（Overlapping）。基于 NIXL、Ceph RGW 和 DAOS 在 100 Gbps RoCE 集群上进行了原型开发。

Result: 在 64K 上下文场景中，相比本地 DRAM，ObjectCache 仅增加 5.6% 的延迟；在 4K 上下文且计算掩盖传输较难的情况下，额外延迟仅为 56-75ms。其调度器在共享带宽限制下，将 TTFT 额外开销降低了 1.2-1.8 倍。

Conclusion: ObjectCache 有效解决了大语言模型推理中前缀 KV 缓存超出 GPU/本地内存限制的问题。通过存储协议与传输调度的协同设计，该方案能够将 KV 缓存从本地/远程内存迁移至对象存储（S3），在维持极低 TTFT 额外开销的同时，极大降低了集群存储成本和规模。

Abstract: Prefix KV caching has become a key mechanism in LLM serving: it reduces time to first token (TTFT) by avoiding redundant computation across requests that share a prefix (i.e., the system prompt). However, the accumulated KV cache is often larger than what GPU memory and local DRAM can hold. To preserve latency, current systems keep the KV cache in remote DRAM pools, increasing serving-cluster size and cost. In this paper, we explore a different approach: storing the KV cache in S3-compatible object storage so that capacity is no longer the constraint, while minimizing the impact on TTFT. We propose ObjectCache, which co-designs the storage protocol and transfer schedule so that the storage server delivers KV cache data in the order the GPU consumes it, overlapping data transfer with compute across concurrent requests. We prototype ObjectCache on a 100 Gbps RoCE cluster with NIXL (an inference library that abstracts storage and memory), Ceph RGW (an Object Gateway for clusters), and DAOS (an open source storage system). For 64K contexts, common in today's systems, ObjectCache adds only 5.6\% latency over local DRAM; for 4K contexts, where less compute is available to mask transfer, ObjectCache adds 56--75\,ms over the optimal local layerwise baseline. Under shared bandwidth caps, our scheduler reduces added TTFT by 1.2--1.8x compared with equal bandwidth sharing.

</details>


### [68] [Budgeted Dynamic Trace Structures for Token-Efficient Sequential Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22879)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BDTS 是一种受资源预算约束的高效数据结构框架，通过多种压缩和缓存机制，实现了大规模顺序计算轨迹的实时管理与显著压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着顺序计算产生的轨迹日益复杂（包含嵌套分支、状态转换、文本载荷及历史摘要），如何在有限的内存或令牌预算下有效存储、检索和管理这些大规模轨迹成为挑战。

Method: 引入了“预算动态轨迹结构”（BDTS）框架，集成了状态过滤可达性、游标分页、软上限最近度日志、引用计数观测键、增量覆盖（delta overlays）、有界成本缓存以及摘要-后缀压缩技术。

Result: 在 10,000-40,000 个顶点的合成轨迹上，构建时间为 0.58-2.72 ms，后代枚举为 0.24-1.42 ms；能将 350k-2.71M 的历史压缩至 1k-4k 令牌，在实际模型目标测试中将约 3,360 个令牌压缩至 432-433 个。

Conclusion: BDTS 成功证明了在严格的资源预算约束下，通过多种数据结构优化（如增量覆盖、压缩与软上限日志）可以高效管理大规模、高复杂性的顺序计算轨迹，为长上下文处理提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Sequential computation increasingly produces long traces containing nested branches, status transitions, textual payloads, and compact summaries of earlier execution. This paper introduces budgeted dynamic trace structures (BDTS), a data-structural framework for maintaining rooted trace graphs and append-only histories under an explicit byte or token budget. BDTS combines status-filtered reachability, cursor pagination, soft-capped recency logs, reference-counted observation keys, delta overlays, bounded cost caches, and summary-plus-suffix compaction. We give formal invariants, asymptotic bounds, and an ancillary Rust implementation with reproducible benchmarks. Across synthetic traces with 10,000-40,000 vertices, the prototype builds graphs in 0.58-2.72 ms, enumerates all descendants in 0.24-1.42 ms, and compacts histories of 350k-2.71M approximate tokens to 1,048-4,120 approximate tokens. Tokenizer and forward measurements with three public model targets reduce 3,359-3,360 trace tokens to 432-433 tokens.

</details>


### [69] [Orbax: Distributed Checkpointing with JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23066)
*Colin Gaffney,Shutong Li,Daniel Ng,Anastasia Petrushkina,Niket Kumar,Adam Cogdell,Mridul Sahu,Yaning Liang,Nikhil Bansal,Justin Pan,Angel Mau,Abhishek Agrawal,Marco Berlot,Ruoxin Sang,Kiranbir Sodhia,Rakesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Orbax 是一个专门为 JAX 设计的高性能模块化检查点库，旨在简化分布式系统中的模型状态保存与加载，其性能表现显著优于 PyTorch 的现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JAX 虽然在高性能分布式机器学习中占据重要地位，但缺乏标准化的检查点机制，且现有的实现往往难以处理分布式加速器系统的复杂性。

Method: 开发了一个 JAX 原生的模块化检查点库 Orbax，通过抽象分布式加速器系统的复杂性，支持灵活的检查点操作，并针对分布式环境进行了深度性能优化。

Result: Orbax 在检查点保存性能上比 PyTorch 的同类产品最高提升了 3.5 倍，在加载性能上提升了 2 倍。

Conclusion: Orbax 为 JAX 生态系统提供了一个模块化、高性能且标准化的检查点（checkpointing）解决方案，有效解决了 JAX 原生缺乏统一保存/加载机制的问题。

Abstract: In a landscape of high-performance distributed ML systems, JAX has emerged as a framework of choice. However, JAX's modular design philosophy leaves it without a standardized checkpointing solution. In this paper, we introduce Orbax, a modular, JAX-native checkpointing library that abstracts the complexities of distributed accelerator systems while also providing flexibility for user-friendly checkpoint manipulations throughout the ML model lifecycle. We demonstrate performance exceeding comparable PyTorch competitors by up to 3.5$\times$ for saving and 2$\times$ for loading. The library is available at https://github.com/google/orbax.

</details>


### [70] [AlignedServe: Orchestrating Prefix-aware Batching to Build a High-throughput and Computing-efficient LLM Serving System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23389)
*Fengyao Bai,Hongbin Zhang,Zhitao Chen,Jiangsu Du,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AlignedServe 是一种通过前缀感知批处理和跨 GPU KV 缓存预取技术，旨在解决 LLM 推理解码迭代中因 KV 缓存长度差异而引发的性能瓶颈，从而大幅提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理框架多关注请求级和批级调度，忽略了单次解码迭代内部因 KV 缓存长度不一致导致的计算延迟差异（由于长 KV 缓存带来的瓶颈），从而产生迭代内的气泡。

Method: 1. 前缀感知批处理（Prefix-aware batching）：将具有相似 KV 缓存长度的请求分组；
2. 大内存支撑：利用 CPU 内存维持充足的待处理请求以实现有效分组；
3. 批级调度策略：进一步减少批处理层面的空闲（气泡）；
4. GPU-Prefetch-For-GPU 架构：实现跨 GPU 的 KV 缓存预取，降低 CPU 到 GPU 的数据搬移延迟。

Result: 实验结果表明，与现有最先进（SOTA）系统相比，Decoding 吞吐量最高提升 1.98 倍，延迟最高降低 7.4 倍。

Conclusion: AlignedServe 通过前缀感知批处理和优化的 GPU 预取架构，显著缓解了 LLM 推理中的迭代内气泡问题，在大规模推理场景中实现了极高的吞吐量和低延迟。

Abstract: High-throughput inference serving is essential for applications built on large language models (LLMs). Existing serving frameworks reduce request-level and batch-level bubbles through batching and scheduling, but often overlook bubbles within each decode iteration. Tokens generated in the same iteration may incur different costs because they depend on KV caches of different lengths; tokens with long KV caches can become bottlenecks and delay the next iteration. We propose AlignedServe, an LLM serving framework built around prefix-aware batching. It groups requests with similar KV-cache lengths into the same batch to reduce iteration-level bubbles. To support this policy efficiently, AlignedServe uses large CPU memory to maintain sufficient in-flight requests for batching and applies a batch-level scheduling policy to reduce batch-level bubbles. It also introduces a GPU-Prefetch-For-GPU architecture, where one GPU prefetches KV cache for another to reduce CPU-to-GPU transfer latency. Experiments on synthetic and application workloads show that AlignedServe improves decoding throughput by up to 1.98 times and reduces latency by up to 7.4 times over state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [71] [Multi-Round Visibility: A Post-Consensus Ordering Layer for DAG-Based BFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23432)
*Pengkun Ren,Dong Hai,Nasrin Sohrabi,Zahir Tari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 DAG-BFT 协议中因高并发导致排序证据不足的问题，本文提出了 MRV，利用已提交 DAG 中的结构元数据进行后共识排序，在不影响共识性能的前提下实现了鲁棒的交易排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 DAG 的 BFT 协议通过解耦分发与共识提高了吞吐量，但这种高并发导致执行边界的排序证据减弱，传统的排序方案（如在共识过程中增加证据收集）往往会将排序逻辑引入关键路径，降低系统效率。

Method: 提出 Multi-Round Visibility (MRV) 层。该方法将已提交的 DAG 作为排序证据基底，利用顶点携带的验证元数据（创建者、轮次、祖先关系）推导多轮结构可见性，并在有界证据范围内比较“公平性原子单元”（AUFs），通过拜占庭鲁棒的可见性优势推导优先约束；对于无法直接确定的情况，通过确定性图补全（deterministic graph completion）解决不确定性。

Result: 在 Narwhal/Tusk 原型上的评估（5-50 个副本）显示，MRV 有效保持了 DAG-BFT 协议的高吞吐特性，实现了高性能的后共识结构化排序。

Conclusion: MRV 证明了一种可以在不增加共识关键路径负担的前提下，为 DAG-BFT 提供后共识结构化排序的有效方案，成功解决了高并发与排序透明度之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Directed acyclic graph (DAG)-based Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) protocols achieve high throughput by decoupling dissemination from agreement and allowing many vertices to be committed concurrently. This same concurrency, however, weakens ordering evidence at the execution boundary: once units are committed in a shared DAG frontier, their final linearization is driven by traversal or deterministic tie-breaking rather than verifiable structural precedence. Prior fair-ordering designs address ambiguity by collecting or reconstructing transaction-level ordering evidence within the consensus workflow. While effective, this couples ordering with agreement and places ordering logic on the critical path. This paper presents Multi-Round Visibility (MRV), a post-consensus structural ordering layer for DAG-based BFT that reinterprets the committed DAG as an ordering evidence substrate. Committed vertices inherently carry authenticated creator, round, and ancestry metadata, enabling replicas to derive multi-round structural visibility without extra consensus-path messages. MRV accumulates this visibility within a bounded evidence horizon, compares concurrently committed atomic units of fairness (AUFs) after they coexist in the DAG, and derives precedence constraints from Byzantine-robust visibility advantages. When the DAG lacks such constraints, MRV exposes and resolves the remaining ambiguity through deterministic graph completion rather than hiding it inside traversal rules. We implement MRV on a Narwhal/Tusk-based prototype. Evaluation across 5-50 replicas under various fault settings shows MRV preserves the high-throughput regime of the DAG-BFT stack, proving it provides post-consensus structural ordering without burdening the consensus-critical path.

</details>


### [72] [Herring: Parallel Batch-Order-Fairness on DAG-based Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23648)
*Marko Putnik,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Herring 是一种新型 DAG BFT 协议，通过在公平性层引入并行化设计，在防止 MEV 恶意重排序的同时，突破了现有公平排序协议的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交易排序公平性（batch-OF）协议存在严重的性能代价。基于领导者的协议（如 Themis）存在单点拥塞，而现有的 DAG 方案（如 FairDAG, DoD）由于强制公平性层串行化，未能发挥出 DAG 并行处理的优势。

Method: 1. 提出 Herring：一个 $\gamma$-batch-OF DAG BFT 协议，通过在已提交的子图上并行化图构建开销。
2. 技术创新：将公平排序转变为 CPU 密集型任务，而非串行瓶颈；利用 DAG 的可靠广播层进行缺失边解析。
3. 安全补丁：识别并修复了 FairDAG-RL 和 DoD 中存在的恶意客户端导致活性（Liveness）中断的漏洞。

Result: 在基于 Narwhal & Tusk 的实现中，Herring 在 10,000 tx/s 时仍能保持极高的吞吐量，且饱和吞吐量比 FairDAG-RL 高约 90%，比 DoD-W 高约 100%，同时显著降低了延迟。

Conclusion: Herring 证明了通过将公平排序逻辑与共识层的图构建解耦，可以在保持 $\gamma$-batch-OF 公平性的同时，实现 DAG BFT 协议的高性能，其性能显著优于现有的 FairDAG 和 DoD 等方案。

Abstract: Transaction ordering attacks extract billions of dollars annually from decentralized finance users in the form of Maximal Extractable Value (MEV). Byzantine Fault-Tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols guarantee total order but place no constraint on how that order is chosen, leaving the door open for adversarial reordering. Batch-order-fairness (batch-OF) protocols close this gap, but existing designs pay a steep performance price for this guarantee. Leader-based protocols such as Themis concentrate all fairness decisions at a single replica, while recent DAG-based proposals FairDAG and DAG of DAGs (DoD) force their fairness layer into strictly serial execution despite running on multi-proposer DAGs.
  We present Herring, the first $γ$-batch-OF DAG BFT protocol whose fairness layer parallelizes the dominant graph construction cost across committed subdags. Herring combines post-consensus graph construction with explicit missing edge resolution piggybacked on the DAG's reliable broadcast layer, a pairing that turns fair ordering from a per-round serial bottleneck into a CPU-bound task. We also uncover previously unreported liveness vulnerabilities in both FairDAG-RL and DoD that a malicious client can trigger to halt the fairness layer indefinitely, and propose patches that we integrate into our reimplementations.
  We implement Herring on top of the Rust implementation of Narwhal \& Tusk and evaluate it against FairDAG-RL, DoD-W, and Themis. Herring tracks the throughput of Narwhal \& Tusk closely up to roughly $10{,}000$\,tx/s, achieves roughly $90\%$ higher saturation throughput than FairDAG-RL and $100\%$ higher than DoD-W, and substantially reduces execution latency at saturation.

</details>


### [73] [AMP: Arc Multi-Proposer Protocol with Bounded Inclusion Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23677)
*Daniel Cason,Gordon Liao,Sergio Mena,Nenad Milošević,Adi Seredinschi,Alessandro Sforzin,João Sousa,Preston Vander Vos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 为了防止区块链验证者利用单方面打包权限进行市场操纵，本文提出了 AMP 协议。通过引入多提议者机制及分离交易传播与共识过程，AMP 有效限制了单一节点的权力，并提升了系统的安全性和处理效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链系统中的“单个验证者拥有交易打包和排序的单方面权力”引发了类似传统金融市场中先行交易与操纵的风险，缺乏监管合规性。现有设计存在中心化控制漏洞，亟需一种协议来限制单一验证者的权力。

Method: 提出了一种基于 Tendermint 的多提议者协议（AMP）。核心机制包括：1. 引入专用节点（提议者）处理交易打包；2. 废除 mempool，将交易载荷广播至所有验证者；3. 采用“传播与共识分离”的设计；4. 验证者公共证明接收，通过确定性排序算法处理多提议者提交的载荷。

Result: AMP 实现了交易包含的公平性约束，消除了单一验证者对区块内容的独裁控制。由于多个提议者并行工作且采用批量处理，该方案在增强抗操纵性的同时，能够实现更高的系统吞吐量。

Conclusion: AMP 通过引入多提议者机制和分离传播与共识的设计原则，有效地限制了单个验证者的垄断权力，防止了交易审查和操纵，同时也提升了区块链系统的吞吐量，为受监管的金融交易环境提供了更具鲁棒性的去中心化方案。

Abstract: Blockchain systems that settle financial transactions face a structural tension: the single validator that assembles each block holds unilateral power over transaction inclusion and ordering. Traditional markets curb this very power through front-running and market-manipulation laws. Regulators have flagged the absence of such rules as a first-order concern for blockchain-based financial infrastructure. In response, we introduce AMP, a multi-proposer protocol, on top of the Tendermint consensus algorithm, where no validator can control the flow of transactions into blocks. Instead, dedicated nodes called proposers sit between users and validators. They collect user transactions, group them into payloads, and broadcast the payloads to all validators. Consequently, there is no mempool, and AMP applies the design principle of separating dissemination from agreement, which can lead to higher throughput. Validators publicly attest to receiving payloads and run consensus to decide the set of payloads to include in the next block. When all correct validators attest to a given payload, AMP guarantees that payload will be included in the next block; a block thus contains payloads from multiple proposers, allowing for bulk finalization. This bounded inclusion guarantee along with a deterministic ordering algorithm which is run over all payloads included in a block, curbs the power of any single validator. Validators no longer control what is included in a block, nor can they arbitrarily order the contents of blocks.

</details>


### [74] [Flare: Leveraging Serverless Elasticity to Absorb Microservice Load Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23707)
*Dilina Dehigama,Shyam Jesalpura,David Schall,Antonios Katsarakis,Marios Kogias,Rakesh Kumar,Boris Grot*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Flare 是一种混合微服务架构，通过动态结合 VM 与 Serverless 技术，解决了突发流量下的性能与成本平衡挑战，实现了对现有应用的透明集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于 VM 的微服务部署在应对不可预测的流量激增时扩容缓慢，导致性能下降；而简单地通过过度配置（over-provisioning）来避免性能问题又会导致成本高昂和资源利用率低下。

Method: 提出了一种混合微服务架构 Flare，利用 VM 处理平稳负载，并在检测到特定服务负载激增时，将超额流量动态卸载到 Serverless 环境中。该方案无需修改应用程序，且对现有的自动扩缩容及 Serverless 基础设施兼容性良好。

Result: Flare 成功实现了微服务架构下的灵活扩缩容，既避免了因扩容滞后导致的性能降级，又通过精准卸载负载优化了资源利用率并降低了运营成本。

Conclusion: 通过结合虚拟机（VM）的成本效益和无服务器计算（Serverless）的弹性，Flare 有效解决了微服务架构在应对突发负载时的性能降级与资源闲置问题，为在线服务提供了一种高效的弹性扩展方案。

Abstract: Online services strive to maintain application responsiveness even when the traffic is unpredictable and fluctuating. Today's online services are commonly deployed as chains of microservices, each microservice packaged as one or more containers inside virtual machines (VMs). While performant and affordable when the load is steady, VM-based deployments are known to be slow to scale when the load spikes, resulting in degraded performance for end-users of the service. To avoid such performance degradations, service providers can over-provision their deployments; however, such a strategy is costly and inefficient, leaving resources under-utilized for extended periods.
  To address the challenge of unpredictable load spikes, we propose Flare, a hybrid microservice architecture that combines VMs with serverless computing. Flare utilizes VMs to cost-effectively handle steady workloads and leverages serverless elasticity to absorb traffic spikes. When a spike occurs, Flare detects which specific service(s) are overloaded and shifts the excess load of only those services to serverless, thus minimizing the cost overhead. Flare seamlessly integrates into existing auto-scaling and serverless infrastructure, requiring minimal changes to the control plane and no modifications to the application.

</details>


### [75] [HyperParallel-MoE: Multi-Core Interleaved Scheduling for Fast MoE Training on Ascend NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23764)
*Zewen Jin,Congkun Ai,Guangpeng Zhang,Hanbo Zhang,Haoran Wang,Shihan Xiao,Da Lei,Xuefeng Jin,Teng Su,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyperParallel-MoE 框架通过在 Ascend NPU 上实现图块级的异构计算（AIC/AIV）与通信并发调度，有效解决了算子级串行执行带来的性能瓶颈，显著加速了 MoE 模型的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MoE 训练框架在 Ascend NPU 上主要采用串行内核执行方式，无法充分利用 AIC（矩阵）和 AIV（向量）等异构片上计算资源，导致资源利用率低下。

Method: 提出了一种编译器和调度框架：1. 将算子级执行转化为静态调度的图块级异构任务流；2. 引入 AIV 驱动的单向通信以消除主机同步；3. 实现依赖保留的任务生成技术，统一通信与计算抽象；4. 采用事件驱动的静态调度，在统一运行时内核中并发驱动 AIC 和 AIV 计算单元。

Result: 在 MindSpore 和 MindFormers 栈及 Ascend A3 集群上，针对 DeepSeek 风格模型进行测试，Dispatch-to-Combine MoE-FFN 延迟最高降低了 1.58 倍。

Conclusion: HyperParallel-MoE 通过将图块级（tile-level）异构任务调度与通信计算重叠机制相结合，成功解决了现有 MoE 训练框架在 Ascend NPU 上利用率不足的问题，显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models increasingly rely on large-scale AI accelerator clusters for efficient training. Ascend NPUs expose heterogeneous on-chip compute resources, including matrix-oriented AIC units and vector-oriented AIV units with explicit cross-queue synchronization support. However, existing training frameworks largely execute MoE operators in a serialized kernel-by-kernel manner, leaving substantial heterogeneous parallelism underutilized.
  This paper presents HyperParallel-MoE, a compilation and scheduling framework for MoE training on Ascend NPUs. HyperParallel-MoE transforms operator-level MoE execution into a statically scheduled tile-level heterogeneous taskflow spanning AIC and AIV resources. It introduces AIV-driven one-sided communication to eliminate host-side collective synchronization, dependency-preserving tile task generation to unify communication and computation under a common task abstraction, and event-driven static scheduling to coordinate cross-queue execution with low runtime overhead. HyperParallel-MoE further executes the compiled taskflow within a unified runtime that concurrently drives AIC and AIV workers inside a single kernel launch, enabling fine-grained overlap among communication, matrix computation, and vector computation while preserving existing optimized operators. We implement HyperParallel-MoE in the MindSpore and MindFormers stack and evaluate it using DeepSeek-style MoE models on Ascend A3 clusters. Across multiple expert-parallel configurations, HyperParallel-MoE reduces Dispatch-to-Combine MoE-FFN latency by up to 1.58x, demonstrating that tile-level heterogeneous scheduling can substantially improve MoE training efficiency on modern NPUs.

</details>


### [76] [Enhancing Energy Efficiency in Scientific Workflows through CFD based PIVAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23850)
*Ali Zahir,Ashiq Anjum,Mark Wilkinson,Jeyan Thiyagalingam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息变分自编码器（PIVAE）的AI调度框架，通过优化HPC环境中的任务调度，在微小性能损耗下实现了显著的节能效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC科学工作流日益复杂，传统调度策略难以处理热动力学、工作负载多样性和系统可扩展性之间的交互，导致能源效率低下且不可持续。

Method: 提出了一种AI辅助的调度框架，核心在于集成计算流体力学（CFD）与物理信息变分自编码器（PIVAE），以生成物理真实的合成工作负载数据；并基于资源利用率对工作流进行分类，评估了位置感知（Locality Aware）和推测感知（Speculative Aware）等多种调度策略。

Result: 实验表明，在CPU性能降低约15%的情况下，可实现高达10%的节能效果，且周转时间仅增加5-6%，从而找到了能源与性能的最佳平衡点。

Conclusion: 通过结合物理信息生成模型与任务调度，证明了在HPC环境中实现能源效率与计算性能平衡的可行性，为下一代可持续HPC基础设施提供了自适应方案。

Abstract: The growing complexity and scale of scientific workflows in high performance computing (HPC) environments have led to significant challenges in managing energy consumption without compromising computational performance. Traditional scheduling strategies often fail to account for the complex interplay between thermal dynamics, workload diversity, and system scalability, leading to inefficient and unsustainable energy usage. This paper introduces a novel, scalable, and AI-assisted scheduling framework for optimizing energy consumption in HPC environments without compromising performance. Central to our approach is the integration of Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) with a Physics-Informed Variational Autoencoder (PIVAE), enabling the generation of physically realistic synthetic workload data that bridges the gap between thermodynamic behavior and scheduler decision-making in complex, multi-scale HPC environments. By categorizing workflows based on resource utilization profiles, we evaluate multiple scheduling strategies such as Locality Aware and Speculative Aware Scheduling. These workflows, ranging from event reconstruction to anomaly detection, represent diverse computational intensities. Our results show that modest reductions in CPU performance (e.g., to 15%) can yield substantial energy savings (up to 10%) with only minor turnaround time increases (approximately 5-6%), identifying an optimal operational sweet spot. This work demonstrates how physics-informed generative modeling can enable adaptive, sustainable, and data-efficient scheduling for next-generation HPC infrastructures.

</details>

### [77] [ModeSwitch-LLM: A Lightweight Phase-Aware Controller for Cross-Mode LLM Inference on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23057)
*Aman Sunesh,Ali Alshehhi,Hivansh Dhakne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ModeSwitch-LLM 是一种轻量级路由控制器，通过根据请求特征动态切换推理模式（如量化、投机解码），在不牺牲精度的情况下，显著提升了单GPU的LLM推理速度并降低了功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对单GPU环境下使用单一静态配置进行LLM推理效率低、功耗高的问题，旨在不改变模型架构的前提下，通过智能路由分配实现推理性能的优化。

Method: 提出一种轻量化的请求边界控制器（ModeSwitch-LLM），利用低成本的工作负载特征，在FP16、量化、投机解码及混合模式（如GPTQ+前缀缓存、INT8+持续批处理）之间进行动态路由切换。

Result: 在NVIDIA A100 GPU上测试显示，相比纯FP16模式，平均推理时延加速2.10倍，能效提升51.7%，且模型精度保持一致（均值偏离仅+0.17%）。此外证明了简单的规则驱动路由优于复杂的学习型路由。

Conclusion: 通过简单的请求感知路由机制，无需重新训练模型或修改架构，即可显著提升单GPU环境下的LLM推理效率，且在保持模型性能的同时大幅降低了功耗。

Abstract: ModeSwitch-LLM is a lightweight request-boundary controller for improving single-GPU large language model inference efficiency by routing each request to an appropriate fixed inference mode. Instead of relying on one static serving configuration, the system selects among FP16, quantized modes, speculative decoding, and hybrid modes such as GPTQ plus prefix caching and INT8 plus continuous batching using cheap workload-level features. We evaluate ModeSwitch-LLM on Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct served on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU. On deployment-style synthetic workloads, the online controller achieves a 2.10x mean latency speedup over FP16 and a 0.48x mean energy ratio, corresponding to 51.7% lower energy per token. On automatic benchmarks used as a quality gate, accuracy remains close to FP16 with a mean delta of +0.17 percentage points. We also evaluate lightweight learned routers, but find that they do not clearly outperform the rule-based controller because they add routing overhead and more often select modes that violate quality, energy, or memory constraints. These results show that simple request-aware routing can recover substantial efficiency from existing inference modes without retraining the model or changing its architecture.

</details>


### [78] [Latent Cache Flow: Model-to-Model Communication Without Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22863)
*Maximillian Rossi,Prajwal Raghunath,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 LCF 的新型 LLM 智能体通信框架，通过对 KV 缓存进行高效压缩与增量摘要传输，解决了传统文本通信的性能瓶颈及现有 KV 缓存交换方案的适配性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本通信方式存在高延迟和信息丢失问题；现有的 KV 缓存交换方法（如 C2C）存在适配器体积庞大、训练昂贵且要求上下文完全一致的局限性，不适用于异构智能体间的通信。

Method: 提出 Latent Cache Flow (LCF) 方法：1. 对键值 (KV) 缓存进行联合转换与压缩，大幅减小适配器规模；2. 设计了一种针对不同上下文的摘要传输机制，仅传递目标模型缺失的新信息。

Result: 在同等上下文设置下，13 MB 的 LCF 适配器优于 956 MB 的 C2C 适配器；在不同上下文设置下，LCF 的准确率比文本通信高出 23%，速度提升 8.5 倍。

Conclusion: LCF 是一种高效、通用的 LLM 智能体通信方案，能够显著降低通信开销并在不同上下文下实现比传统文本交互更高的准确性和速度。

Abstract: LLM agents today communicate via text, which incurs considerable latency and information loss due to the need to autoregressively decode the sharer model's state and encode at the receiver model. Recent work such as Cache-to-Cache (C2C; Fu et al., 2026) seeks to exchange KV caches by learning adapters that translate sharer KV matrices to the receiver model. However, the adapters are large and expensive to train, and translate individual tokens, which requires the target context to be identical. This is unsuitable for agent communication, where the LLMs have differing context.
  We introduce Latent Cache Flow (LCF). To address efficiency, we observe that keys and values can be jointly translated and compressed, reducing the adapter to about 4% of C2C's size. To address differing context, we design the adapter to transmit a summary of new information that the target model does not have. Our early experiments show that a 13 MB LCF adapter can be more accurate than a 956 MB C2C adapter in shared-context settings; for different contexts, LCF is 23% more accurate and 8.5x faster than text-based communication.

</details>


### [79] [Cost-Effective Model Evaluation with Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23595)
*Trinh Pham,Viet Huynh,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Thanh Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaEvaluator 是首个模型无关的无标签数据评估框架，通过元学习实现跨架构的高效性能预测，解决了模型评估成本高、难以迁移的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型数量激增，导致在未见过的无标签数据上验证新模型可靠性的难度加大；现有方案高度依赖昂贵的标注、重复微调，且通常缺乏模型间迁移能力。

Method: 提出 MetaEvaluator 框架，利用元学习（meta-learning）基于参考模型池获取可迁移的初始化参数，从而能够评估跨架构、跨模态的新模型，无需对单个模型进行重新训练或标注数据。

Result: 实验表明，MetaEvaluator 与传统方法相比，在大幅降低成本的同时，能够提供稳定且准确的性能评估。

Conclusion: MetaEvaluator 证实了在无需人工标注数据或对每个模型进行重复微调的情况下，通过元学习实现对未见模型进行准确、高效性能评估的可行性。该框架为大规模、无标签数据集下的模型基准测试提供了一种经济且普适的解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid growth of machine learning has produced an ever-expanding ecosystem of models, making it increasingly challenging to verify the reliability of newly released models on unseen, unlabeled data. Conventional evaluation pipelines depend on expensive annotation, repeated fine-tuning, or narrow assumptions that fail to transfer across model families. We present MetaEvaluator, a cost-effective, model-agnostic framework for rapid, label-free assessment of unseen models spanning diverse architectures and modalities. MetaEvaluator leverages meta-learning over a pool of reference models to obtain a transferable initialization, enabling accurate evaluation of new models while amortizing cost across the pool and removing the need for per-model retraining. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first model-agnostic framework capable of evaluating new models on entirely unlabeled datasets. Extensive experiments show that MetaEvaluator produces stable and accurate performance estimates at substantially reduced cost compared to conventional approaches, making scalable benchmarking of emerging models on unlabeled data practical.

</details>


### [80] [Reading Calibrated Uncertainty from Language Model Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22864)
*Aliai Eusebi,Alexander Herzog,Xiaoyu Liang,Marie Vasek,Enrico Mariconti,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MSP方法校准不足的问题，本文提出通过追踪模型内部逐层更新的几何轨迹来量化不确定性，不仅在准确性上超越了MSP，还提供了关于模型决策过程的深度可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大Softmax概率（MSP）方法尽管计算成本低，但在评估大模型生成不确定性时存在校准不良（miscalibrated）的问题；且现有的基于激活状态的探测方法忽略了模型表征形成的深层演化轨迹。

Method: 提取11个尺度不变（scale-invariant）的几何特征，追踪多层感知机（MLP）逐层更新的累积路径，并将其输入到一个稀疏线性探测器（sparse linear probe）中进行不确定性分类。

Result: 该几何探针在选择性弃权（selective abstention）任务中显著优于MSP，在校准度差的基线模型上AURC指标提升高达21点；此外，探针的可解释性允许研究者追踪错误在模型深度中的演化路径。

Conclusion: 通过分析语言模型层级表征的几何轨迹，可以比单一的MSP更准确地量化不确定性。该方法揭示了模型内部的决策过程，展示了为何模型在产生错误时会偏离最终表征。

Abstract: The maximum softmax probability (MSP) represents a default approach when evaluating uncertainty quantification for language model generation with structured output. Although cheap, it is often miscalibrated. Methods that probe the model's internal activations feed raw hidden states into opaque classifiers, reading activations as static snapshots and leaving implicit the layer-wise trajectory by which a representation is formed. Yet, similar endpoints can arise from very different paths, and how evidence accumulates, reinforces, or reverses across depth might reveal uncertainty that final probabilities obscure. We extract eleven scale-invariant geometric features, tracing the cumulative path of per-layer MLP updates, and feed them to a sparse linear probe. The probe outperforms MSP under selective abstention, with gains scaling with baseline miscalibration up to 21 AURC points. Because every feature has a closed-form geometric meaning, the probe's coefficients trace how and where along depth errors take shape -- which layers commit prematurely, which contradict the running state, where trajectories drift away from their endpoint.

</details>


### [81] [FusionSense: Tri-Stage Near-Sensor Learning for Runtime-Adaptive Multimodal Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22868)
*Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Minhyoung Na,Hyunwoo Oh,Yoshiki Yamaguchi,Wenjun Huang,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionSense通过一种软硬结合的融合感知框架，优化了边缘自动驾驶系统的数据传输与计算决策，在大幅降低能耗的同时保持了多模态感知任务的高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前边缘自动驾驶系统中，多模态传感器数据激增，但现有方法要么依赖后端服务器融合导致高延迟/带宽消耗，要么采用忽略跨模态依赖的单模态过滤导致数据冗余或漏检，因此需要一种能耗更低、感知更智能的边缘任务决策框架。

Method: 提出了FusionSense框架，包含三步训练流程：（1）在服务器端训练融合模型；（2）通过“过滤安全（FoS）”标签量化各模态对融合决策的必要性；（3）通过注入近传感器预测作为辅助信号，压缩并部署边缘侧融合模型，实现运行时动态决策。

Result: 在RGB+LiDAR/Depth设置下，相比最优基线，FusionSense实现了高达33倍的能耗降低（1% FoI），在固定30%数据缩减下质量损失降低92.3%，且整体能耗节省较现有最优方法提升约1.5倍。

Conclusion: FusionSense通过在近传感器侧利用辅助信号进行融合感知，实现了在保持任务精度的同时，显著降低边缘设备在计算和通信方面的能耗，优于现有的单模态滤波方法。

Abstract: Autonomous systems and smart-industry deployments increasingly split computation across near-sensor, edge, and cloud resources, where tight energy, latency, and reliability budgets demand run-time adaptivity. In practice, deciding what to compute and transmit at each point is pivotal; yet as multimodal sensor suites (cameras, LiDAR/depth, etc.) proliferate at the edge, most prior approaches either (i) fuse modalities on powerful servers or (ii) apply uni-modal near-sensor filters that ignore cross-modal dependencies, leading to redundant transmissions or missed events. We present FusionSense, a fusion-aware intelligent sensing framework for energy-constrained autonomous edge systems. Lightweight near-sensor classifiers are trained via a three-step procedure: (i) a server-side fusion model learns the downstream task, (ii) filter-out-safe (FoS) labels quantify each modality's necessity relative to the fused decision, and (iii) an edge-side fusion model is compacted by injecting near-sensor predictions as auxiliary signals. The result is a run-time decision layer that jointly reduces compute and communication while scaling linearly with sensor count. On a dual-modality (RGB+Depth/LiDAR) setup with SynDrone, FusionSense sustains task quality at substantially higher data-reduction rates than uni-modal filters and delivers large end-to-end gains: up to 33x lower energy at 1% FoI prevalence, 11x at 10%, a 92.3% reduction in quality loss at a fixed 30% data reduction, and roughly 1.5x higher energy savings than the best prior filtering baseline.

</details>


### [82] [FuRA: Full-Rank Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Spectral Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22869)
*Yequan Zhao,Ruijie Zhang,Liyan Tan,Niall Moran,Tong Qin,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FuRA 是一种新型微调框架，通过利用预训练权重的谱结构进行预处理（固定 SVD 基底），在保持参数高效性的同时实现了优于全参数微调的性能，解决了传统方法容易破坏预训练鲁棒特征的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的微调方法（如 Full FT 和 LoRA）在更新权重时忽略了预训练阶段形成的谱结构，导致微调数据的噪声梯度破坏了预训练阶段获得的鲁棒特征。

Method: 提出 FuRA (Full-Rank Adaptation) 方法，采用块张量列（block tensor-train）分解 $W = LSR$。核心思想是固定大规模核心 $L$ 为预训练的块级 SVD 基底，仅优化紧凑核心 $R$ 和块级奇异值 $S$，从而实现全秩谱预处理。

Result: FuRA 在多个任务（LLM 推理、数学推理、视觉指令微调）中性能稳定优于 Full FT。在 LLaMA-3-8B 上提升了 1.37 个点的常识推理能力，且其 4-bit 量化版本 QFuRA 超越了 QLoRA。同时，该方法在参数量、内存占用和计算时间上与 LoRA 相当。

Conclusion: 提出 FuRA 框架，通过利用预训练权重的谱结构（全秩 SVD）进行预处理，在保持参数高效性的同时，实现了比全微调（Full FT）更好的性能，证明了在微调中考虑谱结构对于保留鲁棒性至关重要。

Abstract: Both full fine-tuning (Full FT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA introduce weight updates without accounting for the spectral structure established during pretraining. As a result, noisy gradients from limited fine-tuning data can perturb robust pretrained features. We identify spectral preconditioning as the missing ingredient: reparameterizing each weight matrix through its full-rank singular value decomposition (SVD) and freezing one singular basis constrains updates to the pretrained column space, yielding a preconditioned optimization scheme that outperforms unconstrained Full FT at the same trainable parameter count. Building on this insight, we propose FuRA (Full-Rank Adaptation), an efficient full-rank adaptation framework based on a block tensor-train factorization W = LSR, where the large core L is fixed to the pretrained block-wise SVD basis, while only the compact core R and the block-wise singular values S are optimized. This design simultaneously provides full-rank spectral preconditioning, preserves full-rank update expressivity, and achieves parameter, memory, and step-time efficiency comparable to LoRA. FuRA consistently outperforms Full FT across multiple settings, including LLM fine-tuning (+1.37 on LLaMA-3-8B commonsense reasoning), LLM reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning, and visual instruction tuning for VLMs. Furthermore, the 4-bit quantized variant, QFuRA, also surpasses QLoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/olokevin/FuRA-NIPS

</details>


### [83] [Learning-Augmented Online Scheduling with Parsimonious Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23255)
*Mugen Blue,Sungjin Im,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对学习增强在线调度中“频繁抢占”的问题，提出了一种限制抢占次数并优化任务延迟的新框架，实现了理论性能与工程约束的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习增强在线调度主要关注降低延迟，但过度依赖频繁且无序的抢占（preemption），忽略了抢占带来的系统开销与性能之间的权衡。因此，研究需要在优化延迟的同时限制抢占次数。

Method: 提出了有界抢占的学习增强调度方案；设计了适用于单机和异构并行机环境的算法，证明了其在预测准确时实现O(1)-competitive，且抢占次数保持在O(1)；分析了算法性能随预测误差增长的对数级缩放特性。

Result: 在单机和异构并行机模型中，实现了在准确预测下每个任务仅需O(1)次抢占且具有O(1)竞争比的表现；对于任务执行不可预测的情况，其性能随预测误差呈对数级退化；在不可预测及可塑性机器模型上实现了首个有界抢占保证，并通过实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了有界抢占下的学习增强调度理论基础，证明了算法在保证性能的同时，能够通过极少的抢占次数实现接近最优的延迟，为更符合实际的调度场景提供了理论与实践支持。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms have emerged as a powerful paradigm to surpass traditional worst-case lower bounds by integrating potentially noisy predictions. While this framework has seen success in online scheduling, existing work primarily optimizes job latency while relying on frequent, ``blind'' preemptions. This ignores the fundamental trade-off between algorithmic performance and preemption complexity. We provide the first systematic study of learning-augmented scheduling that curbs preemption while optimizing latency. We establish that the gap between theoretical latency bounds and preemption overhead can be bridged with solid analytical foundations.
