Our agent maintains a set of imprecise hypotheses, updates them using infra-Bayesian conditioning, and selects actions by maximizing worst-case expected value. We apply this implementation of the infra-Bayesian maximin decision process to an environment with Knightian uncertainty, and demonstrate a lower worst-case regret as compared to classical reinforcement learning agents. We also investigate Newcomb's problem and show that the infra-Bayesian agent picks the optimal strategy, outperforming classical decision theory agents. Our results provide a step towards reinforcement learning agents that remain robust under model misspecification and policy-dependent uncertainty.

</details>


### [120] [Pure Exploration for a Good Policy in Reinforcement Learning with Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23182)
*Zitian Li,Wang Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出BEE-GPI算法，将纯探索强化学习的目标从寻找最优策略转向寻找“足够好”的策略（GPI），有效降低了样本复杂度，使其在正实例中不再受状态空间和动作空间大小的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的纯探索强化学习研究大多集中在“最优策略识别（BPI）”，但在实际应用中，寻找一个“足够好”的策略（Good Policy Identification, GPI）往往已足够，且可能比寻找最优策略更低耗。因此，作者旨在研究如何以最小的样本复杂度识别出满足一定奖励阈值的策略。

Method: 1. 提出了一种名为BEE-GPI（Best-Enough Exploration for GPI）的新型算法，用于固定置信度下的Good Policy Identification问题；2. 在理论上推导了该算法在正样本（存在满足阈值的策略）和负样本（不存在满足阈值的策略）下的样本复杂度上界；3. 通过下界分析证明了该算法在样本复杂度方面的近优性。

Result: 1. 算法BEE-GPI在正实例中的样本复杂度系数为O(H^2/(V* - μ0)^2)，该复杂度不依赖于状态空间和动作空间的大小；2. 理论下界分析表明该复杂度关于奖励差距的依赖项是必要的；3. 数值实验验证了BEE-GPI相较于现有方法在处理GPI任务时具有显著的效率优势。

Conclusion: BEE-GPI算法在GPI任务中是近优的，其实践性能优于传统BPI方法。理论上证明了在正实例中样本复杂度与状态-动作空间大小无关，仅依赖于奖励差距（1/(V*-μ0)^2），验证了该算法的效率。

Abstract: Pure exploration in episodic Reinforcement Learning has primarily focused on Best Policy Identification (BPI), which seeks to identify a (near)-optimal policy with high confidence. Motivated by practical settings where a ``good enough'' policy suffices, we study an alternate objective of Good Policy Identification (GPI). For a given reward threshold $μ_0$, GPI only requires identifying a policy with expected reward in an episode at least $μ_0$ if such a policy exists (positive instance), or declaring None if no such policy exists (negative instance). We formalize GPI under the fixed-confidence setting. We require the output to be correct with probability $\geq 1-δ$, and seek to minimize the expected sample complexity, which is the expected number of episodes explored for the output. We propose a novel algorithm BEE-GPI, and derive theoretically-grounded upper bounds on its sample complexity for positive and negative instances. Notably, for positive instances, the coefficient of $\log 1/δ$ in our upper bound is $O(H^2/(V^* - μ_0)^2)$, where $H$ is the episode length and $V^*$ is the optimal expected reward in an episode. The coefficient does not depend on the action and state space sizes otherwise, in sharp contrast to the sample complexity in BPI. We further establish lower bound results to show the near-optimality of BEE-GPI and the necessity of the $1/(V^* -μ)^2$ term. Numerical experiments further validate the efficiency of our approach.

</details>


### [121] [Empirical Bayes Conformal Prediction for Vision and Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23189)
*Jiapeng Zeng,Yogesh Prabhu,Zhanpeng Zeng,Michael A. Newton,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种利用“r-value”来衡量预测不确定性的共形预测框架，通过结合均值和变异性信息来改进预测集的筛选，在保证理论覆盖率的同时提高了预测的稳健性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的共形预测（CP）通常依赖于单一或平均后的非共形分数，忽略了模型输出中的变异性信息。这导致不稳定的候选样本（仅因偶然或样本波动而得分高）容易被错误地包含在预测集中，降低了预测的质量和可信度。

Method: 提出了一种基于经验贝叶斯（Empirical Bayes）的共形预测框架，引入“r-value”指标。该指标综合了得分的均值和不确定性（变异性），将得分变异性转化为信息丰富的非共形分数，并提供了闭式正态-正态估计器和非参数后验采样估计器两种实现方式。

Result: 在图像分类、CLIP视觉语言模型及大语言模型（LLM）等多个基准测试中，r-value共形预测不仅保持了目标覆盖率，还显著提升了排名稳定性，并在变异性信息丰富时减小了预测集尺寸。

Conclusion: r-value通过量化得分的不确定性，在保持共形预测（CP）理论覆盖保证的前提下，有效提高了模型输出的排名稳定性并减小了预测集的大小，在视觉和语言模型任务中表现出优越性。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) gives distribution-free coverage for modern vision and language models, but it is often forced to make a ranking decision from a single unstable nonconformity score. Standard CP uses one realization, while average-then-calibrate variants smooth multiple realizations into a point estimate. Both options discard the inconsistency that can help identify whether a candidate is indeed stable. A weak answer can enter the conformal set even if the evidence is not strong, simply because one posterior sample or prompt phrasing made it look strong. But variability can help distinguish a stable signal from noise-driven fluctuations. We describe an empirical Bayes conformal prediction framework that uses $r$-values to convert score variability into an uncertainty informed nonconformity score. The resulting $r$-value estimates how likely a candidate's latent score belongs to the top-ranked group after accounting for both its mean score and its uncertainty. It admits both a closed-form Normal-Normal empirical Bayes estimator and a nonparametric posterior-sampling estimator. Using the $r$-value as the nonconformity score preserves the target conformal coverage while provably reducing the inclusion of high variance false candidates under mild regularity conditions. Across image classification, CLIP-based VLM benchmarks, and LLMs, we show that $r$-value conformal prediction preserves target coverage while improving ranking stability and reducing set size when variability is informative, and reverting to CP-like behavior when variability vanishes.

</details>


### [122] [Label-Efficient Dataset Pruning via Semi-Supervised Pseudo-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23198)
*Yeseul Cho,Baekrok Shin,Changmin Kang,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SemiPrune 是一种标签高效的数据集修剪框架，通过半监督学习为未标注数据生成伪标签，从而在无需全量标注的情况下，利用监督学习的修剪策略筛选出高质量的核心集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集修剪方法要么过度依赖全量标注数据，要么使用无监督（标签无关）方法却受限于预训练模型的分布偏差，导致在特定目标数据集上表现不佳。

Method: 提出 SemiPrune 框架：使用极少量随机标注数据通过半监督学习生成目标数据集的伪标签；利用伪标签诱导训练动态（training dynamics）评估样本难度；基于此难度指标进行核心集（coreset）选择。

Result: 在领域特定数据、图像受损数据和长尾数据集上达到了 SOTA 水平，在标准基准数据集上也表现出竞争力。

Conclusion: SemiPrune 通过利用少量标注数据和半监督学习产生的伪标签，能够适应目标数据分布，在多种挑战性场景（如领域变异、图像损坏、长尾分布）下实现了优于现有标签无关和标签高效修剪方法的性能。

Abstract: Dataset pruning reduces the storage and training costs of deep learning by selecting an informative subset from a large dataset. However, most existing pruning methods require fully labeled data, which limits their applicability in realistic settings where unlabeled data are abundant and annotation is costly. Recent label-free pruning methods address this issue, but they rely on features from pretrained models to estimate example difficulty. This dependence can be unreliable when the target dataset differs substantially from the pretraining distribution. We propose SemiPrune, a label-efficient dataset pruning framework, using only a small randomly labeled subset, that uses semi-supervised learning to generate pseudo-labels for unlabeled data, allowing existing supervised pruning methods that require label information to be seamlessly applied to the resulting pseudo-labeled training pool. We then estimate example difficulty from pseudo-label-induced training dynamics and select a coreset. By learning directly from the target dataset, our method better captures the target distribution and provides more reliable signals for difficulty estimation and coreset selection. We validate our approach on domain-specific, image-corrupted, and long-tailed datasets, where it achieves state-of-the-art performance among label-free and label-efficient baselines, while also demonstrating competitive performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [123] [PaP-NF: Probabilistic Long-Term Time Series Forecasting via Prefix-as-Prompt Reprogramming and Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23219)
*Minju Kim,Youngbum Hur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PaP-NF框架，通过Prefix-as-Prompt机制结合冻结的大语言模型与正态流解码器，实现了稳健的概率时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数时间序列预测方法多为确定性模型，难以满足真实世界中复杂且不确定的环境需求；通过概率预测来量化和表示不确定性是必要的。

Method: 提出PaP-NF框架，采用Prefix-as-Prompt机制将连续时间序列表示与冻结的大语言模型（LLM）对齐，并使用正态流（Normalizing Flow）解码器基于LLM提取的全局上下文进行条件建模。

Result: 在多个长期时间序列预测基准测试中，PaP-NF在连续排名概率评分（CRPS）指标上表现出色，能够稳健地捕捉复杂的不确定性。

Conclusion: PaP-NF框架能够在保持竞争力的点预测精度的同时，有效捕获多模态不确定性，验证了利用冻结大语言模型提取全局上下文进行概率预测的有效性。

Abstract: Time series forecasting plays a central role in many real-world applications and has been extensively studied. Most existing approaches rely on deterministic models. However, real-world environments exhibit inherently uncertain and complex future behaviors, making single-point predictions insufficient. This highlights the need for probabilistic forecasting methods that can quantify and represent uncertainty. In this work, we propose PaP-NF, a probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns continuous time series representations with a frozen large language model (LLM) using a Prefix-as-Prompt mechanism, and conditions a normalizing flow decoder on the global context extracted by the LLM. The quality of the resulting predictive distributions is evaluated using the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS), a standard metric in probabilistic forecasting. Across a variety of long-term forecasting benchmarks, PaP-NF robustly captures multi-modal uncertainty while maintaining competitive point forecasting accuracy. The official implementation is available at: https://github.com/democracy04/PaP-NF

</details>


### [124] [WMAttack: Automated Attack Search for Adversarial Evaluation of World-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23220)
*Zhixiang Guo,Siyuan Liang,Shi Fu,Cheng Guo,Andras Balogh,Mark Jelasity,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对世界模型对抗性评估难的问题，WMAttack 提出了结合自纠正搜索和表征检索的自动化框架，在实现评估准确性提升的同时确保了有限算力下的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在决策场景下的对抗性鲁棒性缺乏有效的自动化评估方法。传统方法若手动调优攻击会导致鲁棒性高估，而穷举搜索在闭环仿真中计算成本过高。

Method: 提出 WMAttack 框架：1) 将鲁棒性评估建模为有限预算下的攻击配置搜索；2) 引入“自纠正攻击搜索 (SCAS)”机制，利用奖励退化、动作不稳定性等反馈动态调整攻击策略；3) 引入“表征引导攻击检索 (RGAR)”机制，通过历史上相似任务的攻击经验为新环境提供初始化建议。

Result: 在 Atari 和 DeepMind Control (DMC) 任务上，WMAttack 均显著优于现有基线。在 DreamerV3 Atari 上，归一化奖励下降幅度（衡量攻击强度）从 0.497 提升至 1.034；在 DMC 上从 0.319 提升至 0.682。

Conclusion: WMAttack 通过自适应的攻击配置搜索框架，解决了世界模型在对抗性评估中准确度与效率难以平衡的问题。实验证明该方法在 Atari 和 DMC 任务中均能发现比基线方法更强的攻击，显著提升评估的严谨性。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of world models as decision-making agents, their adversarial robustness remains underexplored due to the lack of dedicated automated evaluation methods. A key obstacle is that attack evaluation must be both accurate and efficient: weak manually tuned attacks can overestimate robustness, while exhaustive hyperparameter search is prohibitively expensive because each candidate requires closed-loop rollouts through learned latent dynamics. We introduce WMAttack, an automated attack-search framework for adversarial evaluation of world-model agents. WMAttack formulates robustness evaluation as a finite-budget search over attack configurations, including attack families, perturbation budgets, optimization steps, restarts, and allocation rules. To improve search accuracy, Self-Correcting Attack Search (SCAS) refines the attack proposal distribution using feedback from reward degradation, action instability, runtime cost, and rollout variability. To improve search efficiency, Representation-Guided Attack Retrieval (RGAR) retrieves effective historical configurations from representation-similar tasks, providing a warm start for unseen environments. We provide a theoretical explanation showing that proposal refinement improves finite-budget search when it shifts probability mass toward high-utility attacks. Across Atari and DeepMind Control tasks, WMAttack consistently discovers stronger attacks than the evaluated baselines, improving normalized reward drop from 0.497 to 1.034 on DreamerV3 Atari and from 0.319 to 0.682 on DMC. Ablations further show that RGAR improves initial candidate quality and SCAS improves final attack utility under fixed evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [125] [Assessing Predictive Models for Fairness Based on Movement Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23234)
*Francesco Lettich,Mario A. Nascimento,Chiara Pugliese,Chiara Renso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将空间公平性的定义从“单一居住地”扩展到“移动轨迹”，提出一种利用多尺度空间扫描统计量检测预测模型在个体移动模式上是否存在公平性偏差的新方法，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的空间公平性研究主要假设个体仅关联单一位置（如居住地），忽略了动态移动模式可能导致的不公平性。为了更全面地评估预测模型，需要将空间公平性的范畴扩展至地理移动行为。

Method: 提出了一种基于移动模式的空间公平性评估方法：1. 将个体移动轨迹关联到多个不同分辨率和对齐方式的空间分区；2. 利用空间扫描统计量（Spatial Scan Statistics）检测模型是否基于移动模式对特定区域群体存在统计上的偏差。

Result: 在数千个合成不公平数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效检测出基于移动模式的不公平性，并能准确识别受不公平对待的群体集合，同时揭示了检测效果与空间分辨率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种评估轨迹数据空间公平性的新框架，证明了考虑个体移动模式对于揭示更深层次的算法公平性至关重要，并验证了多分辨率空间扫描统计量在检测和定位此类不公平性方面的有效性。

Abstract: Assessing the spatial fairness of predictive models involves establishing whether they are statistically penalizing (favoring) individuals associated with certain geographical locations. Literature on this topic makes the fundamental assumption that each individual is assigned to a single geographical location (e.g., place of residence). However, fairness with respect to the set of locations where one has been, i.e., their movement patterns over different regions, also matters when fairness is considered. Consequently, we argue that it is necessary to generalize the notion of spatial fairness to also include movement patterns, leading to the novel problem of assessing predictive models for fairness relative to the movements of individuals. To deal with this problem, we propose an approach that first associates the movements of individuals to certain geographic regions, considering multiple spatial partitions with different resolutions and alignments, and then employs a suitable spatial scan statistic to assess whether a predictive model is fair based on movement patterns. In the experimental evaluation, we study the performance of our approach over thousands of synthetic unfair datasets, showing that it is effective at detecting this new type of unfairness and at retrieving the set of objects treated unfairly, while localization performance exhibits a consistent multi-resolution trade-off.

</details>


### [126] [Convex Low-resource Accent-Robust Language Detection in Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23235)
*Miria Feng,William Tan,Mert Pilanci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对语音系统在方言和低资源环境下识别率低的问题，提出了一种名为CLD的新型凸优化框架，通过高效的ADMM算法实现了高精度、鲁棒性强的语音语言检测与识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决全球化背景下，当前口语对话系统在处理少数方言和口音时识别失败，以及现有微调方法在资源受限场景下计算成本高且易过拟合的问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于凸优化的语言检测框架（CLD），利用JAX框架实现多GPU协同的交替方向乘子法（ADMM），确保模型全局最优性，并在理论上证明了其具有经认证的边际稳定性和抗特征扰动能力。

Result: 该方法在低资源约束下表现出高样本效率，在挑战性语境中达到了97-98%的准确率，且具有极高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CLD框架通过将凸优化引入语种识别，在低资源环境下实现了高准确率、计算效率和鲁棒性，为解决多样化口音及方言带来的语音对话系统失效问题提供了有效的理论与实践方案。

Abstract: Globalization and multiculturalism continue to produce increasingly diverse speech varieties. Yet current spoken dialogue systems frequently fail on under-represented dialects and accents, often misidentifying the input language and causing cascading failures in downstream dialogue tasks. Addressing this dialectal variance under low-resource constraints remains an open challenge, as standard fine-tuning is computationally expensive and prone to overfitting on high-dimensional speech data. We propose Convex Language Detection (CLD), a novel framework that integrates theoretically grounded convex optimization techniques into the spoken dialogue systems pipeline. Our method is efficiently implemented via multi-GPU Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) in JAX, thus providing global optimality guarantees and fast training in polynomial time. Theoretically, we prove that our convex objective induces certified margin stability and provide guarantees against feature perturbations. Empirically, we demonstrate sample efficiency and robustness to input dialectical variation, achieving 97-98% accuracy in challenging low-resource regimes. Our open-source package is available at https://pypi.org/project/jaxcld/

</details>


### [127] [RelPrism: A Multi-Faceted Pre-training Framework with Self-Generated Tasks for Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23241)
*Jinyu Yang,Cheng Yang,Junze Chen,Zedi Liu,Muhan Zhang,Hanyang Peng,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决关系数据库多任务中对不同信息侧重和粒度的需求，RelPrism 引入多面、多粒度的自监督学习框架，显著提升了数据库表征学习在各类下游任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关系深度学习（RDL）自监督学习方法通常仅关注单一维度（如节点属性或子图结构），难以满足下游任务对不同粒度和多面信息的需求，导致表征的适用性受限。

Method: 提出自监督学习框架 RelPrism。它从固有属性、关系结构和混合信息三个维度构建不同视角，并结合多粒度聚类形成伪任务池（pseudo-task pools），通过覆盖更全面的特征空间进行预训练。

Result: 在5个真实数据集上的14个任务中表现优异，分类任务的ROC-AUC提升了4.15%，回归任务的MAE降低了10.75%，优于现有的前沿基准方法。

Conclusion: RelPrism 通过多面（intrinsic, relational, hybrid）和多粒度（multi-granularity）的自监督学习，有效解决了关系数据库在不同预测任务中对信息需求异构性的挑战，显著提升了关系数据库表征学习的通用性和下游任务的效果。

Abstract: Relational databases (RDBs) remain the cornerstone of modern data systems and support diverse predictive tasks. Recent relational deep learning (RDL) methods enable end-to-end prediction by converting RDBs into graphs, where rows are represented as nodes and inter-table interactions are represented as edges, and then applying graph-based models for representation learning. Despite the strong capability of RDL, effective self-supervised pre-training for RDBs remains non-trivial. RDB tasks often require multi-faceted information across different perspectives and granularities. For example, user churn classification may rely more on interaction patterns, whereas consumption value prediction requires both user-item behaviors and intrinsic user attributes for fine-grained regression. Such heterogeneous needs challenge RDB representation learning, as pre-training objectives should cover comprehensive information for downstream adaptation. However, existing SSL methods typically derive supervision from a single facet, such as node-level intrinsic attributes or subgraph-level relational structures, providing limited adaptability. To this end, we propose RelPrism, a multi-faceted self-supervised learning framework for RDBs. RelPrism constructs intrinsic, relational, and hybrid attributes from distinct perspectives, and applies multi-granularity clustering to each perspective to form corresponding pseudo-task pools. Pre-training over these pools exposes representations to broader perspectives and granularity levels, yielding a stronger basis for downstream adaptation. Experiments on 14 tasks across 5 real-world datasets show that RelPrism improves ROC-AUC by 4.15% for classification and reduces MAE by 10.75% for regression over state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RelPrism.

</details>


### [128] [A Simple Plug-in for Improving Eviction-Based KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23258)
*Yuping Lin,Jiayuan Ding,Yue Xing,Pengfei He,Jiliang Tang,Subhabrata Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VECTOR 是一种新型 KV Cache 压缩方法，通过结合重要性与可重构性信号进行“三向令牌路由”，在减少 KV Cache 内存占用的同时，最大化保留了推理所需的关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 KV Cache 压缩方法（如简单的二进制抛弃或单一的表示近似）往往会导致信息分配低效，难以兼顾重要令牌的精确保存与可重构令牌的信息恢复。

Method: 提出了一种即插即用的增强机制 VECTOR，引入了“三向令牌路由”：结合基础评分器（重要性信号）与基于回归的离线校准模型（可重构性信号）进行决策，将令牌分为保留（Retention）、近似（Approximation）和抛弃（Eviction）三类。

Result: 实验表明，与传统的二进制抛弃方法相比，VECTOR 在中高压缩比下提升了模型输出质量，并在严格的内存预算限制下表现出明显的性能改进。

Conclusion: VECTOR 框架通过引入三向令牌路由机制（保留、近似、抛弃），成功改善了长文本推理中 KV Cache 的质量-内存权衡，特别是在高压缩比场景下表现优异。

Abstract: KV cache growth is a major bottleneck for long-context inference in large language models. Existing methods are often dominated by binary eviction or representation approximation, which may underutilize tokens that are not critical for exact retention but are still reconstructable. We present VECTOR, a plug-and-play augmentation for eviction-based pipelines that introduces three-way token routing: retention, approximation, and eviction. VECTOR combines an importance signal from the base scorer with a reconstructability signal from an offline-calibrated regression-based value estimation. By leveraging reconstructability, VECTOR recovers useful value information that would otherwise be irreversibly lost under binary eviction, while preserving key vectors for attention routing stability. Experimental results show that VECTOR improves quality-memory trade-offs under medium-to-high compression, with especially clear gains in stricter budget regimes.

</details>


### [129] [When Good Equations Get Bad Scores: Improving Symbolic Regression Through Better Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23272)
*Boxiao Wang,Kai Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yang Huang,Runxiang Wang,Ziwen Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对符号回归搜索过程中因参数拟合不良导致正确方程被误删的问题，本文提出了 SAGE-Fit，通过利用符号结构的语义和结构先验优化拟合过程，消除了“好结构、坏得分”的优化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归中存在的“好结构、坏得分”（Good Structure, Bad Score）现象，即因内层参数拟合不佳导致正确结构的真实潜力被低估，从而误导搜索过程。

Method: 提出了 SAGE-Fit (Structure-Aware and Semantics-Guided Evaluator) 框架。利用符号表达式特有的双重先验（结构先验与语义先验），设计了专门的模块来优化参数拟合，解决非线性算子导致的非凸优化难题。

Result: 实验表明，SAGE-Fit 有效改善了评估准确度，并作为即插即用模块提升了多种现有算法在符号回归任务上的表现。

Conclusion: SAGE-Fit 作为一种即插即用的评估模块，能够显著提升符号回归中方程结构的评估质量，从而普遍改善现有符号回归系统的搜索效率和整体性能。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) plays a central role in scientific knowledge discovery by distilling mathematical equations from observational data. Most existing SR methods function within a bi-level optimization framework: an outer loop that searches for the discrete equation structure, and an inner loop that optimizes the continuous parameters of that structure. Crucially, parameter-fitting quality directly determines a structure's score and thus the outer-loop search. However, nonlinear operators make the inner loop highly non-convex, and budget-driven reliance on fast local solvers (e.g., BFGS) often yields poor local minima and underestimated scores for correct structures. This ``Good Structure, Bad Score'' phenomenon becomes a key bottleneck, degrading efficiency and misguiding the search away from the true equation. To resolve this, we propose SAGE-Fit (Structure-Aware and Semantics-Guided Evaluator for Symbolic Regression), an SR-native fitting framework that exploits the dual native priors of symbolic expressions. By capitalizing on the structural and semantic priors unique to SR, we design tailored modules for each property, thereby effectively mitigating this optimization bottleneck. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach, as a plug-and-play module, significantly enhances evaluation fidelity and universally improves the performance of various SR systems.

</details>


### [130] [Reinforcement Learning for Microcanonical Graph Ensemble with Assortativity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23285)
*Hoyun Choi,Junghyo Jo,Deok-Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究人员提出了一种基于强化学习的深度微正则图生成器（DMGG），实现了在保持图度序列的同时，精确生成满足特定同配性约束的图，解决了传统微正则系综采样困难的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图生成模型（如ERGMs）通常仅能满足期望约束，而微正则系综虽然能满足硬约束，但除了度序列外缺乏有效的采样方法。研究旨在解决如何实现精确控制图结构属性（如度-度相关性）的约束采样问题。

Method: 提出了深度微正则图生成器（DMGG），这是一个基于强化学习的框架；通过策略引导搜索执行保持度序列的重连（rewiring），以精确达到预设的同配性（assortativity），替代了传统的Metropolis-Hastings动力学。

Result: DMGG消除了繁琐的参数调整，生成速度比传统方法提高了至少一个数量级，同时保持了配置多样性；该方法在不同规模、稀疏性和拓扑结构的图上具有普适性，且能有效地进行次级可观测变量（如聚类系数）的定量分析。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了强化学习（RL）在生成具有严格约束的图模型方面是一种实用且高效的范式，通过解决微正则系综采样难题，为研究图的结构-功能关系提供了一种排除系综伪影（ensemble artifacts）的有效方法。

Abstract: How network structure determines function is a fundamental question, and it can be investigated by graph ensembles with precisely controlled structural properties. Canonical approaches, formulated as exponential random graph models (ERGMs), enforce constraints only in expectation, allowing individual realizations to fluctuate around the target. Conversely, microcanonical ensembles impose hard constraints exactly, but practical sampling methods beyond fixing the degree sequence have remained out of reach. Here we introduce the Deep Microcanonical Graph Generator (DMGG), a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that transforms any given graph through degree-preserving rewirings to exactly reach a prescribed assortativity, which characterizes the degree--degree correlation of adjacent nodes. Instead of relying on the entropically dominated Metropolis--Hastings dynamics of the ERGM, DMGG employs a policy-guided search that maximally alters the joint-degree matrix. This eliminates exhaustive parameter tuning and accelerates generation by at least an order of magnitude while preserving configurational diversity. As DMGG generalizes across various graph sizes, sparsities, and topologies, it provides exact null models that allow for the quantitative isolation of secondary observables, such as the clustering coefficient. These results establish RL as a practical and powerful paradigm for generating hard-constrained graphs, opening avenues to investigate structure-function relationships free from ensemble artifacts.

</details>


### [131] [Prudent-Banker: No Extra Fees for Baseline Safety in Adversarial Bandits With and Without Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23351)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对具有延迟反馈的对抗性多臂老虎机问题，提出Prudent-Banker算法，在确保对安全基线策略保持常数遗憾的同时，实现了极小极大最优的加性遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗性多臂老虎机算法在处理延迟反馈时，无法平衡安全约束（即保持对基线策略的常数遗憾）与探索之间的权衡，延迟会导致保守策略与探索策略切换的时机错误，从而破坏安全性。

Method: 提出Prudent-Banker算法，结合了延迟自适应的在线镜像下降（Online Mirror Descent）和改进的分阶段激进机制（phased-aggression mechanism），并引入了延迟校准的重启阈值来处理未观察到的反馈导致的失真。

Result: 理论上证明了在延迟环境下实现了伪遗憾 $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$ 以及对安全基线的 $\widetilde{O}(1)$ 遗憾；实验证实该算法优于标准的延迟鲁棒基线。

Conclusion: Prudent-Banker是首个在存在任意延迟的情况下，同时实现最优遗憾保证（$O(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$）和基线安全性（$O(1)$ regret）的算法，且其理论性能达到了下界最优。

Abstract: We study adversarial multi-armed bandits with and without delayed feedback under a safety-aware goal: achieving minimax-optimal worst-case regret while keeping nearly constant regret relative to a designated "safe" baseline policy. Existing approaches can balance this trade-off with immediate feedback for smooth comparators, but arbitrary delays can mistime transitions between conservatism and exploration, endangering the safety guarantee. To bridge this gap, we propose Prudent-Banker, a novel algorithm that combines a delay-adapted variant of Online Mirror Descent with a modified phased-aggression mechanism. Its key technical contribution is a delay-calibrated restart threshold that rigorously accounts for the worst-case distortion induced by unobserved feedback and reliably detects comparator suboptimality. We also establish new lower bounds for safety-constrained adversarial delayed bandits, showing that the regret guarantees of Prudent-Banker are unimprovable, up to logarithmic factors, under the baseline-safety requirement. To the best of our knowledge, Prudent-Banker is the first algorithm to achieve the optimal safety--robustness trade-off: pseudo-regret $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$ together with $\widetilde{O}(1)$ regret against the safe comparator, both with and without delays. Experiments across diverse delay distributions show that, unlike standard delay-robust baselines, Prudent-Banker effectively balances safety and learning.

</details>


### [132] [Score-Based One-step MeanFlow Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23365)
*Kyungyoon Kim,Donghyeon Ki,Hee-Jun Ahn,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线强化学习中扩散模型推理速度慢的问题，SOM 算法通过将 Q 函数映射为速度场，实现了高效的单步策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配虽在强化学习中表现出色，但多步去噪带来的高推理成本严重阻碍了在线强化学习的应用，而既有的 MeanFlow 方法在缺乏目标分布样本时难以直接应用。

Method: 提出了一种名为“基于分数的单步均值流策略优化（SOM）”的演员-评论家算法，通过分数估计（score estimation）将 Q 函数转化为目标速度场，省去了对目标分布样本的显式依赖。

Result: 在 locomotion 任务中实现了单步推理的 SOTA 性能，并显著降低了训练和推理的计算开销。

Conclusion: SOM 通过利用 Q 函数导出的目标速度场，实现了单步采样的高效策略优化，解决了扩散模型和流匹配在在线强化学习中推理延迟高的问题，并达到了 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching have emerged as expressive policy classes in reinforcement learning, but their reliance on multi-step denoising imposes substantial computational overhead at inference time, which is particularly problematic in online RL. MeanFlow offers a promising alternative by learning an average velocity field that maps noise to data in a single network evaluation. However, MeanFlow typically requires samples from the target distribution to construct its target velocity field, which are unavailable in online RL. We propose Score-Based One-step MeanFlow Policy Optimization (SOM), an actor-critic algorithm that resolves this by constructing the target velocity field directly from the Q-function via score estimation and a probability flow ODE, thereby concentrating probability mass on high-value modes. In the fully online RL setting, SOM achieves state-of-the-art performance on locomotion tasks with a single generation step, while substantially reducing both training and inference time compared to prior diffusion- and flow-matching-based policies.

</details>


### [133] [Every Component is a Lookup: Token Attribution and Composition from a Single Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23393)
*Po-Kai Chen,Niki van Stein,Aske Plaat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种称为 Unpack 的高效递归算法，通过解析 Transformer 的通用计算结构，实现了无需干预或梯度的机制路径与交互归因分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 的机械可解释性不仅需要确定哪些组件重要，还需要解析这些组件如何组合成计算路径，而现有方法通常依赖昂贵的干预或梯度分析。

Method: 开发了名为“Unpack”的后向递归算法，利用 Transformer 中注意力机制和 MLP 共享的“键-值”模板（φ(S)U）进行机制分解；该方法无需模型干预、梯度计算或辅助训练。

Result: 在 IOI 任务上成功复现了 GPT-2 Small 的已知组合连接及其模式定位；在 Pythia 系列模型（160M 至 6.9B）上验证了其能准确识别特定的重复标记抑制模式，证明了其跨规模的可扩展性和准确性。

Conclusion: Unpack 是一种高效的 Transformer 机械可解释性分析方法，无需干预或梯度即可从单次前向传播中揭示详细的路径交互及归因，且在不同规模模型中表现稳健，能够精准捕获复杂的计算结构。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of transformers requires identifying not just which components matter but how they compose into the computational route that produced a prediction. Both attention and MLP follow a shared key-value template $φ(S)U$. We exploit this structure to develop Unpack, a backward recursion that decomposes credit through both sublayers, producing interaction strengths between any two components, named end-to-end paths with K/Q/V composition labels, and per-token attribution from a single forward pass, without intervention, gradients, or auxiliary training. We evaluate on the indirect object identification task. On GPT-2 small, the method recovers all three composition connections described by Wang et al. (2023), including the mode-specific routing of each connection (K, Q, or V). To test token-level attribution beyond trivial copying, we compare two occurrences of the same name in the same decomposition: the first mention retains strong credit while the duplicate-detection position is suppressed, a pattern absent in matched control prompts. Across the Pythia family from 160M to 6.9B parameters, this suppression pattern is consistently recovered at every scale, demonstrating that the method tracks mechanistic structure without ground-truth circuit labels. Code is available at https://github.com/Fun-Cry/unpacklm.

</details>


### [134] [CoSPlay: Cooperative Self-Play at Test-Time with Self-Generated Code and Unit Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23491)
*Zhangyi Hu,Chenhui Liu,Tian Huang,Jindong Li,Yang Yang,Jiemin Wu,Zining Zhong,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 代码生成中依赖真实单元测试（GT UTs）的难题，本文提出了 CoSPlay 框架，通过代码与单元测试的协同自博弈，在无需 GT 数据的情况下，实现了代码与测试的共同进化及性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习验证奖励机制（RLVR）和测试时扩展（TTS）方法过度依赖真实基准测试（GT UTs），而完全基于自生成单元测试的 GT-free 路径又面临 UT 质量参差不齐且难以独立验证的困境。

Method: 提出 CoSPlay 框架：1. 探索多样化解决方案并识别失效模式以生成判别性 UT；2. 利用 Code-UT 执行矩阵的双向通过率信号进行迭代筛选与修复；3. 采用最大输出共识（output-consensus）策略进行最终候选代码选择。

Result: 在 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 上，平均 BoN（Best-of-N）从 22.1% 提升至 33.2%，UT 准确率从 14.6% 提升至 78.3%，性能媲美或超越了 RLVR 模型 CURE-7B；并在 CURE-7B 上进一步提升了 5.7% 的 BoN。

Conclusion: CoSPlay 提出了一种无需真实基准测试（GT-free）、无需训练（training-free）的合作自博弈框架，有效地解决了现有测试时扩展（TTS）方法在缺乏高质量单元测试（UT）时的性能瓶颈，实现了代码生成与UT质量的协同提升，在多个基准测试中表现出色。

Abstract: Recently, Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and Test-Time Scaling (TTS) have advanced LLM code generation through executable verification. Yet Ground-Truth Unit Tests (GT UTs) remain a bottleneck: SOTA RLVR methods require them for costly training, while existing TTS methods lose competitiveness without them. This motivates GT-free TTS, where existing methods directly use self-generated UTs to refine and select code candidates. Yet such UTs are often noisy or spuriously coupled with wrong code, and UT quality in turn cannot be validated without reliable code. The key challenge is therefore to jointly improve both. To this end, we present CoSPlay, a GT-free, training-free framework that jointly improves codes and UTs through cooperative self-play. It first explores diverse solution ideas and identifies their potential failure modes to produce discriminative UT ideas. It then uses bidirectional pass-count signals from the Code-UT execution matrix to iteratively prune or fix weak codes and refresh or replace unreliable UTs, letting the two pools co-evolve. Finally, when multiple codes remain tied at the highest pass count, it picks the final code from the largest output-consensus cluster, since correct codes agree on the same inputs while wrong codes diverge. Experiments on four challenging benchmarks show that CoSPlay on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct improves average BoN from 22.1% to 33.2% and UT accuracy from 14.6% to 78.3%, matching or surpassing the RLVR model CURE-7B. When applied to CURE-7B, it further improves BoN by 5.7%. CoSPlay also generalizes across diverse backbones and outperforms GT-free TTS baselines under comparable token budgets, with continued gains as the budget scales up. These results suggest a scalable inference strategy for competitive code generation without any GT data.

</details>


### [135] [DiLaDiff: Distilled Latent-Augmented Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23605)
*Jean-Marie Lemercier,Tomas Geffner,Karsten Kreis,Morteza Mardani,Arash Vahdat,Ante Jukić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiLaDiff 提出了一种基于连续潜在空间和一致性蒸馏的扩散语言模型，在显著提升生成质量的同时，大幅降低了推理计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型在处理解码标记间的相关性时存在内在缺陷，导致采样质量与生成吞吐量之间存在严重的权衡问题（Trade-off）。

Method: 提出 DiLaDiff 方法，包含三个核心组件：1. 基于预训练掩码扩散模型微调得到的、具备语义能力的连续潜在空间；2. 学习潜在空间先验分布的潜在扩散模型；3. 将先验分布提炼为少步数生成的一致性蒸馏模型。

Result: 实验表明，DiLaDiff 在无需蒸馏的情况下即优于基准模型并加速了推理；引入一致性蒸馏后，进一步大幅降低了计算开销，使得潜在向量生成时间几乎可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: DiLaDiff 通过引入连续潜在空间、潜在扩散模型及一致性蒸馏，有效解决了扩散语言模型在处理标记间相关性时的性能与效率瓶颈，实现了高质量且高效的序列生成。

Abstract: Diffusion language models intrinsically fail to capture correlations between decoded tokens, which leads to a harsh trade-off between sampling quality and throughput. To solve this issue, we propose DiLaDiff, a variant of masked diffusion language models with three components: (1) a continuous latent space with semantic capabilities, learned by an auto-encoder fine-tuned from an existing masked diffusion language model; (2) a latent diffusion model learning the prior over the encoder distribution; (3) a consistency model distilling the learned prior into a few-step latent generative model. We show that, even without distillation, our latent-guided diffusion model outperforms the masked diffusion baseline while significantly accelerating inference. Consistency distillation further lowers the computational overhead of continuous diffusion, such that the latent is generated in negligible time compared to discrete decoding.

</details>


### [136] [Parametric Prior Mapping Framework for Non-stationary Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23402)
*Jinglin Li,Jun Tan,QI Fang,Ning Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PPM的架构，通过将参数化结构先验融入生成模型，有效解决了非平稳多元时间序列预测中模型表达能力与计算效率难以兼顾的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数化方法缺乏灵活性，而深度生成模型在处理复杂时间依赖时往往需要大量数据和计算，难以有效建模非平稳多元时间序列的动态特征。

Method: 提出参数化先验映射（PPM）框架，将参数化结构先验注入生成式建模过程。利用参数化估计器导出动态自适应先验，并通过可学习的映射引导复杂预测分布的学习，采用混合目标函数进行训练。

Result: 在处理非平稳数据方面优于现有基准模型，在预测准确性和计算效率之间实现了更好的权衡，并提供了校准良好的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: PPM通过结合参数化先验与生成式建模，成功在非平稳多元时间序列预测中实现了表达能力与鲁棒性的平衡，证明了结构化先验对于提升生成模型性能的有效性。

Abstract: Effectively modeling non-stationary dynamics in probabilistic multivariate time series(MTS) forecasting requires balancing expressiveness with robustness. Existing parametric approaches benefit from strong inductive biases but lack flexibility, whereas deep generative models struggle to capture complex temporal dependencies without extensive data and computation. We introduce Parametric Prior Mapping (PPM), a framework that injects parametric structural priors into a generative modeling process. Specifically, PPM utilizes a parametric estimator to derive a dynamic, adaptive prior that guides the learning of a complex predictive distribution via a learnable mapping. This design allows the model to retain the efficiency of parametric methods while exploiting the expressive power of generative models. Trained with a hybrid objective, PPM yields precise forecasts with well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. Empirical results show that PPM outperforms existing baselines in handling non-stationary data, offering a superior trade-off between accuracy and computational efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/ljl8336/PPM.

</details>


### [137] [Strong Teacher Not Needed? On Distillation in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23857)
*Taiming Lu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过多种实验设计挑战了知识蒸馏必须依赖强教师的定论，发现模型性能提升不完全取决于教师强度，并明确了蒸馏在提升模型鲁棒性和泛化能力方面的实际价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“知识蒸馏中强教师总是产生更好学生”这一传统假设，并系统研究大语言模型（LLM）预训练中不同强度师生关系对蒸馏效果的影响。

Method: 通过改变架构规模和训练令牌（token）预算，构建强到弱、同等规模、弱到强等多种师生关系配置；探索语言模型损失（LM loss）与知识蒸馏损失（KD loss）的混合策略；在不同规模和训练阶段下评估蒸馏效果。

Result: 1. 即使是弱教师，在正确混合损失函数的情况下也能提升大模型性能；2. 教师能力存在饱和点，过度增强教师（更多参数或训练数据）可能导致增益停滞甚至下滑；3. 蒸馏对提升模型泛化能力（OOD及下游任务）的效果显著优于域内拟合。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏在预训练中并不总是需要“强教师”，教师模型的强度并非越高越好。通过合理的损失函数混合，小型或训练不足的教师同样能提升大模型的性能。该研究颠覆了传统观点，强调了蒸馏主要有助于提升模型泛化能力而非简单的域内拟合。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation generally assumes a strong-to-weak relationship where stronger teachers yield better students. In this work, we examine this assumption about distillation in large language model pretraining. By varying architecture sizes and training token budgets, we create strong-to-weak, same-level, and weak-to-strong teacher-student relationships, and study distillation's effectiveness under each. We find that the teacher need not be strong: with proper mixing of the language modeling and knowledge distillation losses, even small and undertrained teachers improve larger students. At the same time, a stronger teacher is not always better: pushing the teacher further, through more parameters or more training tokens, can saturate or even reverse the distillation gains. We further observe that distillation improves generalization (out-of-distribution and downstream performance) more readily than in-domain fitting. Together, these results challenge the common belief that distillation pretraining always requires a strong teacher.

</details>


### [138] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Corrective Diffusion Modeling for Meteorological Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23403)
*Rui Wang,Edoardo Pasetto,Amer Delilbasic,Morris Riedel,Kristel Michielsen,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种将变分量子电路嵌入扩散模型瓶颈层的混合方案，成功实现了风场统计降尺度的性能提升，验证了量子层在有效捕捉复杂空间特征方面的潜力，但也指出了计算规模限制与OOD泛化能力的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计降尺度对于从粗分辨率输入生成高分辨率气象数据至关重要，但传统的动力降尺度计算成本极高。作者旨在探索利用量子计算的特性（如高效的特征映射能力）是否能提升现有经典扩散模型的降尺度效果，并验证量子电路在隐层处理中的实际贡献。

Method: 提出一种混合量子-经典纠错扩散模型进行天气降尺度。方法是在扩散UNet的最压缩瓶颈层插入变分量子电路（VQC）层，其余回归分支保持经典架构。通过评估不同量子ansätze（内通道与跨通道）在10m风场数据上的表现，测试量子电路作为隐空间通道混合的非线性特征映射能力。

Result: 实验显示，2020年验证集上，混合模型保持了生成风场的稳健性和空间组织结构，在MAE和CRPS指标上优于纯经典模型。结构诊断表明其能保持风能谱和风速分布，并对极端风速的定位有所改善。模拟噪声测试显示当前电路规模下噪声影响可控，但在OOD测试中泛化能力有所下降。

Conclusion: 瓶颈层的量子混合技术对天气统计降尺度具有潜力，可实现非平庸的性能提升。但当前量子电路的规模及其在真实硬件上的执行保真度仍是主要限制因素，且模型在处理时间维度上的分布外（OOD）迁移时存在泛化缺口，需进一步通过稳定化和正则化手段改进。

Abstract: Statistical downscaling is a crucial component of the weather modeling field, where high-resolution outputs must be reconstructed from coarse-resolution inputs with the full cost of dynamical refinement. In this work, we investigate a hybrid quantum-classical corrective diffusion model for probabilistic statistical downscaling of weather fields. The proposed model inserts variational quantum circuit layers into the most compressed bottleneck of the diffusion UNet while leaving the regression branch fully classical. This placement tests whether quantum circuits can act as compact nonlinear feature maps for latent-channel mixing. We evaluate intra-channel and cross-channel ansätze on 10m wind components. On the 2020 validation set, the hybrid models remain stable, preserve the large-scale spatial organization of the generated wind fields, and improve both MAE and CRPS relative to a classical corrective diffusion model in several configurations. Structural diagnostics further show that the hybrid variants preserve kinetic-energy spectra and windspeed distributions similar to its classical counterpart while producing controlled changes in tail behavior, extreme-windspeed localization, and joint wind field components structure. Backend studies on the 2020 validation set show negligible impact from simulated device noise at the tested circuit scale, whereas real-hardware deployment remains limited by qubit availability and execution fidelity. The 2021 out-of-distribution test shows that these in-distribution gains do not transfer uniformly under temporal shift, revealing a generalization gap that motivates future mitigation through stabilization and regularization. These results show that bottleneck-level quantum hybridization can make a nontrivial contribution to weather statistical downscaling, while also highlighting that circuit scale and hardware deployment remain key limiting factors.

</details>


### [139] [Sample-wise Targeted Adversarial Attacks on Test-time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23411)
*Phuc Duc Nguyen,Quang Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对测试时自适应（TTA）模型，本文提出了一种样本级目标攻击方法，通过元学习优化与优先级感知梯度策略，实现了在维持全局标签分布、逃避检测的同时，对特定触发样本的高效误分类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对测试时自适应（TTA）的攻击通常以类别为单位，会导致目标标签频率异常升高，容易被检测器识别。因此，作者旨在开发一种针对携带特定触发器（trigger）样本的“样本级”攻击，在实现误分类的同时，通过保持全局标签分布的一致性来规避检测。

Method: 提出了一种基于元学习（meta-learning）的攻击框架，核心技术包括：1. 优先级感知梯度对齐策略（priority-aware gradient alignment）；2. 将梯度更新形式化为椭圆信赖域（ellipsoidal trust-region）优化问题，有效解决了攻击成功率与分布隐蔽性之间的目标冲突。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C和ImageNet-C等基准测试中，该攻击方法不仅实现了高针对性攻击成功率，且保持了与无攻击基准一致的标签分布，并对现有的相关防御机制表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究定义了一种新型的测试时攻击威胁模型（样本级针对性攻击），揭示了TTA在面对更具隐蔽性和针对性威胁时的脆弱性，其提出的攻击框架在保持高攻击成功率的同时具备极强的隐蔽性，对现有TTA模型的鲁棒性提出了严峻挑战。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) effectively counters distribution shifts but exposes models to adversarial manipulation via the unlabeled test stream. Existing class-wise targeted attacks remain impractical for stealthy exploitation in this setting: since TTA operates on batches, forcing a subset of samples toward a target label unintentionally pulls similar benign samples along, resulting in a conspicuously high frequency of the target label that is easy to detect. To capture a more realistic threat, we introduce a sample-wise targeted attack. Unlike prior approaches, the attacker aims to misclassify only inputs carrying an attacker-chosen trigger, while preserving the global label distribution of benign queries to evade detection. To achieve this, we propose a meta-learning-based attack with a novel priority-aware gradient alignment strategy that explicitly prioritizes attack success. The strategy formulates the gradient update as an ellipsoidal trust-region problem, mitigating the misalignment between attack success and distributional stealth, while providing theoretical guarantees for effective optimization of the attack objective in the presence of gradient misalignment. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-C across TTA protocols demonstrate that our method achieves high targeted success rates while maintaining a label distribution that is consistent with the no-attack baseline, making it difficult to detect in unlabeled TTA deployment scenarios. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our attack shows strong robustness against existing defenses.

</details>


### [140] [Reflex: Reinforcement Learning with Reflection Symmetry Exploitation in State-Based Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23415)
*Shuai Zhen,Yifan Zhang,Yuling Wang,Yanhua Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflex框架，通过在状态空间控制任务中引入反射对称性（轴对称与双侧对称），有效提升了PPO和SAC等强化学习算法的样本效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习面临样本效率低下的问题；现有利用群不变性（Group-invariant MDPs）的研究多集中于图像输入和旋转对称性，忽略了状态空间输入及反射对称性的应用潜力。

Method: 1. 将反射对称性形式化为轴对称（axial reflection）和双侧对称（bilateral reflection）；2. 基于对称性保持原则，设计了相应的策略学习正则化机制；3. 将该框架成功集成至主流RL算法（PPO和SAC）中，无需改变基准算法的底层架构。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control基准测试中，集成Reflex的算法表现均优于标准基线算法，且在样本效率上表现出显著提升。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Reflex框架通过引入轴对称与双侧对称性，弥补了现有研究在状态空间控制任务及镜像对称性方面的不足，证明了利用对称性先验可显著提升强化学习算法的样本效率与性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has long struggled with poor sample efficiency. One promising approach to mitigate this problem is leveraging group-invariant Markov Decision Processes ($G$-invariant MDPs). Existing works in this direction have primarily focused on image-based RL and rotational symmetry such as $\mathrm{SO(2)}$, leaving state-based RL and reflection symmetry largely underexplored. In this work, we focus on state-based continuous control tasks and exploit reflection symmetry by introducing Reflex, a paradigm that seamlessly integrates with both on-policy and off-policy RL algorithms. We formalize two types of reflection-axial reflection and bilateral reflection, and characterize their corresponding transformations. Building on a theoretical analysis of symmetry-preserving optimal value functions and policies, Reflex integrates reflection symmetry into policy learning through principled symmetry regularization mechanisms. We integrate Reflex with PPO and SAC, and evaluate it on a suite of OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control benchmarks, demonstrating superior performance over standard baselines while improving sample efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/TonyStark042/Reflex.

</details>


### [141] [An Open-Source Training Dataset for Foundation Models for Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23417)
*Aaron Klein,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Luca Thale-Bombien,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了BBO-Pile，这是目前最大的公开黑盒优化数据集，并利用其训练了一系列不同规模的基础模型，证明了通过大规模预训练实现黑盒优化是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黑盒优化方法依赖大量超参数调优，且缺乏大规模、真实世界且公开的预训练数据集，限制了通用基础模型的发展和复现。

Method: 构建并开源了BBO-Pile数据集，包含超过50万条优化轨迹，涵盖3095个黑盒函数；并在此基础上训练了一系列参数规模（2M至80M）和训练Token规模（200M至2B）的基础模型，研究其性能扩展规律。

Result: BBO-Pile作为当前最大的公开黑盒优化数据集，验证了基础模型在该领域的扩展定律（Scaling Laws），证明了大规模预训练模型能够有效模仿黑盒优化行为。

Conclusion: 大规模预训练对于模仿黑盒优化算法是可行且有效的，为构建黑盒优化基础模型提供了基准和研究方向。

Abstract: Most black-box optimization methods require extensive hyperparameter tuning, often limiting their ability to generalize across different optimization domains. Foundation models for black-box optimization that learn optimization principles from a large collection of optimization trajectories offer a promising alternative, with the potential to outperform manually designed methods across diverse problem classes. However, prior work has either relied on non-public datasets or on purely synthetic data, limiting reproducibility and generalization to real-world problems. As a result, progress in this area has been constrained by the lack of large-scale, real-world, publicly available pre-training data. We introduce BBO-Pile, the first open-source dataset comprising over 500K optimization trajectories evaluated across 3095 different black-boxes for different optimizers, which represents by far the largest public dataset for this task. Using this dataset, we train a family of foundation models at multiple scales, ranging from 2M to 80M parameters and from 200M to 2B training tokens, and study their scaling behavior with respect to compute. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pre-training is a viable and effective approach to imitate black-box optimization methods, paving the way for future research in this direction.

</details>


### [142] [Hinge Regression Trees and HRT-Boost: Newton-Optimized Oblique Learning for Compact Tabular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23422)
*Hongyi Li,Jun Xu,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新型斜决策树框架HRT，通过非线性最小二乘重构分裂过程并结合牛顿优化，同时配套了高效的Boosting集成算法，在保持高性能的同时实现了更好的模型压缩空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的斜决策树学习方法由于分裂优化过程中的离散性和非凸性，难以获得高质量的树结构。为了克服这一挑战，作者寻求一种更稳定、理论可解释的优化框架。

Method: 1. 提出了Hinge Regression Tree (HRT)框架，将斜分裂重构为两个线性预测器的非线性最小二乘问题（利用max/min包络实现ReLU式的表达能力）。
2. 将节点级优化解释为阻尼牛顿法（Damped Newton Method），并引入回溯线性搜索以保证目标的单调递减。
3. 提出了HRT-Boost，通过将节点级牛顿更新与阶段性函数梯度下降相结合，强化了整体模型的优化力。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了HRT是具有O(δ^2)显式逼近率的通用逼近器。
2. 证明了HRT-Boost在平方损失下具有阶段性经验风险降低保证。
3. 实验表明，HRT在单树基准上具有很强的竞争力，HRT-Boost对比现有强集成基线在取得优异性能的同时，能够生成更紧凑的模型。

Conclusion: HRT通过将斜决策树分裂点转化为非线性最小二乘问题，在理论上证明了其作为通用逼近器的有效性。与提升算法（HRT-Boost）结合后，不仅在性能上与现有的强集成模型竞争，还实现了更优的模型紧凑性。

Abstract: Learning high-quality oblique decision trees remains a significant challenge due to the discrete and non-convex nature of split optimization. We present the Hinge Regression Tree (HRT) framework, which reframes each oblique split as a nonlinear least-squares problem over two linear predictors whose max/min envelope induces ReLU-like representation capacity. We show that the resulting node-level optimization can be interpreted as a damped Newton method, and we establish the monotonic decrease of the node objective for its backtracking line-search variant. We establish, theoretically, that HRT is a universal approximator with an explicit $O(δ^2)$ approximation rate. Building upon this base learner, we propose HRT-Boost, a mathematically synergistic ensemble extension that couples node-level Newton updates with stage-wise functional gradient descent. We show that this ensemble construction admits a stage-wise empirical risk reduction guarantee under the squared loss. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that HRT is highly competitive with established single-tree baselines, and HRT-Boost compares favorably with strong ensemble baselines and often yields substantially more compact models. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Hongyi-Li-sz/HRT-Boost.

</details>


### [143] [Onsager-Machlup Posterior Transport for Deep Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23434)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于路径传输的DGP推理新方法OM-Path，通过Onsager--Machlup正则化提升了确定性采样性能，在大规模数据集上显著优于传统变分推理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度高斯过程（DGP）通过诱导变量进行近似推理时存在计算瓶颈，现有方法要么通过ELBO拟合显式密度，要么通过MCMC采样，这些方法在效率和准确性上仍有提升空间。

Method: 提出“后验传输”（posterior transport）框架，将DGP推理视为学习一个确定性采样器。具体实现为OM-Path，利用Song的概率流ODE作用于Doob桥接的正向SDE，使用Onsager--Machlup动作对路径进行正则化，通过训练最小化临时Doob桥接路径后验的负对数非归一化密度。

Result: 在7个UCI回归数据集上的测试显示，OM-Path在大规模数据集（power, protein）上性能显著优于DBVI；在小规模、高噪声数据集上表现略逊于DBVI。结果表明，降低路径目标的方差比精确的目标密度跟踪对最终性能更重要。

Conclusion: 通过引入Onsager--Machlup作用量作为路径正则化项，OM-Path在大型数据集上实现了对传统变分推理方法（DBVI）的性能超越，证明了在路径空间进行推理不仅可行，且在处理复杂分布时比依赖精确密度估计的ELBO变体更具优势。

Abstract: Approximate inference over inducing variables is the central computational bottleneck of Deep Gaussian Processes (DGPs). Existing methods either fit an explicit density $q_φ(\bU)$ by an ELBO (DSVI, IPVI, DDVI, DBVI) or sample by MCMC (SGHMC). We instead frame DGP inference as \emph{posterior transport}: learn a deterministic sampler that maps a tractable reference measure to posterior-relevant inducing variables, regularised by a path prior derived from the Doob-bridged reference diffusion. Our realisation, \textbf{OM-Path} (formally FBVI-bridge-Path), uses Song's probability-flow ODE applied to DBVI's Doob-bridged forward SDE; the reference drift is closed-form from the bridge marginal coefficients (no score matching) and the path regulariser is the \textbf{Onsager--Machlup action}. At the finite-$ε$ value used at training, the objective is the negative log unnormalised density of a tempered Doob-bridge path posterior, and Theorem 1 identifies it with the same posterior's small-noise MAP path via the Freidlin--Wentzell LDP. Two strict path-space ELBO variants on the same bridge backbone (FFJORD log-det; OM-regularised CNF) are derived as ablations. Under a matched-seed paired Wilcoxon test against DBVI on seven UCI regression benchmarks, OM-Path delivers statistically significant wins on the two largest datasets (\textit{power}: $p\!=\!0.014$, NLL $\mathbf{0.012}$ matching the DSVI baseline of $0.017$; \textit{protein}: $p\!=\!0.002$, RMSE $\mathbf{0.716}$ vs.\ $0.764$, NLL $\mathbf{1.086}$ vs.\ $1.149$), statistical ties on \textit{yacht} / \textit{qsar}, and concedes \textit{boston} / \textit{energy} / \textit{concrete} to DBVI on small-$N$ noisy data. The strict-ELBO variants do not clear DBVI on any UCI metric: in this regime, reducing the variance of the path objective dominates exact-density tracking.

</details>


### [144] [Commutator-Induced Uncertainty in VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23449)
*Tahereh Dehdarirad,Michael Felsberg,Gabriel Eilertsen,Ziliang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有VAE难以处理非交换潜结构的问题，提出了一种Lie Group VAE框架，通过诊断并对齐潜空间代数特性与解码器重构行为，显著增强了模型对复杂几何变换的建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对称感知VAE（Symmetry-aware VAEs）通常强制执行交换性约束，这会抑制数据中固有的非交换结构。本文旨在通过明确诊断并反映非交换性，改善模型对具有非交换几何结构数据的建模能力。

Method: 提出了一个两阶段Lie Group VAE框架：第一阶段为无约束训练，用于诊断非交换结构（基于BCH偏差）与重构行为（基于顺序交换测试）之间的尺度失配；第二阶段引入形变稳定性约束（deformation-stability constraint），并使用数据驱动的校准常数将解码器敏感性与代数非交换性对齐。

Result: 在dSprites、3DShapes、3DCars和CelebA数据集上，该方法有效提升了重构质量，实现了比beta-VAE、CLG-VAE和CFASL更一致的潜在因子交互，并展现出更清晰的顺序依赖性重构能力。

Conclusion: 引入Lie Group VAE框架，通过两阶段训练成功对齐了潜空间非交换结构与解码器重构行为。实验证明该方法在处理具有复杂几何变换的数据集时，表现出的重构质量和潜在因子交互一致性优于现有基准模型。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) often struggle to represent non-commutative structure in learned latent spaces. Symmetry-aware VAEs commonly address this issue by enforcing commutativity through algebraic regularization, which is appropriate for commutative transformation groups but can suppress meaningful non-commutative structure when it is intrinsic to the data. We argue that non-commutativity should instead be explicitly diagnosed and reflected in reconstruction behavior. We introduce a Lie Group VAE framework that combines geometric and algebraic perspectives on uncertainty while separating discrete generative factors from continuous geometric transformations. In a first phase, the model is trained without structural constraints while algebraic non-commutativity is measured through finite Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff deviations and decoder order sensitivity is measured through reconstruction order-swap tests. These diagnostics reveal a scale mismatch between latent non-commutativity and reconstruction behavior under unconstrained training. In a second phase, we introduce a deformation-stability constraint with a data-driven calibration constant that aligns decoder sensitivity with algebraic non-commutativity. We evaluate the framework on dSprites, 3DShapes, 3DCars, and CelebA against generic and symmetry-aware baselines, including beta-VAE, CLG-VAE, and CFASL. Across synthetic benchmarks, the method improves reconstruction quality and yields decoder-level behavior more consistent with latent non-commutative structure. Qualitative analyses show clearer order-dependent latent compositions and more stable reconstructions. On CelebA, the model yields more faithful reconstructions and factor-specific latent traversals than CFASL, while also exhibiting meaningful order-dependent interactions between learned latent directions.

</details>


### [145] [Class-Dependent Hybrid Data Augmentation for Multiclass Migraine Classification under Severe Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23453)
*Elvin Somón,Miguel A. Gutiérrez-Naranjo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过纠正偏头痛分类研究中的方法论盲点，结合临床亚型合并与比例约束的数据增强策略，成功提升了模型的分类性能与跨模型稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往偏头痛分类研究存在方法论缺陷（数据泄露、指标偏差），导致评估性能虚高；且未充分考虑临床定义的优化对分类性能的影响。

Method: 1. 修正先前研究的数据泄露与指标偏差；2. 基于ICHD-3标准合并偏瘫性偏头痛亚型；3. 实施类相关的混合数据增强策略；4. 引入“保真度不对称性”概念，采用比例受限增强而非强制类平衡。

Result: 修正基准后的Macro-F1为0.71；提出的框架在8种分类器上表现优于现有增强算法（Macro-F1均值0.862 vs 其他0.801-0.836）；Peak性能（FT-Transformer）达到0.914。

Conclusion: 偏头痛分类研究的核心在于合理的临床问题定义（如亚型合并），而非仅仅依赖增强算法。该框架显著提升了模型在不同分类器间的鲁棒性和平均性能。

Abstract: We conducted a reproducibility-oriented re-evaluation of prior migraine classification studies, correcting for data leakage and metric bias. We then introduced (i) a clinically motivated aggregation of two hemiplegic subtypes following ICHD-3 §1.2.3, (ii) a class-dependent hybrid augmentation strategy that assigns generation methods based on per-class sample size, and (iii) the concept of fidelity asymmetry, motivating proportionally constrained growth as an alternative to full class balance. Experiments were performed on a dataset of 400 patients across seven migraine subtypes under a two-stage protocol, including the six-class configuration described above. Models were evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with macro-averaged F1 as the primary metric.
  Correcting methodological flaws reduces previously inflated performance estimates, with the corrected macro-F1 baseline standing at 0.71. The proposed framework consistently outperformed individual augmenters in macro-F1 averaged across the eight evaluated classifiers (0.862 vs. 0.836 for Gaussian Copula, 0.815 for CTGAN, and 0.801 for the no-augmentation baseline), and achieved its peak result of 0.914 with FT-Transformer under proportional augmentation. The no-augmentation FT-Transformer baseline (0.896) shows that, at the per-classifier ceiling, clinically motivated class aggregation accounts for most of the absolute improvement; the framework's principal measurable contribution is the gain in average robustness across classifiers, highlighting the dominant role of problem formulation.

</details>


### [146] [S$^3$GNN: Efficient Global Mixing and Local Message Passing for Long-Range Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23467)
*Dai Shi,Luke Thompson,Linhan Luo,Lequan Lin,Andi Han,Junbin Gao,José Miguel Hernández Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出了现有谱GNN应对过平滑（OSQ）时的理论局限性，并提出了S$^3$GNN模型，通过轻量级设计有效改善了长距离信息捕获能力，显著提升了模型性能与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于谱滤波的GNN虽然能缓解过平滑（OSQ），但其理论保证（如Jacobian能量稳定）在实际应用中难以达到，且往往依赖于过于严苛的假设。作者旨在寻找一种无需此类限制假设且计算效率更高的OSQ缓解方案。

Method: 提出了S$^3$GNN框架。该方法通过轻量级方式重新引入被现有谱滤波器忽略的关键组件，从而在不增加复杂约束的情况下有效缓解OSQ，同时保持了特征变换的稳定性。

Result: 在长距离基准测试、KGQA（知识图谱问答）和基于网格的流体力学等任务中，S$^3$GNN实现了高达一个数量级的误差降低，且参数量减少了高达50%。

Conclusion: S$^3$GNN成功在不依赖严格理论假设的前提下缓解了过平滑（OSQ）问题，并在计算效率和模型参数量上实现了显著优化，在多项长距离依赖任务中表现优于现有方法。

Abstract: Message-passing neural networks (MPNNs) often suffer from an information bottleneck when capturing long-range dependencies, leading to the oversquashing (OSQ) phenomenon. Alongside spatial connectivity enrichment (e.g., rewiring), recent studies have shown that spectral filtering can yield strong long-range learning outcomes, as spectral operators enable global information mixing that alleviates OSQ. These approaches achieve this either by stabilizing the Jacobian energies in deep propagation or by guaranteeing OSQ mitigation under strong theoretical assumptions. We revisit these conclusions and show that the associated Jacobian sensitivity lower bound is generally difficult to achieve in practice. We then propose S$^3$GNN, which mitigates OSQ without such restrictive assumptions by lightweightly reintroducing omitted components with substantially lower computational complexity, while standard stability constraints on feature transformations remain effective under our new dynamics. Extensive experiments across diverse domains (e.g., long-range benchmarks, KGQA, and mesh-based fluid dynamics) demonstrate that S$^3$GNN achieves up to an order-of-magnitude error reduction with up to 50\% fewer parameters. Our code can be found in https://github.com/EEthanShi/S3-GNN.git.

</details>


### [147] [Automated Random Embedding for Practical Bayesian Optimization with Unknown Effective Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23473)
*Hong Qian,Xiang Shu,Xiang Xia,Xuhui Liu,Yangde Fu,Bei Liang,Huibin Wang,Liang Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高维贝叶斯优化中有效维度难以预估的问题，DSEBO 通过动态调整嵌入维度并共享历史查询数据，实现了更高的优化效率和精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯优化在高维场景下表现不佳，“随机嵌入”虽能通过低维子空间降维，但难以预先确定任务的有效维度。现有方法依赖专家经验或反复试错，效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种名为 DSEBO（动态共享嵌入贝叶斯优化）的算法。该方法从低维子空间开始，若当前解不再提升则动态切换到更高维的子空间；利用不同子空间中解的质量动态决定下一阶段维度，并将已查询的解共享至新空间以优化初始化过程。

Result: 理论上推导了 DSEBO 的遗憾界，证明其能够更好地平衡近似误差与优化误差；实验表明其在多种维度及真实任务中表现出显著的性能优势。

Conclusion: DSEBO 通过在不同子空间进行交替优化，有效解决了高维贝叶斯优化中有效维度未知的问题，在优化遗憾（regret）和运行时间方面均优于现有先进算法。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is widely employed for optimizing complex black-box functions but struggles with the curse of dimensionality. Random embedding, as a dimension reduction strategy, simplifies tasks that possess the effective dimension by optimizing within a low-dimensional subspace. However, determining the effective dimension of a task in advance remains a significant challenge, which influences the selection of the subspace dimensionality and the optimization performance. Traditional methods use fixed subspace dimensions provided by experts or rely on trial and error to estimate subspace dimensions with resources consumed. To this end, this paper proposes an automated random embedding for high-dimensional Bayesian optimization with unknown effective dimension, called Dynamic Shared Embedding Bayesian Optimization (DSEBO). DSEBO starts with a low dimension and switches to a higher subspace if the solutions in the current subspace show preliminary convergence. DSEBO dynamically determines the dimension of the next subspace based on the quality of the solutions in different subspaces and shares the queried solutions with the new subspace for a better initialization. Theoretically, we derive a regret bound for DSEBO and demonstrate that DSEBO can better balance approximation and optimization errors. Extensive experiments on functions with dimensionality of varying magnitudes and real-world tasks with unknown effective dimensions reveal that, compared with state-of-the-art methods, alternating optimization across different subspaces results in significant improvements in high-dimensional optimization, both in terms of optimization regret and time.

</details>


### [148] [VACE: Learning Geometrically Structured Representations for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23504)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Isaac Triguero,Julián Luengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VACE是一种用于多变量时间序列的自监督异常检测方法，通过优化潜在空间中正态轨迹的局部平滑度和方向一致性，实现了高效、高精度的异常识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对比学习方法在表征正态性时缺乏对潜在空间几何结构的显式控制，导致距离度量对异常检测的有效性受限。

Method: 提出了一种名为VACE（速度对齐通道嵌入）的方法，利用通道感知编码器和速度一致性目标进行自监督训练，确保正态轨迹在潜在空间中局部平滑且对齐；检测时结合马氏位置分数和速度库方向分数。

Result: 在TSB-AD-M基准测试中达到了SOTA（最先进）性能，且在模型复杂度和训练预算较低的情况下显著优于其他复杂方法。

Conclusion: VACE通过将正态行为定义为紧凑且与方向一致的轨迹，成功在不需要负样本或合成异常的情况下实现了高精度的多变量时间序列异常检测，超越了现有的复杂模型。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is a critical task across a wide range of real-world applications, where abnormal behaviour is rare, labels are unavailable, and the cost of a miss is high. The central challenge is learning a characterisation of normality precise enough to flag deviations. Representation self-supervised learning, typically through contrastive approaches, addresses this by embedding temporal patches into a latent space where normality occupies a well-defined region, with anomalies detected by geometric deviation. However, contrastive approaches shape this space indirectly through pair-sampling heuristics, providing no explicit control over the geometric structure that distance-based scoring requires. This means how tightly normal representations are grouped, and whether distances are directionally meaningful. We present VACE (Velocity-Aligned Channel Embeddings), a self-supervised anomaly detection method that represents normality as a compact, directionally coherent region in the embedding space. To this end, VACE trains a channel-aware encoder through a velocity-consistency objective, with no negatives and no synthetic anomalies, so that normal trajectories are locally smooth and aligned. At test time, a Mahalanobis positional score and a velocity-bank directional score are combined multiplicatively, flagging points that are simultaneously off-distribution and dynamically atypical. Despite its simplicity, VACE achieves state-of-the-art performance on TSB-AD-M under rigorous evaluation, significantly outperforming more complex methods trained on substantially larger budgets.

</details>


### [149] [Precise: SDE-Consistent Stochastic Sampling for RL Post-Training of Flow-Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23522)
*Jade Zou,Tao Huang,Weijie Kong,Junzhe Li,Yue Wu,Qi Tian,Jiangfeng Xiong,Jianwei Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Zhao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了优化扩散和流匹配模型的强化学习对齐，本文提出了 Precise 采样器。它通过平衡 SDE 的探索与稳定性，并引入冻结后验均值技术确保离散化过程的 SDE 一致性，从而在显著降低训练耗时的同时大幅提升了对齐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于在线强化学习优化流匹配生成模型时，现有的随机采样器在极小步数设置下往往会引入过多的离散化噪声或偏离理论轨迹，限制了奖励优化的效果和稳定性。

Method: 提出了 Precise 采样器：1. 分析并设计了平衡探索与稳定性的 SDE 调度；2. 采用了一种新的近似方法，通过冻结干净潜变量的后验均值（frozen clean-latent posterior mean）来确保去噪轨迹的 SDE 一致性。

Result: 在强化学习训练中实现了更快、更稳定的奖励优化；在 PickScore 和 HPSv2.1 等指标上达到了最先进的对齐水平，并将训练时间缩短了 13.1%-53.2%。

Conclusion: Precise 采样器通过保持 SDE 一致性，解决了现有采样器在强化学习应用中存在的过度噪声和偏离流匹配轨迹的问题，显著提升了奖励优化的性能和效率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective way to improve prompt alignment and perceptual quality in diffusion and flow-matching generators. A critical step for applying online RL to flow matching is turning the deterministic sampling trajectory into a stochastic policy, typically by replacing the reverse-time Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) with a Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE). The stochastic sampler, controlling the exploration behavior and denoising dynamics, is thus part of the policy, and its design can significantly affect the reward optimization performance. We break down the sampler design into two interdependent components: choosing the right amount of stochastic exploration, and discretizing the resulting SDE faithfully at the small step counts used in RL. To address the first component, we analyze the inherent tension between exploration and stability in denoising and derive an SDE schedule that balances the two. Turning to the discretization challenge, we use a toy example to show that existing samplers can deviate from the flow-matching process, either by introducing excessive discretization noise or by relying on heuristic rules that do not guarantee convergence to the data distribution. To address these issues, we propose Precise, a new stochastic sampler that balances effective exploration with stability. Crucially, Precise keeps the denoising trajectory SDE-consistent through a novel approximation that freezes the clean-latent posterior mean, resolving the excess noise issue in standard samplers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this formulation leads to significantly faster and more stable reward optimization via reinforcement learning, achieving state-of-the-art alignment scores (e.g., PickScore, HPSv2.1) while requiring 13.1-53.2% less wall-clock training time to match the best in-domain performance of prior samplers.

</details>


### [150] [When One Point Is Not Enough: Addressing Ambiguous Instances in Dimensionality Reduction by Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23540)
*Diede P. M. van der Hoorn,Alessio Arleo,Fernando V. Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过复制“模糊实例”来缓解降维中局部邻域信息丢失问题的方法，通过在投影图中多次映射该实例，实现了对复杂数据结构更忠实的视觉呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的降维（DR）方法在投影过程中存在不可避免的信息丢失，尤其是对于“模糊实例”（即在高维空间中同时属于多个互不相似邻域的数据点），单一位置映射会导致“局部邻域嵌入缺失”，使得其复杂的结构无法被完整展示。

Method: 提出了一种基于图的方法，旨在识别高维空间中相似度分布广泛的“模糊实例”，并通过在降维投影中多次复制这些实例，将其放置在各自归属的多个邻域中。该方法可与UMAP等基于局部图的降维技术结合使用。

Result: 通过实验和定量分析，证明了该方法有效减少了局部邻域嵌入的失真，并成功在投影中显影了此前被隐藏的邻域成员关系。

Conclusion: 通过允许将模糊实例复制到多个邻域中，该方法不仅减少了降维过程中的损失，还揭示了之前在单一投影表达中被隐藏的数据结构，为高维数据可视化提供了更准确的呈现。

Abstract: Dimensionality Reduction (DR) methods are widely used to visualize high-dimensional data. One key task in DR-based analysis is discovering neighborhoods, which relies on analyzing the fine-grained local structure of a projection. However, DR is an inherently lossy process; no technique can perfectly preserve the high-dimensional relationships, and projections therefore contain visual artifacts. In this paper, we highlight a typically overlooked source of visual artifacts: ambiguous instances. These are instances that are highly similar to multiple mutually dissimilar neighborhoods in the high-dimensional space. Standard DR methods cannot faithfully project such instances, since each data instance is mapped to a single point in the visual space. As a result, such an instance is placed in only one of its neighborhoods (or in none at all), so only part of its neighborhood structure is represented. We call this distortion partial neighborhood embedding. In this paper, we introduce a graph-based approach that identifies ambiguous instances and replicates them as multiple points in the projection, placing each copy within its respective neighborhood. We use UMAP for our results, but our approach also generalizes to other local graph-based DR techniques, and we show that our approach reveals previously hidden neighborhood memberships in projections and reduces partial neighborhood embedding across multiple examples, and is further supported by quantitative analyses.

</details>


### [151] [Understanding Goal Generalisation in Sequential Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23565)
*Jason Ross Brown,Edward James Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种预测强化学习智能体在分布外（OOD）环境下泛化行为的方法——“潜在策略梯度”，通过分析整个训练流水线中潜在变量的演化，有效揭示了强化学习智能体目标泛化的底层机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对强化学习智能体在训练分布之外出现的目标导向行为的系统性理解，特别是无法根据训练历史解释智能体如何泛化到新环境。

Method: 引入“潜在策略梯度”（latent policy gradients）方法。该方法将训练过程抽象为低维潜在变量的演化，通过建立潜在变量到行为的简化映射模型，预测特定训练流水线可能诱发的OOD行为。此外，通过评估超过100个序列化训练流水线及250个OOD环境对该方法进行验证。

Result: 研究发现显著特征（salient features）驱动泛化，且早期学习的目标会持久存在并影响后续目标的习得。所提出的潜在策略梯度方法在预测准确性、跨训练流水线泛化能力以及可解释性方面表现出色。

Conclusion: 强化学习智能体的分布外（OOD）行为虽然复杂，但并非不可预测。该研究证明了训练流水线（training pipeline）中存在可捕获的潜在结构，通过潜在策略梯度可以理解并预测智能体从发育视角出发的目标泛化规律。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents often exhibit unintended goal-directed behaviour outside their training distribution, but we currently lack a principled understanding of how such agents will generalise to novel environments based on their training history. We address this gap for agents trained sequentially on one or more tasks. We study over 100 sequential training pipelines, evaluating behaviour across over 250 out-of-distribution environments. We find that salient features drive generalisation, and that goals learnt early in training can persist and influence those acquired later. To explain these phenomena, we introduce latent policy gradients, a method that predicts what out-of-distribution behaviour a training pipeline will likely induce. Our method simulates the evolution of low-dimensional latent variables during training according to what would achieve high reward on the training objective with respect to a simple model of how the latent variables map to behaviour. It achieves strong predictive accuracy, generalises to unseen types of training pipeline, and is interpretable. Our findings demonstrate that while out-of-distribution RL agent behaviour is dependent on the whole training pipeline, this dependence has an underlying structure we can capture, laying groundwork for understanding goal generalisation from a developmental perspective.

</details>


### [152] [MARS: Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23563)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Afsaneh M. Nejad,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对传统 CD 图在评估机器学习模型时忽略性能差距量级的问题，提出了 MARS 方法，通过加权排名机制实现了更准确、更全面的模型性能比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关键差异（CD）图在评价机器学习模型时，仅依赖于离散的排名（ranks），完全忽略了模型之间性能差距的具体量级（magnitude-blindness），导致无法全面衡量模型的鲁棒性和一致性。

Method: 提出了一种名为“幅度感知秩统计”（Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics, MARS）的方法；它在传统离散秩的基础上，引入了一个相对边际系数（relative margin coefficient）作为权重，并结合动态投影技术来处理边界情况。

Result: MARS 生成的统计结果能够更真实地展现模型性能的差异，为在大规模实验设置中评估和比较不同算法提供了更深入的见解。

Conclusion: MARS 是一种比传统 CD 图更有效的评估方法，它通过引入相对边际系数解决了“量级盲点”问题，能够更真实、更具洞察力地反映模型间的性能差异。

Abstract: Comprehensive evaluation of machine learning models is the key to make sure that they perform as robustly and consistently as desired. In order to summarize the experimental results and pick a winner, Critical Difference (CD) diagrams are used. Standard CD diagrams rely on discrete ranks, discarding the magnitude of performance gaps between models, raising an issue which we call magnitude-blindness. In order to address this issue, we propose Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics (MARS) that incorporates a relative margin coefficient as a weight for the discrete ranks. This coefficient scales ranks based on the distance between the best and worst performers, with a dynamic projection to handle boundary cases. Followed by the calculation of a CD value, MARS results in a more realistic statistical representation of differences of model performances and more insights on how methods actually perform in vast and extensive experimental settings.

</details>


### [153] [Push Your Agent: Measuring and Enforcing Quantitative Goal Persistence in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23574)
*Yuandao Cai,Yuzhang Zhu,Liyou Gao,Wensheng Tang,Shengchao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长周期语言智能体无法完成定量目标的问题，本文提出了PushBench基准测试，揭示了智能体在维护任务进度和识别完成条件方面的局限性，并证明了专用控制器架构在提高持久性和可靠性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期语言智能体在执行任务时，往往能完成局部步骤，但无法维持进度直到达到预定的定量目标（Quantitative Goal Persistence, QGP），表现出中途过早停止、重复提交或进度漂移的问题。

Method: 提出了PushBench基准测试，用于衡量智能体在仓库工件收集任务中的定量目标持久性。设计并对比了多种控制器架构（状态跟踪检索控制器、待办清单跟踪工作单元控制器），并通过评估 Claude Code 和 Codex CLI 等模型进行验证。

Result: 状态跟踪检索控制器表现最佳（69-78%成功率），待办清单控制器在标准模型失败的场景下实现了25-50%的成功率。虽然前沿模型（Claude Code, Codex CLI）能解决50个工件的任务，但在100个工件的大规模任务下成功率显著下降，暴露了模型在长周期任务中的可靠性瓶颈。

Conclusion: 定量目标持久性（QGP）是长周期语言智能体面临的关键可靠性挑战。智能体必须具备维护经过验证的进度并在完成全部工作后才停止的能力，而不仅仅是依赖局部任务的正确性。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents can make many plausible local tool calls yet fail to persist until a requested count is actually complete. We study this gap as Quantitative Goal Persistence (QGP): whether an agent keeps working until an external verifier confirms enough distinct valid items. PushBench turns this into a benchmark for repository-artifact collection and verifier-backed work units, so repeated work, duplicate submissions, false completion, and progress drift are measured directly rather than hidden behind a final success flag. In matched controller comparisons, a state-tracking retrieval controller reaches 69-78% success while eliminating duplicate submissions, and a backlog-tracking work-unit controller reaches 25-50% success in settings where standard and completion-gated controllers complete no task instances. Black-box frontier-agent evaluations with Claude Code (Sonnet 4.6) and Codex CLI (gpt-5.4) solve many 50-artifact tasks but drop to 3 out of 9 successes per condition at 100 artifacts. The results show that quantitative goals stress a different reliability requirement from local task competence: agents must maintain verified progress and stop only when the requested work is complete.

</details>


### [154] [Preisach Attention: A Hysteretic Model of Sequential Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23603)
*Piotr Frydrych*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于 Preisach 迟滞算子的 PAL 架构，通过替换 softmax 注意力机制，实现了更低推理成本（O(n log n)）的高效长序列建模，并在处理速率无关的历史统计任务上展现了超越标准 Transformer 的潜能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有 Transformer 在长序列建模中存在的 O(n²) 推理成本问题，以及其对位置和时间间隔的高度依赖，同时探索替代 softmax 注意力机制在序列记忆能力和计算效率上的新路径。

Method: 引入 Preisach 注意力层（PAL），将传统的 Preisach 迟滞算子（通过学习到的激活/去激活阈值参数化的二元继电器）替代标准的 softmax 注意力机制，并维护一个局部极值栈作为内状态。

Result: 证明了单层 PAL-Transformer 在任意精度下是图灵完备的（仅需 O(1) 深度）；证明了 PAL 与 Transformer 的函数计算能力具有不可比性（PAL 在处理历史范围统计时效率更高）；证明了极值栈是速率无关函数值的充分统计量；推理成本降至 O(n log n)。

Conclusion: PAL 是一种高效的序列建模架构，特别适用于长情境记忆且对位置依赖较弱的任务。它不仅在计算复杂度和推理成本上优于标准 Transformer，还在函数表达能力上与 Transformer 形成了互补的不可比性，通过利用迟滞效应实现了对输入序列局部极值的高效处理。

Abstract: We introduce the Preisach Attention Layer (PAL), a novel sequence modelling architecture grounded in the classical Preisach hysteresis operator from mathematical physics. PAL replaces the softmax attention mechanism with a binary relay operator parameterised by learned activation and deactivation thresholds, maintaining a stack of local extrema as its internal state. A single-layer PAL-Transformer with O(1) depth is Turing-complete under arbitrary precision arithmetic, achievable through simulation of a two-stack pushdown automaton -- in contrast to the O(log n) depth required by standard hard-attention transformers. Second, we prove that the function classes computable by PAL and by the transformer are incomparable: PAL computes historical range statistics in O(1) layers that require O(log n) layers for transformers, while transformers support random-access retrieval that PAL cannot perform without auxiliary state. The separating property is rate-independence -- PAL responds only to the sequence of local extrema, not to absolute token positions or temporal spacing. Third, we show that the extremum stack constitutes a minimal sufficient statistic of the input history for all rate-independent functionals, providing a formal analogue of the wiping property in classical hysteresis theory. PAL is thus an efficient architecture for tasks with long episodic memory and weak positional dependence, with O(n log n) total inference cost versus O(n^2) for standard attention.

</details>


### [155] [Valid and Expressive Copulas for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23632)
*Christian Klötergens,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CopFITi 模型用于非规则多元时间序列预测，通过结合归一化流和混合高斯 Copula 实现了边缘与联合分布的解耦，在保证边缘化一致性的同时提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型直接拟合全联合分布存在局限；旨在解决非规则多元时间序列（IMTS）的概率预测问题，并改善边缘分布建模的准确性。

Method: 结合了用于建模单变量边缘分布的归一化流（Normalizing Flows）和用于建模联合依赖结构的混合高斯 Copula（Gaussian Mixture Copula）。构建了具有边缘化一致性的 IMTS Copula 模型。

Result: 实验结果表明，该 Copula 方法能够解耦边缘和联合分布，其边缘分布预测优于直接拟合全联合分布的架构，并确立了 IMTS 联合密度建模的新基准。

Conclusion: CopFITi 是一种针对非规则多元时间序列（IMTS）有效的概率预测框架，通过解耦边缘分布与联合依赖结构，实现了边缘化一致性，并在联合密度建模任务中达到了新的 SOTA 水准。

Abstract: We introduce CopFITi, a copula model for probabilistic forecasting of irregular multivariate time series (IMTS). Our model combines the expressivity of normalizing flows for univariate marginals with the consistency and flexibility of a Gaussian Mixture Copula for the joint dependency structure. Our experiments show that copula-based approaches, which decouple the marginals from the joint, yield better marginal models than architectures that directly fit the full joint.
