With CopFITi, we propose the first IMTS copula that is marginalization-consistent by construction and establish a new state of the art in joint IMTS density modeling.

</details>


### [156] [It's the humans, not the data: Geopolitical bias in LLMs originates in post-training, amplified by the language of the prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23825)
*Stuart Bladon,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，大语言模型中的地缘政治偏见主要是在后训练（对齐）阶段由人工干预形成的，而非源于预训练数据，且具有显著的开发商地域依赖性和语言敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战学术界普遍认为“大模型地缘政治偏见源于预训练数据”的传统观点，旨在探究模型地缘政治偏见的真实来源及对齐过程的作用。

Method: 对来自7个不同实验室的7对基座（Pre-training）与对话（Post-training）大模型进行对比测试；基于成对场景的强制选择探测任务（Forced-choice probe），涵盖28个国家对，且实验语种包括英语、法语和中文。

Result: 研究发现，在后训练后，模型会表现出明显的地理偏好，且这种偏向通常与模型开发实验室的所在地高度相关。例如，阿里巴巴的Qwen 2.5模型在后训练后表现出显著的选择偏向变化。此外，这种偏见具有语境依赖性，如Mistral模型仅在法语提示下表现出亲法倾向。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的地缘政治偏见主要源于后训练（Post-training）阶段而非预训练阶段。这表明模型对国家和文化的呈现受到对齐过程的显著影响，因此迫切需要对模型对齐过程进行更高的透明度审核与监督，以规范其政治视角。

Abstract: It has generally been assumed that geopolitical bias in language models originates from the training data used during the pre-training phase. We tested seven open-weight LLM pairs consisting of the base model (pre-training only) and the chat model (pre-training and post-training) from seven labs on a paired-scenario forced-choice probe over 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese, and found that geopolitical bias originates in post-training rather than in pre-training. Across seven AI labs, six showed shifts in the direction associated with the country or region of the model developer after post-training. This shift is strongest in Alibaba's Qwen 2.5: while the base is neutral on China-favourability (-0.15 log-odds, p=0.15), the post-trained chat variant is at +2.91 (p<10^-4), an 18x shift in odds. We also observe shifts in biases toward other countries across all models. Additionally, the magnitude of this shift depends on the language used to prompt the model: the French-made Mistral becomes pro-France only under French prompting (FR-EN shift +1.91, p<10^-4). These findings suggest that geopolitical preferences in language models are not simply inherited from large-scale internet data but are actively shaped during post-training, highlighting the need for greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes that influence how models represent nations, cultures, and political perspectives.

</details>


### [157] [Leveraging Foundation Models for Causal Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23861)
*Aneesh Komanduri,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FM-CGM框架，利用预训练基础模型实现零样本视觉因果推理与反事实图像生成，并通过CSG机制确保干预的语义一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果生成模型在训练过程中整合因果约束时，往往缺乏一个统一的框架来利用预训练基础模型（Foundation Models）强大的零样本推理能力，导致其在处理复杂视觉因果推理任务时存在局限。

Method: FM-CGM框架包含三个核心组件：概念提取器（Concept Extractor）、概念操纵器（Concept Manipulator）和反事实生成器（Counterfactual Generator）。利用大推理模型进行因果推理，结合文本到图像扩散模型生成图像，并引入“因果语义引导”（CSG）机制来确保干预效果在概念层级中的传播及不变区域的保持。

Result: 实证研究表明，该方法能够有效地识别合理的因果结构，并在不需要微调的情况下，实现忠实于原意图的反事实图像生成。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于预训练基础模型的模块化因果生成框架（FM-CGM），通过因果语义引导（CSG）机制实现了零样本的因果发现、干预和反事实生成，证实了在大规模生成模型中实现可信因果推理的可行性。

Abstract: Causal generative modeling is essential for developing reliable and transparent AI systems capable of counterfactual reasoning. While existing approaches focus on integrating causal constraints during the training of generative models, they often lack a unified framework to leverage the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pretrained foundation models. We introduce FM-CGM, a modular framework for end-to-end visual causal reasoning using pretrained foundation models. FM-CGM formalizes the causal pipeline through three core components: a concept extractor, a concept manipulator, and a counterfactual generator. By leveraging a large reasoning model for causal inference and a text-to-image diffusion model for generation, our approach enables zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation. We then develop Causal Semantic Guidance (CSG), a cross-attention-based mechanism that ensures semantic interventions propagate to descendant concepts while preserving invariant regions. We empirically show that our approach can identify plausible causal structures and is suitable for faithful counterfactual image generation.

</details>


### [158] [LLMs as Noisy Channels: A Shannon Perspective on Model Capacity and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23901)
*Xu Ouyang,Deyi Liu,Yuhang Cai,Jing Liu,Yuan Yang,Chen Zheng,Thomas Hartvigsen,Yiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了基于信息论的“香农标度律”（Shannon Scaling Law），通过引入信噪比分析机制，成功解释并预测了LLM训练中出现的非单调性能下降现象，显著优于传统的单调标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Scaling Laws多为单调幂律，无法解释大模型训练中出现的灾难性过拟合、量化导致的性能劣化等非单调现象。

Method: 将大模型训练建模为有噪信道下的信息传输，结合香农-哈特莱定理，提出模型参数与信道带宽、训练Token与信号功率的映射；引入信噪比（SNR）概念来分析模型性能随参数/数据规模的变化。

Result: 在Pythia和OLMo2模型上的实验显示，新模型能准确拟合损失函数中的“U型”谷底，在$R^2$指标上超越经典及现有修正模型；支持对更大规模模型和更多训练数据的外推预测（$R^2=0.847$）。

Conclusion: Shannon Scaling Law提供了一个比传统单调幂律更准确的框架，通过SNR视角解释了模型性能下降现象，并具备极强的预测和外推能力。

Abstract: Existing scaling laws for Large Language Models (LLMs), predominantly monotonic power laws, fail to explain emerging non-monotonic phenomena such as catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation, where performance deteriorates despite increased compute.
  We propose the Shannon Scaling Law, a unified theoretical framework that models LLM training as information transmission over a noisy channel, grounded in the Shannon-Hartley theorem. By mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, our formulation explicitly captures the interaction between learning signal and intrinsic noise. This perspective reveals a fundamental Shannon capacity for LLMs: scaling model size or data without preserving a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) inevitably amplifies noise, inducing a transition from monotonic improvement to U-shaped performance degradation.
  We validate our theory through experiments on Pythia and OLMo2 under perturbations, including Gaussian noise, quantization and supervised fine-tuning on math, QA and code tasks. The Shannon Scaling Law consistently outperforms classical scaling laws and recent perturbation-aware laws, achieving strong $R^2$ scores and accurately capturing loss basins missed by prior approaches. It also extrapolates: fitted on $\leq$6.9B Pythia models with $\leq$180B tokens, it predicts the unseen 12B model up to 307B tokens at pooled $R^2{=}0.847$, while monotonic baselines collapse.

</details>


### [159] [Approaching I/O-optimality for Approximate Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23751)
*Pál András Papp,Aleksandros Sobczyk,Anastasios Zouzias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文通过新的算法设计，将大模型注意力机制计算的I/O复杂度从二次方降至近线性，并证明了其接近I/O最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的注意力机制计算方法（如FlashAttention）的I/O复杂度与序列长度$n$呈二次方关系，而在理论上存在仅需$Ω(nd)$的I/O下界，论文旨在缩小这一差距。

Method: 受Alman和Song提出的近似注意力机制框架启发，开发了一种新的I/O高效算法。

Result: 证明了所提算法的I/O成本几乎与$n$呈线性关系，并给出了相应的下界证明。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种新的注意力机制计算算法，该算法在大多数参数范围内实现了近乎线性的I/O复杂度，并从理论上证明了该算法接近I/O最优。

Abstract: We revisit the I/O complexity of attention in large language models. Given query-key-value matrices $Q,K,V\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, and a machine with fast memory size $M$, the goal is to compute the "attention matrix" $A=\text{softmax}(Q K ^{\top}/\sqrt{d}) V$ with the minimal number of data transfers between fast and slow memory. Existing methods in the literature, most notably FlashAttention and its variants, incur an I/O cost that depends quadratically on $n$, while a trivial lower bound only requires $Ω(nd)$ I/O's to read the inputs and write the output. In this work, we present a technique for computing attention where the I/O cost only depends almost-linearly on $n$ in most parameter regimes. This is achieved by developing I/O-efficient algorithms inspired by the recent approximate attention framework of Alman and Song. We also prove corresponding lower bounds in each parameter regime to show that our algorithms are indeed close to I/O-optimal.

</details>


### [160] [SeedER: Seed-and-Expand Retrieval from Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23753)
*Hamed Shirzad,Frederik Wenkel,Dominique Beaini,Danica J. Sutherland,Emmanuel Noutahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对知识图谱检索的高效与精准难题，提出了一种名为SeedER的框架。该方法通过“先种子、后扩展”两阶段策略，利用强化学习优化局部决策，在保证覆盖率的同时有效控制查询成本，显著优于现有密集检索及图增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱的非规则结构导致现有检索方法存在局限：传统的自我图（ego-graph）扩展因爆炸式增长而昂贵，密集嵌入方法难以处理多跳组合查询，而现有的基于Agent的探索方法在海量数据下算力成本过高。

Method: SeedER（Seed-and-Expand Retrieval）框架：首先通过轻量级密集检索和实体检索确定“种子”节点，随后利用强化学习训练的图感知策略（graph-aware policy）对种子集合进行选择性扩展，从而实现低成本的图结构利用。

Result: 理论上论证了密集检索处理组合查询的局限性；实验表明，SeedER在紧凑候选集规模下展现出比强基线模型更高的召回率，具有显著的组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: SeedER有效地结合了密集检索的效率和图探索的可解释性，是知识密集型推理系统中一种高效的首阶段检索框架，在保持召回率的同时大幅度降低了查询成本。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer a rich representation for relational knowledge, but their irregular structure makes retrieval challenging: ego-graph expansion grows rapidly, and dense embedding methods struggle with multi-hop compositional queries. Existing agent-based graph exploration approaches, while expressive, are often too expensive for large-scale retrieval. We introduce SeedER (Seed-and-Expand Retrieval), a retrieval framework that explicitly leverages KG structure through iterative, low-cost expansion. SeedER first seeds a compact set of core nodes using lightweight dense and entity-based retrieval, then selectively expands this set via a learned graph-aware policy trained with reinforcement learning. This design decomposes global reasoning into reusable local decisions, enabling efficient discovery of query-relevant nodes while tightly controlling expansion cost. We show theoretical limitations of dense retrieval on compositional graph queries, and establish advantages of SeedER from both compositional generalization and graph-constrained submodular optimization perspectives. Empirically, SeedER substantially improves recall with compact candidate sets over strong dense and graph-augmented baselines, making it an effective first-stage retriever for knowledge-intensive reasoning systems.

</details>


### [161] [LLM-driven design of physics-constrained constitutive models: two agents are better than one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23754)
*Marius Tacke,Matthias Busch,Kian Abdolazizi,Jonas Eichinger,Kevin Linka,Roland Aydin,Christian Cyron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多智能体（Creator-Inspector）LLM框架，通过自动化的物理约束审核机制，解决了通过LLM生成本构模型时存在的物理违规问题，实现了高精度、物理有效且可靠的自动化模型发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单智能体LLM生成本构模型的方法缺乏系统性的物理约束校验机制，导致生成的模型可能违反基本物理定律，限制了其在实际工程和科研中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种多智能体LLM框架：Creator智能体负责根据数据生成本构模型，Inspector智能体负责基于预定义的九项物理约束进行严苛审核，若发现违规则反馈并要求重构；并在脑组织及橡胶材料数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。

Result: 在Claude Opus和Kimi上应用该方法，物理约束满足率分别提升至100%和56%，同时模型在保持高预测精度的前提下，展现出了优异的物理外推泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过引入多智能体（Creator-Inspector）架构，确保了LLM生成的本构模型在数学形式上严谨符合物理定律，显著提升了生成模型的可信度与实用性，为自动化、物理感知（physics-aware）的科学发现提供了可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Developing constitutive models that capture how materials deform under load traditionally requires years of specialized expertise in continuum mechanics, machine learning, and scientific programming. Large language models (LLMs) have recently been shown to lower this barrier by generating constitutive models on demand, but existing single-agent pipelines lack systematic checks that the resulting models respect fundamental physical laws. To close this gap, we introduce the first multi-agent LLM-driven approach for constitutive model generation: a Creator agent proposes a model tailored to the data, while an Inspector agent critically audits each proposal against nine physical constraints and returns it for refinement whenever a violation is detected. We demonstrate this concept with constitutive artificial neural networks (CANNs) and benchmark it on brain tissue, experimental rubber, and synthetic rubber, using two different LLM backbones (Claude Opus 4.7 and Kimi K2.5). Adding the Inspector raises the share of exported models that truly satisfy all physical constraints from 91% to a perfect 100% for Opus and from 37% to 56% for Kimi, while preserving near-baseline accuracy and remarkable generalization to unseen loading paths. In combination, the generated models are physically valid, highly accurate, and extrapolate reliably beyond the training data - properties that together make them directly usable in practice. Separating generation from inspection thus turns LLM-driven constitutive modeling into a genuinely trustworthy process. The paradigm is deliberately technique-agnostic and scales automatically with advances in LLM capability, opening a promising path toward automated, physics-aware model discovery.

</details>


### [162] [Debiased Negative Mining Improves Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23797)
*Bo Peng,Jie Lu,Guangquan Zhang,Zhen Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型（VLM）中OOD检测的“假阴性”问题，提出了一种基于去偏负标签挖掘的理论框架，通过蒙特卡洛采样方法显著提升了检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的OOD检测方法依赖启发式规则从无标签语料中挖掘负标签，存在严重的“假阴性”问题，限制了模型的检测潜力。

Method: 提出了一个理论框架用于修正负标签的采样偏差，通过间接近似负标签分布的方法，将去偏负标签挖掘转化为基于ID标签和无标签野外语料数据的蒙特卡洛采样过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在多种OOD检测基准测试中均达到了当前最优（SOTA）水平。

Conclusion: 该方法通过理论框架成功解决了视觉语言模型（VLM）中离群点（OOD）检测的采样偏差与标签挖掘难题，在多种OOD检测设置中取得了新的SOTA效果。

Abstract: Aiming at identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes, out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a pivotal approach to enhancing the reliability of machine learning models. This paper focuses on the burgeoning paradigm of post-hoc OOD detection with pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), where a popular pipeline is to detect OOD inputs by examining their affinities between ID labels and negative labels, i.e., those semantically different from ID labels. Due to the unavailability of target OOD labels, existing works predominantly rely on heuristic rules to mine negative labels from unlabeled wild corpus data. Despite the empirical success, we argue that the power of VLM-based OOD detection has yet to be fully unleashed since the notorious false negative problem is far from addressed in the literature. With this motivation, we are interested in addressing the challenge of mining true negative labels for OOD scoring. To this end, we develop a theoretical framework for correcting the sampling bias of negatives labels by indirectly approximating the distribution of negative labels. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that the debiased negative mining can be naturally converted into Monte-Carlo sampling based on ID labels and the unlabeled wild corpus data. Extensive experiments empirically manifest that our method establishes a new state-of-the-art in a variety of OOD detection setups. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/60pen9/Debiased-Negative-Mining-Improves-OOD-Detection-with-Pre-trained-VLMs}{\textcolor{red}{here}}.

</details>


### [163] [Complete-muE: Optimal Hyperparameter Transfer and Scaling for MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23893)
*Hongwu Peng,Ohiremen Dibua,Yuanjun Xiong,Yifan Gong,Jianming Zhang,Yan Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Complete-muE 是一个通用的超参数迁移框架，通过两阶段桥接技术解决了密集模型与稀疏 MoE 模型之间的超参数转换难题，实现了“调优一次，迁移至所有 MoE 配置”的实用化迁移，降低了大规模模型训练的调参成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超参数迁移方法（如 μP 或 SDE）无法直接应用于 MoE 模型，因为 MoE 的扩展（如专家数量、激活专家数变化）本质上改变了模型架构和每个专家的处理量。

Method: 提出名为 Complete-muE 的框架，通过两阶段桥接系统解决迁移问题：Bridge I 利用 active-width μP 和归一化路由比例处理密集 FFN 到密集 MoE 的转换；Bridge II 利用激活专家缩放（activated-expert scaling）处理密集 MoE 到稀疏 MoE 的转换，通过校正一阶 SDE 学习率/权重衰减偏移来处理模型结构变化。

Result: 在语言模型和扩散模型预训练实验中，Complete-muE 在不同架构和参数规模下保持了超参数最优值的稳定性，仅存在与 SDE 理论一致的微小偏移。

Conclusion: Complete-muE 实现了“一次调整密集模型超参数，即可迁移至所有 MoE 配置”的目标，在不进行昂贵超参数搜索的情况下，使 MoE 模型能够利用扩容带来的收敛速度提升。

Abstract: We propose Complete-muE, a framework which targets hyperparameter transfer across dense FFN and any Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) setups in transformer blocks. Existing tools such as $μ$P (requires fixed architectue) or SDE (requires fixed per-step token count) cannot directly solve the hyperparameter transfer problem in MoE setups because Dense to MoE transfer or MoE total experts scaling changes both architecture and tokens per expert. Complete-muE solves this challenge with a two-bridge system: Bridge~I maps between dense FFN and Dense MoE by active-width $μ$P with a normalized router scale. Bridge~II maps between Dense MoE and sparse MoE by activated-expert scaling, where the first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while a bounded residual $σ_0$ shift remains. The resulting transfer rule, which we term as Complete muE, covers changes in activated experts, total capacity, granularity, and shared/group-balanced hybrids for MoE models as well as network width/depth, batch size, and duration changes for general Transformer models. Extensive language model and diffusion model pretraining experiments confirm that complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts -- with only minor drift consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge~II. In practice this drift is small enough that hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations -- \emph{tune dense once, transfer to all} is the practical recipe at the core of Complete-muE. This enables MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling model capacity without costly hyperparameter search.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Conceptual Schema Inference for Tabular Datasets using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23105)
*Zhenyu Wu,Jiaoyan Chen,Norman W. Paton*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对异构 tabular 数据难以组织的问题，本文提出两种基于LLM的自动概念模式推断方法（GeSI与EmSI），能直接从表头和数值中提取实体类型、属性及关系，实现了大规模数据仓库的模式构建与结构化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 tabular 数据通常来源于异构数据源，导致在表现形式上存在不一致性，难以有效地理解和组织。现有研究多侧重于数据发现与探索，缺乏针对原始表格自动推断底层概念模式（实体类型、属性、关系）的有效解决方案。

Method: 提出了两种基于大语言模型（LLM）的模式推断方法：1. GeSI：利用生成式LLM通过表级和列级语义直接推断层级类型、属性及实体间的关系，并将其融合为全局模式；2. EmSI：利用基于LLM的表嵌入技术，按语义对表进行分组，在组内推断属性，并通过共享的属性模式构建层级结构。

Result: 实验分析表明，该方法在推断出的模式组件的简洁性和结构质量上表现优异，且具备良好的可扩展性，能够有效应对大规模数据存储库。此外，案例研究进一步验证了该方案在端到端模式推断场景下的实用价值。

Conclusion: 本文提出的GeSI和EmSI方法能有效从异构表格集合中自动推断出高质量、结构化的概念模式，为组织和管理大规模数据湖提供了一种可行的自动化方案。

Abstract: Large collections of tabular data from data lakes, web tables and open data portals often originate from heterogeneous sources, leading to representational inconsistencies. Understanding and organizing such repositories therefore remains a major challenge. While prior work has primarily focused on dataset discovery and exploration, this paper addresses the complementary problem of conceptual schema inference: automatically deriving a conceptual schema that captures entity types, attributes and inter-type relationships directly from raw tables. We propose two large language model (LLM)-based approaches that use only column headers and cell values: GeSI uses generative LLMs to infer hierarchical types and their attributes from table- and column-level semantics, and to integrate them into a global schema that also captures relationships across types; EmSI employs LLM-based table embeddings to group tables by column-level semantics, infer attributes within each group, and construct hierarchical structures from shared attribute patterns. Finally, we report an experimental analysis demonstrating the effectiveness of our approaches in terms of the conciseness and structural quality of the inferred schema components, their scalability to large repositories, and a case study illustrating end-to-end schema inference.

</details>


### [165] [BCTuner: LLM-Guided Monte Carlo Tree Search for Efficient Blockchain Knob Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23280)
*Yaoyi Deng,Chongyang Tao,Mingxuan Li,Xuelian Lin,Han Sun,Mingchao Wan,Shuai Ma*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BCTuner 提出了一个结合 LLM 知识推理与 MCTS 结构化搜索的区块链参数自动调优框架，显著降低了调优开销并大幅提升了系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许可链系统架构复杂、参数间存在耦合、且调优过程中的性能评估（部署与基准测试）成本极高，现有的调优方法难以有效建立参数逻辑与优化目标之间的映射关系。

Method: 提出 BCTuner 框架，核心包含两个关键技术：1. 基于 LLM 的知识引导推理，利用多源知识理解参数语义、约束及上下文；2. 基于蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）的结构化参数搜索方法，通过增量构建和自适应剪枝策略，在减少性能评估开销的同时优化配置。

Result: 在 Hyperledger Fabric 和 ChainMaker 上的测试表明，BCTuner 最高可带来 211.38% 的吞吐量提升，相较于当前最先进的方法性能提升 20%，同时将与系统的交互次数减少了多达 8 倍。

Conclusion: BCTuner 通过结合大模型推理与结构化搜索（MCTS），有效解决了许可链参数调优中的语义鸿沟和性能评估成本高昂的问题，为区块链系统的自动化调优提供了一种高效、智能的解决方案。

Abstract: Knob tuning plays a critical role in improving the performance of permissioned blockchains. However, efficient tuning remains challenging due to the architectural complexity of blockchains and the semantic gap between knob-specific logic and the numerical optimization requirements of tuning tools. In addition, configuration changes are often coupled across different stages of the transaction pipeline, making their performance impact difficult to isolate and predict. Since each trial requires deployment and distributed benchmarking, ineffective exploration incurs substantial cost. These challenges motivate BCTuner, a Large Language Model (LLM)-guided framework that combines knowledge-guided reasoning with structured search. BCTuner organizes multi-source tuning knowledge to support LLM-based reasoning over knob semantics, constraints, and deployment context. It formulates tuning as a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) process over structured action trajectories, where configurations are incrementally constructed, validated, evaluated, and refined rather than generated in one step. BCTuner further applies adaptive pruning to discard infeasible or low-potential branches before system evaluation. We evaluate BCTuner on Hyperledger Fabric and ChainMaker under diverse workloads and network settings. Experimental results show that BCTuner achieves up to 211.38% throughput improvement over default configurations and outperforms the state-of-the-art blockchain tuning method by up to 20% in performance, while requiring up to 8x fewer interactions with the blockchain system.

</details>


### [166] [A Pragmatic Approach to Learned Indexing in RocksDB: Targeted Optimizations with Minimal System Modification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23815)
*Shubham Vashisth,Olivier Michaud,Bettina Kemme,Oana Balmau*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了MountDB，通过在RocksDB中集成并优化“成品”学习型索引，解决了学习型索引在生产环境中的适应性与性能瓶颈，实现了在低改造成本下的显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型索引虽然具有低内存高吞吐的优势，但因缺乏对并发和持久化的支持以及过高的系统复杂性，难以在生产系统（如RocksDB）中直接应用，且在写密集型负载下效果不佳。

Method: 1. 提出针对Memtable的“重用机制（reuse mechanism）”，在频繁的数据刷新中保留结构化知识以应对写密集型负载；2. 在存储层通过适配器模式实现学习型索引替换，无需修改底层存储逻辑；3. 设计块感知（block-aware）的只读学习索引，优化I/O路径，确保单次查找的效率。

Result: 构建了MountDB系统，实验结果显示其写吞吐量比基准系统高出1.5倍，读吞吐量高出2.1倍。

Conclusion: 学习型索引完全有能力在不进行深度存储引擎重构的前提下集成至生产级数据库中。MountDB通过重用机制和块感知优化，成功在RocksDB中实现了性能的显著提升，为学习型索引的实际落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Learned indexes have emerged as a promising alternative to traditional index structures, offering higher throughput and lower memory usage by approximating the cumulative key distribution function with lightweight models. Despite these benefits, adoption in production systems remains limited, partly because learned indexes that support concurrency and persistence as effectively as, e.g., the B+-Tree, do not yet exist, while many research prototypes introduce substantial complexity. In this paper, we investigate whether off-the-shelf learned indexes can be integrated into a production database with minimal storage-engine redesign. Using RocksDB as a case study, we exploit its separation between in-memory Memtables and immutable on-disk files to deploy specialized indexes at each level. We show that directly applying existing learned indexes is insufficient under write-heavy workloads because frequent Memtable replacement prevents models from fully adapting. To address this, we introduce a reuse mechanism that preserves structural knowledge across Memtable instances. At the storage level, we replace RocksDB's disk index with a learned index without modifying the storage layer or read path. We further adapt a read-only learned index to be block-aware, enabling worst-case single-I/O lookups. We implement these techniques in MountDB, an extension of RocksDB. Experiments on large-scale workloads with diverse data distributions and access patterns show up to 1.5X higher write throughput and 2.1X higher read throughput than state-of-the-art systems, demonstrating that established learned indexes can be integrated into production systems with minimal overhead and substantial performance benefits.

</details>


### [167] [CHRONOS: Temporally-Aware Multi-Agent Coordination for Evolving Data Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23887)
*Joydeep Chandra*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对时序知识图谱数据市场，CHRONOS 提出了一种集成神经 ODE 索引优化、变点感知定价及差分隐私预算管理的架构，成功解决了数据演变、价值归属与隐私冲突的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态时序知识图谱数据市场存在三大耦合故障：边缘演变导致索引过时（召回率下降）、分布偏移导致 Shapley 定价失效、以及缺乏协调导致差分隐私预算过度消耗。

Method: 提出 CHRONOS 三层架构：第一层利用神经常微分方程 (Neural-ODE) 实现时序边缘衰减以保证召回率；第二层基于检测到的变点进行 Shapley 定价以处理分布偏移；第三层结合 EXP3-IX 算法进行自适应调度，并利用高斯机制保证差分隐私 (zCDP)。

Result: 在四个基准测试中，CHRONOS 达到了 0.937 的召回率（Recall@10），平均处理速度 2.74 QPS，延迟 161 ms，并在 zCDP 组合下将总隐私预算控制在 ε=4.25（δ=10⁻⁶）。

Conclusion: CHRONOS 提供了一个在时序知识图谱数据市场中兼顾数据更新、公平定价与隐私保护的有效解决方案。尽管在高隐私设置下估值存在噪声，系统仍凭借公共索引路由和自适应调度实现了极具竞争力的性能表现。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge-graph data marketplaces face three coupled failures in static designs: stale hybrid index shortcuts reduce recall as edges evolve, stationary Shapley pricing misattributes value after distribution shifts, and uncoordinated agents over-consume a shared differential-privacy budget. We present CHRONOS, a three-layer architecture providing a unified treatment of these challenges with explicit public and private separation. Layer one applies neural-ODE temporal decay to shortcut edges, providing a per-query expected recall-loss bound of Big-O of Pq lambda delta t, with a monotone-envelope guarantee reducing bound looseness to 1.8 to 3.2 times observed loss. Layer two conditions Shapley valuation on detected changepoints and provides finite-sample error guarantees under noise. Layer three uses EXP3-IX to achieve Big-O of the square root of T log T regret while enforcing epsilon and delta differential privacy via moments accounting. CHRONOS releases a privatized affinity matrix per epoch using the Gaussian mechanism; all retrieval and ranking are post-processing, incurring no extra privacy cost. We provide multi-epoch settlement, scalability analysis for 500 sellers, and comparisons against accelerated baselines. Across four benchmarks, CHRONOS shows 0.937 recall at ten, 2.74 queries per second, 161 ms latency, and total epsilon of 4.25 at delta of 10 to the power of negative 6 under zCDP composition. These results indicate a competitive operating point. A limitation is that at this privacy level, released valuations remain noise-dominated; utility derives primarily from public index routing and adaptive scheduling driven by low-sensitivity statistics.

</details>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [LFRAG: Layout-oriented Fine-grained Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Multimodal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22829)
*Yifan Zhu,Yu Mi,Yue Lu,Yanchu Guan,Zhixuan Chu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对视觉丰富文档，本文提出了LFRAG框架，通过将页面级检索优化为细粒度的块级检索，在提升问答精度（+7.2%）的同时大幅减少了冗余输入（-73.07%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多模态RAG系统依赖于粗粒度的页面级检索，无法捕捉视觉丰富文档中的细粒度语义和布局结构，导致检索准确性下降及上下文冗余。

Method: 提出了LFRAG框架：1. 使用布局分割将文档细化为块级（Block-level）检索单元；2. 设计了语义-布局融合编码器，通过交叉注意力（Cross-attention）整合局部语义与全局上下文；3. 采用块级延迟交互（Late interaction）检索机制；4. 构建了细粒度标注的基准数据集LFDocQA。

Result: 在LFDocQA数据集上，LFRAG达到了SOTA检索效果；在问答任务中，回答准确率较最佳基线提升了7.20%，且在生成任务中降低了73.07%的Token消耗。

Conclusion: LFRAG是一种针对视觉丰富文档的高效且精准的多模态RAG框架，通过块级检索显著提升了问答准确率并大幅降低了计算成本，设定了该领域的新基准。

Abstract: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as an effective paradigm for enhancing Large Language Models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, existing multimodal RAG systems predominantly rely on coarse-grained page-level retrieval, which fails to capture fine-grained semantic and layout structures in visually rich documents, thereby compromising retrieval accuracy and leading to redundant context in downstream tasks. To address these issues, we propose Layout-oriented Fine-grained Retrieval-Augmented Generation (LFRAG), a novel framework that advances multimodal RAG from page-level to block-level retrieval. We perform layout segmentation to construct semantically coherent fine-grained retrieval units and design a semantic-layout fusion encoder that integrates local semantics with global context via cross-attention. With block-level late interaction retrieval, LFRAG enables precise query-content alignment and reduces irrelevant content for downstream generation. To enable rigorous evaluation, we construct LFDocQA, a large-scale benchmark with block-level annotations spanning diverse document types, designed to assess both multimodal document retrieval and question answering with greater granularity than existing datasets. Extensive experiments on LFDocQA demonstrate that LFRAG achieves state-of-the-art performance on retrieval tasks, outperforms the best baseline by 7.20% in answer accuracy, and reduces token consumption by 73.07% in generation tasks, confirming LFRAG as an accurate and efficient framework for multimodal RAG over visually rich documents. Our code and datasets will be released soon.

</details>


### [169] [RAG4Outcome: A Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Framework for Prognostic Prediction in Chronic Osteomyelitis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22833)
*Daqian Shi,Pei Han,Jishizhan Chen,Yang Wang,Xiaolei Diao,Xianyou Zheng,Pengfei Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 RAG4Outcome，这是一个利用检索增强生成（RAG）技术处理异构多模态临床数据的新框架，旨在解决慢性骨髓炎预后评估中存在的效率低、数据难以整合及缺乏可解释性等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性骨髓炎因高复发率和复杂的康复路径，使得传统的临床评分系统在规模化、效率和一致性上存在局限；同时，临床数据的异构性使得现有需要对齐输入和大样本标注的多模态学习方法难以有效应对。

Method: 提出了一种名为 RAG4Outcome 的检索增强生成（RAG）框架，该框架整合了多模态临床数据（包括 PET-CT 影像报告、结构化手术与诊断记录、以及非结构化随访笔记），并结合特定领域的检索知识库与专家指导提示（expert-guided prompting）进行预测。

Result: 在真实案例的初步测试中，该框架展现出了显著的有效性和临床相关性。

Conclusion: RAG4Outcome 框架在慢性骨髓炎预后评估中具有良好的应用潜力，能提供更具可解释性、证据充分且临床可靠的预后决策支持，提升了AI辅助感染管理和术后护理的水平。

Abstract: Chronic osteomyelitis presents substantial prognostic challenges due to its high recurrence risk and complex postoperative recovery trajectories. Traditional assessment often relies on manual scoring systems, which limit scalability, efficiency, and consistency in clinical practice. Furthermore, the heterogeneous nature of clinical data poses challenges for current multimodal learning approaches that require aligned inputs and large annotated datasets. In this work, we propose RAG4Outcome, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework for prognostic prediction in chronic osteomyelitis. Our method integrates multimodal clinical data, including PET-CT imaging reports, structured surgical and diagnostic records, and unstructured follow-up notes, into a unified prediction pipeline. By combining a domain-specific retrieval corpus with expert-guided prompting, the framework enables more interpretable, evidence-grounded, and clinically reliable prognosis. Preliminary results on real-world cases demonstrate promising effectiveness and clinical alignment, highlighting the potential of RAG4Outcome for AI-assisted infection management and postoperative decision support.

</details>


### [170] [From Head to Tail: Asymmetric Knowledge Transfer in Long-tail Recommendation with Generative Semantic IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23310)
*Chenyi Yan,Ruocong Tang,Xing Fang,Yang Huang,He Guo,Jing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AKT-Rec 提出了一种利用 LLM 生成语义 ID 的非对称知识迁移框架，通过聚类引导的自适应嵌入和分层特征聚合技术，显著缓解了电商长尾推荐中的数据不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台面临严重的长尾数据不平衡问题；现有模型难以有效融合多模态内容特征与协同过滤信号，且未能处理从头到尾 ID 的非对称知识迁移问题（即尾部噪声干扰头部表示）。

Method: 1. 利用多模态大模型（MLLM）进行监督微调，获取对齐后的语义表示；2. 使用残差量化变分自编码器（RQ-VAE）将表示离散化为语义 ID；3. 提出基于聚类的自适应嵌入模块（Cluster-Guided Adaptive Embedding），通过非对称对比学习将知识从头部 ID 迁移至尾部 ID；4. 采用层次化特征聚合（Hierarchical Feature Aggregation）模块，针对不同活跃度的样本动态融合多视角特征。

Result: 离线实验中 AUC 提升 0.35%，GAUC 提升 1.53%；在线 A/B 测试中 CTR 提升 2.76%，GMV 提升 3.47%。

Conclusion: AKT-Rec 通过引入语义 ID 和非对称知识迁移机制，有效解决了长尾推荐中数据不平衡和协同信号利用不足的问题，并在离线评估和工业在线生产环境中均表现出显著的性能提升。

Abstract: Long-tail recommendation in real-world e-commerce platforms remains challenging due to severe data imbalance. Existing methods often struggle to combine content-based multimodal features with collaborative signals. Many of these methods also ignore an important asymmetry in knowledge transfer between head and tail IDs: noisy signals from tail IDs can hurt representation learning for head IDs. This paper presents AKT-Rec, a framework for Asymmetric Knowledge Transfer in long-tail Recommendation that uses LLM-generated semantic IDs. AKT-Rec uses Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) with supervised fine-tuning to align content representations with collaborative information for both items and users, producing semantic representations. It then discretizes these representations into semantic IDs with a Residual-Quantized VAE (RQ-VAE), which yields semantic clusters of similar entities. AKT-Rec has two main components: (1) Cluster-Guided Adaptive Embedding, which decomposes each ID representation into a cluster-level embedding that captures shared semantics and an individual embedding. Through an asymmetric contrastive objective and an activity-aware gating mechanism, this module directs knowledge transfer from head to tail IDs. (2) Hierarchical Feature Aggregation, which builds parallel feature views and adaptively fuses them to optimize predictions for samples with varying activity levels. Extensive experiments on a large-scale industrial dataset and online A/B testing on the Alibaba Tmall platform demonstrate the effectiveness of AKT-Rec. AKT-Rec improves offline performance by 0.35% in AUC and 1.53% in GAUC, outperforming several competitive baselines. In online A/B testing, AKT-Rec achieves a 2.76% increase in CTR and a 3.47% increase in GMV, validating its utility in real-world production environments.

</details>


### [171] [Towards Generalizable and Efficient Large-Scale Generative Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23312)
*Qiuling Xu,Ko-Jen Hsiao,Moumita Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了将生成式推荐模型从2M扩展至1B参数并在生产环境落地的挑战。通过引入缩放法则诊断工具及针对性的训练/推理优化技术，在实际生产中取得了显著的精度提升，证明了大规模生成模型在推荐系统中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练生成的推荐模型在生产环境中面临挑战，预训练收益往往难以直接转化为下游任务的提升，受限于任务天花板、训练成本、服务延迟及物品新鲜度等生产约束。

Method: 1. 引入偏移缩放法则（offset scaling-law fits）作为模型扩容的诊断工具；2. 采用多Token预测（multi-token prediction）以优化服务延迟；3. 使用采样Softmax（sampled softmax）与投影解码头（projected decoding head）提高训练效率；4. 构建语义物品塔（semantic item towers）结合协同嵌入掩码（collaborative-embedding masking）解决冷启动问题。

Result: 在100万用户的为期一周的生产环境影子评估中，1B参数规模的模型在所有评估任务上均实现了比2M参数模型更高的MRR（平均倒数排名）。

Conclusion: 将模型规模视为生产迁移问题的一个组成部分，需同时关注任务空间（task headroom）、解码成本、服务延迟对齐及冷启动下的语义泛化能力。其所提出的优化方案使1B参数模型在生产环境中表现优于2M基线模型。

Abstract: Generative recommendation models can model user behavior as sequences of events and provide a shared backbone for multiple recommendation tasks. In production, however, pre-training gains do not automatically translate into downstream application improvements: task headroom, repeated-training cost, serving latency, and item freshness all affect transfer. We describe our experience scaling a generative recommender from 2M to 1B backbone parameters, excluding embedding and decoding layers, in a production-scale title recommendation setting. Across multiple downstream tasks, we observe task-dependent scaling behavior: some tasks approach an empirical ceiling within the observed scale range, while others continue to benefit from additional capacity. This motivates using offset scaling-law fits as a diagnostic for where additional model scale may be more or less useful.
  We then study production constraints that arise when applying the model in practice. Frequent retraining over trillions of behavior tokens makes training and decoding efficiency important; cached serving can make the immediate next-token target stale; and newly launched titles may need to be scored from semantic metadata before collaborative ID embeddings are reliable. We address these issues with multi-token prediction for serving-latency alignment, sampled softmax and a projected decoding head for efficient repeated training, and semantic item towers with collaborative-embedding masking for cold-start adaptation. In a one-week production-shadow evaluation over 1M users, the 1B-backbone model achieves higher MRR than the 2M-backbone baseline across all reported tasks. Overall, the results support treating model scale as one component of a production transfer problem, alongside task headroom, decoding cost, serving-latency alignment, and item generalization.

</details>


### [172] [TPMM-DPO: Trajectory-aware Preference-guided Model Merging for Iterative Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23398)
*Lingling Fu,Yongfu Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对迭代式 DPO 中参考模型误差累积导致后期性能不稳定的问题，本文提出 TPMM-DPO，通过自适应融合优化轨迹上的多个模型作为参考，提升了模型对齐的质量与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代式 DPO 中，若将前一轮模型循环作为下一轮的参考模型，会导致偏好数据噪声和参考模型误差的累积，引发模型后期过度优化、性能波动及泛化能力下降。

Method: 提出了一种轨迹感知偏好引导模型合并（TPMM-DPO）方法。该方法将迭代式 DPO 生成的策略模型序列视为优化轨迹，并通过学习得到的融合权重对这些模型进行自适应集成，从而动态构建参考模型。

Result: 与标准迭代式 DPO 相比，TPMM-DPO 在域内和域外评估中均展现出更高的胜率和奖励分数，显著缓解了训练中后期的性能衰退，且在鲁棒性分析中证明了其可学习权重融合方案优于简单的模型平均。

Conclusion: TPMM-DPO 通过自适应融合整个优化轨迹上的模型，构建了更平滑、更鲁棒的参考模型，有效克服了迭代式 DPO 中噪声偏好数据导致的误差累积问题，提升了模型的泛化能力与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has been widely adopted for large language model alignment due to its simple training procedure and lack of an explicit reward model. However, in iterative DPO, when the policy model from the previous iteration is repeatedly used as the reference model for subsequent rounds, noise in preference data and errors in the reference model accumulate over time. This accumulation can lead to late-stage over-optimization, performance fluctuations, and degraded generalization.
  To address these issues, we propose TPMM-DPO, a trajectory-aware preference-guided model merging method. The method treats the sequence of policy models generated during iterative DPO as an optimization trajectory and adaptively integrates them using learned fusion weights, thereby constructing a smoother and more robust reference model. In contrast to conventional iterative DPO, which relies solely on a single previous model, TPMM-DPO effectively mitigates error accumulation induced by noisy preferences and improves training stability.
  Experimental results show that standard iterative DPO often suffers from performance degradation in the middle and later stages of training, whereas TPMM-DPO consistently improves generation quality and achieves higher win rates and reward scores on both in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations. Further ablation studies and robustness analyses demonstrate that, compared with simple averaging, learnable-weight fusion more effectively alleviates late-stage performance degradation caused by noisy preferences.

</details>


### [173] [HARNESS-LM: A Three-Phase Training Recipe for Harnessing SLMs in Sponsored Search Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23572)
*Vipul Gupta,Shikhar Mohan,Lakshya Kumar,Pranjal Chitale,Nikit Begwani,Amit Singh,Manik Varma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大规模商业检索任务，本文提出了HARNESS-LM（HLM）三阶段蒸馏框架，将大型SLM的能力高效迁移至轻量级模型，在保持高精度的前提下，实现了显著的生产环境性能加速与商业指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型嵌入模型（如4B/8B参数的Qwen3）在检索质量上表现优异，但因生产延迟过高而难以在对延迟敏感的高吞吐量商业检索任务（如Bing Ads）中落地。

Method: 三阶段训练框架：(1) 基于十亿级参数SLM训练高性能教师检索器；(2) 通过L2损失函数将知识蒸馏至600M参数以下的轻量级学生编码器；(3) 使用对比学习对学生模型进行精调。

Result: HLM在Bing Ads基准测试中恢复了教师模型98%以上的精度，同时实现了27倍的推理延迟降低和20倍的吞吐量提升；线上A/B测试显示营收（Revenue）提升1%，展示了极佳的工业应用性能。

Conclusion: HLM是一种高效的训练框架，通过三阶段蒸馏将大型生成式检索模型的能力迁移至小参数模型，在保持高检索精度的同时显著降低延迟并提升吞吐量，经生产环境验证具有显著的商业价值。

Abstract: In the competitive landscape of sponsored search, balancing retrieval quality with production latency is a critical challenge. While large retrieval models based on Small Language Models (SLMs) such as Qwen3-Embedding-4B/8B set strong upper bounds on public benchmarks, their deployment in high-throughput, latency-sensitive environments remains impractical. In this paper, we present HARNESS-LM (HLM), a three-phase training framework for transferring the capabilities of large-scale retrievers into compact, cost-efficient models. The approach comprises: (1) training a high-performance reference ("teacher") retriever by fine-tuning a billion-parameter-scale SLM; (2) aligning query representations via an L2 objective to distill knowledge into a sub-600M parameter student encoder; and (3) applying a final contrastive refinement stage to optimize the student for retrieval performance. We also present a comprehensive empirical study of key design choices, including alignment objectives, embedding dimensionality, model scale, architecture, and optimization strategies, to identify configurations that are most effective in production settings. On a real-world Bing Ads evaluation benchmark, HLM recovers over 98% of the reference retriever's precision across multiple settings, while delivering up to 27x lower online query-encoder latency and 20x higher throughput on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. Online A/B testing on Bing Ads further shows a +1% Revenue, +0.6% Impression, and +0.4% Click uplift over the current ensemble of retrievers running in production with the deployed 190M parameter model, clearly highlighting the practical efficacy of the HLM recipe in a real-world sponsored search setting.

</details>


### [174] [Synthetic Sources?: Auditing Generative Search Engine Citations for Evidence of AI-Generated Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23684)
*Mowafak Allaham,Nicholas Diakopoulos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究审计了四款主流生成式搜索引擎，发现它们在回答公共领域查询时，约有16%的引用源来自AI生成内容，表明现有机制未能有效过滤不可靠的合成信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式搜索引擎普及，AI生成内容在网页中泛滥。若搜索引擎无法剔除AI生成的不可靠来源，将误导用户将其等同于权威来源，造成潜在风险。因此，需要评估这些系统在引用源可靠性方面的表现。

Method: 针对政治、健康、环境三大公共领域，向ChatGPT、Copilot、Gemini、Perplexity四款搜索引擎输入712个真实人类查询，通过审计分析其引用的信息来源。

Result: 四款引擎均存在引用AI生成源的情况（约占被引用来源的16%）；发现部分域名被频繁引用，且系统在来源选择上呈现出高度集中于少量域名而分散于大量微量域名的偏好。

Conclusion: 生成式搜索引擎在引用来源时缺乏有效区分AI生成内容与权威内容的能力，存在信息质量风险。研究呼吁提升系统治理水平及公众对AI生成内容局限性的认知。

Abstract: The growing accessibility of Large Language Models via conversational interfaces capable of responding to users' questions by drawing on, synthesizing, and citing information from the web (i.e., Generative Search Engines) has simplified the information-seeking process for users. However, with the proliferation of AI-generated content on the web, it is unclear whether these engines can reliably omit citing synthetic sources (i.e., AI-generated sources). Should these engines be unable to do so, this puts users at risk of harm by treating information from AI-generated sources synthesized in responses of generative search engines as equivalent to information from authoritative or official sources. In a step towards identifying whether AI-generated sources are being cited by these engines, this work presents an audit of four generative search engines (ChatGPT, Copilot, Gemini, Perplexity) using a total of 712 real-world human-generated queries spanning domains of public importance: politics, health, and the environment. Our findings show evidence of AI-generated sources being cited across all four generative search engines (~16% of cited sources) and identifies key source web domains these sources belong to that are frequently cited across these engines and topics. In addition, we observed that generative search engines include a somewhat narrow set of repeatedly cited domains while predominantly surfacing a large number of minimally cited domains in responses to users' queries. These findings contribute to the growing body of work on assessing the risks of generative search engines with the objective of increasing public awareness of their limitations and encouraging appropriate measures to improve information quality and governance of these systems.

</details>


### [175] [TubiFM: Unified Item, Carousel, and Search Ranking for Streaming Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23702)
*Alexandre Salle,Chenglei Niu,Suchismit Mahapatra,Xiaoxiao Chen,Suvash Sedhain,Yaqi Wang,Shervin Shahryari,Saurabh Agrawal,Qiang Chen,Michael Tamir*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TubiFM 提出了一种将用户跨场景行为统一表示为序列的方法（“用户故事”），并利用 Llama 3.2 模型实现了一个统一的排序框架，有效整合了多任务信息，在提升搜索和推荐效果的同时，大幅降低了系统维护复杂度和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化系统往往为搜索、推荐和轮播等不同任务分别训练独立模型，导致各个任务间蕴含的互补用户行为信号（如搜索意图对观看行为的解释、观看史对搜索意图的影响）未得到充分利用，且系统维护复杂。

Method: 1. 引入“用户故事”（user story）：将用户跨场景（搜索、观看、推荐）的历史行为序列化为统一的 token 序列；2. 架构设计：基于 Llama 3.2 1B 模型，采用预训练语言 token 与特定领域事件 token 交替的架构；3. 统一建模：将推荐、搜索及轮播（carousel）排序任务转化为统一的提示词引导下的“下一 Token 预测”任务，取消了传统的任务特定架构。

Result: 1. 线下评估：TubiFM 在搜索、轮播和推荐排序基准测试中均优于对应的专业基础模型；2. 在线 A/B 测试：搜索总观看时长（TVT）提升 3.9%，轮播 TVT 提升 0.30%，项目推荐 TVT 持平（+0.14%）；3. 推理效率：在 L40S GPU 上实现部署，将 p99 延迟从 500ms 降低至 200ms。

Conclusion: “用户故事”（user story）这一统一表示形式为个性化推荐与搜索任务提供了一种高效、简洁且统一的模型架构，证明了多任务学习在跨场景用户数据建模中的有效性，并在降低延迟的同时提升了业务指标。

Abstract: Personalized discovery systems often train separate models for item ranking, carousel ranking, and search, even though these tasks expose complementary signals from the same viewer journey: watches shape carousel and item ranking, search queries reveal intent even when they do not lead to a catalog match, and watch history helps interpret search as rewatching, continuation, or new discovery. We introduce the user story, a serialized representation that turns a user's cross-surface history - attributes, sessions, watch events with surface and carousel context, and search events - into a single token sequence. By interleaving pretrained language tokens with domain-specific event tokens, user stories let heterogeneous recommendation and search tasks be expressed as prompted next-token prediction over a shared grammar. TubiFM is one instantiation of this approach: a Llama 3.2 1B-based model trained on user stories and prompted to rank items, carousels, or search results without task-specific architectures. In offline evaluation, this single model outperforms specialist baselines across item, carousel, and search ranking. In online A/B tests, TubiFM significantly improves search total viewing time (TVT) by $+3.9\%$ and carousel TVT by $+0.30\%$. Item ranking is statistically neutral on TVT ($+0.14\%$), but matches a mature production stack; across all three tasks, TubiFM serves on L40S GPUs and reduces p99 ranking latency from 500ms to 200ms. These results show that shared user stories can improve discovery while simplifying ranking systems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [KAPLAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Prognostic Learnable Activation Networks for Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23082)
*Stelios Boulitsakis Logothetis,Angela Wood,Pietro Li ò*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: KAPLAN-HR 是一种基于 B-spline KAN 的新型非参数生存分析模型，无需手动特征工程即可自动捕捉复杂的协变量交互和时变风险，且具有良好的维度独立收敛性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生存分析方法（如 Cox 模型和 GAM）依赖繁琐的手动指定交互项和时变效应，在面对现代富文本临床数据集时显得力不从心。

Method: 提出 KAPLAN-HR 模型，利用基于 B-spline 的 Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) 对条件风险函数进行非参数估计。利用单层 KAN 回归 GAM 特性，通过深层架构捕捉复杂的交互作用和时变效应。

Result: 在六个临床基准数据集上的实验表明，KAPLAN-HR 的预测性能达到或超过了现有的主流统计方法和深度学习生存分析模型，并证明了其收敛率与协变量维度无关。

Conclusion: KAPLAN-HR 通过引入 KAN 结构，有效地解决了生存分析中高维变量与时间相关性的建模难题，同时具备了良好的可解释性和理论收敛性。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to model how covariates and time jointly shape the time-to-event distribution under right censoring. Classical methods such as the Cox model and generalised additive models (GAMs) require interactions and time-varying effects to be manually specified, which is increasingly impractical on rich clinical datasets. We introduce KAPLAN-HR, a B-spline Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) for nonparametric estimation of the conditional hazard as a joint function of covariates and time. A single-layer KAPLAN-HR model recovers a GAM, while deeper architectures capture interactions and time-varying effects through composition. We establish a convergence rate for the nonparametric KAN hazard estimator that depends only on the smoothness of the underlying KAN representation and not on the covariate dimension, thereby mitigating the curse of dimensionality for KAN-representable targets. In evaluations over six clinical benchmark datasets, KAPLAN-HR matches or exceeds the predictive performance of established statistical and deep learning survival methods.

</details>


### [177] [LLM Sparsity Prior for Robust Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23102)
*Caleb Skinner,Yihan Guo,Meng Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对LLM在变量选择中权重不准导致的性能下降问题，提出LSP模型，通过层次化超先验动态学习并调整LLM权重的影响，提升了鲁棒性与预测准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用LLM进行高维变量选择的方法（如LLM-Lasso）高度依赖于LLM生成的权重质量，当权重不准确时，模型性能会显著下降。

Method: 1. 引入权重质量量化框架；2. 提出LLM Sparsity Prior (LSP)，将LLM权重整合进Spike-and-Slab模型；3. 通过两个可解释超参数控制稀疏性和权重集中度；4. 采用层次化超先验（Hierarchical Hyperpriors）来动态折扣不可靠的LLM权重；5. 设计针对性的提示工程策略。

Result: LSP模型对提示词变化具有稳健性，在低数据量环境下表现尤为出色，能够提高预测准确性，并识别出基线方法遗漏的具有临床意义的特征。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为LSP的方法，通过层次化超先验机制实现了对LLM权重质量的不确定性建模，解决了LLM-Lasso在权重噪声下的脆弱性，并在医疗数据应用中展示了预测性能和特征识别的优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a scalable mechanism to elicit domain-informed prior information for high-dimensional variable selection. However, existing methods such as LLM-Lasso are sensitive to weight quality, with performance degrading substantially when LLM-generated weights are inaccurate. To address this challenge, we first introduce a framework for quantifying the quality of LLM-generated weights, enabling rigorous evaluation of LLM-informed methods across varying weight regimes. We then propose the LLM Sparsity Prior (LSP), which integrates LLM-generated weights into the prior inclusion probabilities of Spike-and-Slab and Spike-and-Slab Lasso models via two interpretable hyperparameters governing global sparsity and weight concentration. Hierarchical hyperpriors on these parameters allow the model to dynamically discount uninformative or misleading weights, improving robustness without sacrificing gains when weights are accurate. Finally, we develop principled prompt engineering strategies and validate the method on a private medical dataset studying Acute Kidney Injury. LSP improves prediction accuracy and identifies clinically relevant features missed by the baselines, with robustness to prompt variation and particular effectiveness in low-data regimes.

</details>


### [178] [Operationalizing Individual Fairness via Gradient Descent and Bradley-Terry Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23145)
*Conlan Olson,Linjun Zhang,Zhun Deng,Pragya Sur*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于三元查询学习马氏距离度量的算法（谱初始化+梯度下降），解决了实现个体公平性时度量设置的难题，并从理论和实验上证明了该方法的收敛性及对提升算法公平性的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体公平性（Individual Fairness）的实现高度依赖于“个体间相似度度量”的获取，但在实践中，学习这种度量标准非常困难。因此，研究旨在通过三元查询直接从数据中学习该度量。

Method: 提出了一种从三元查询（triplet queries）学习马氏距离度量（Mahalanobis similarity metric）的算法。该算法结合了谱初始化（spectral initialization）与梯度下降（gradient descent），并在 Bradley-Terry 模型框架下进行建模。

Result: 理论上证明了算法能快速收敛至真实度量；实验结果验证了算法的收敛速度，并展示了在下游公平性预测任务中的优良表现，证实了该方法能有效提升公平性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，尽管损失函数具有非凸性，但所提出的算法能有效学习到马氏距离度量；且证明了基于估计度量实现的个体公平性，足以推广到真实度量下的公平性，为解决个体公平性中度量学习难题提供了理论与实验支撑。

Abstract: Individual fairness, the notion that "similar individuals should be treated similarly," provides a strong and flexible fairness guarantee for algorithmic decision makers. However, a barrier to implementing individual fairness in practice is the difficulty of learning the similarity metric over individuals. In this work, we present an algorithm for learning a Mahalanobis similarity metric from triplet queries of the form "is individual $i$ more similar to individual $j$ or $k$?" We work in the standard Bradley-Terry model for pairwise comparisons. Our algorithm consists of a spectral initialization step followed by gradient descent. We provide extensive theoretical guarantees on our algorithm, showing that it converges quickly to the ground truth metric despite the non-convexity of the loss in our model. Because our focus is on fairness, we also show that individual fairness with respect to an estimated metric is sufficient to achieve similar fairness with respect to the true metric. We also discuss potential applications of our work to AI model tuning. Finally, we present experimental results that demonstrate the convergence of our algorithm and the fairness performance of downstream fair predictors trained on our estimated metric.

</details>


### [179] [Coupled Training with Privileged Information and Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23268)
*Jiahao Shi,Omar Hagrass,Jason M. Klusowski*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对部署时缺失额外训练信息的问题，本文提出了一种联合训练方法，替代容易产生错误传递的“两阶段”训练，成功解决了弱/噪声辅助信息造成的性能恶化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多预测场景中，训练时可用的额外信息（如昂贵或延时数据）在部署时无法获取。虽然传统的“两阶段法”（先利用所有数据训练教师模型，再由其指导部署模型）很常见，但在额外信息质量较差（弱或噪声大）时，这种方法会将错误传递给部署模型，导致预测准确率下降。

Method: 提出一种“联合训练”（Joint Training）方法，将部署模型与辅助模型同时进行训练，而非像传统“两阶段法”那样串行训练。同时推导了联合训练提升预测准确率的理论边界，并设计了一种适用于大规模高维模型的交替训练算法。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界预测任务中的实验表明，该联合训练方法鲁棒性更强，且在预测准确率上持续优于标准的“两阶段”基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过联合训练方法，模型能够有选择性地从训练时特有的额外信息中获益（仅在有效时利用），从而避免了两阶段法可能导致的错误传递。该方法在理论上提供了解析，并在实验中表现出比两阶段法更强的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: In many prediction problems, we have extra information during training (for example, measurements that are expensive or slow to collect) that will not be available when the model is deployed. A common strategy is to first train a model that uses all training information, then use its predictions on unlabeled examples to train a second model that only uses the inputs available at test time. However, when the extra training-only information is weak or noisy, this Two-Stage approach can mislead the deployment model and even hurt accuracy. We propose a joint training method that learns the two models together, so the deployment model can benefit from the extra information only when it actually helps, instead of inheriting its mistakes. We provide guarantees that describe when joint training improves prediction accuracy and analyze a simple alternating training algorithm for large, high-dimensional models. Experiments on synthetic data and real-world prediction tasks show that our approach avoids these failures and robustly outperforms standard Two-Stage baselines.

</details>


### [180] [Concomitant DAG Learning: On the Roles of Noise Adaptivity, Sparsity, and Non-negativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23537)
*Gonzalo Mateos,Samuel Rey,Hamed Ajorlou,Mariano Tepper*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本教程综述了利用连续优化方法克服传统DAG学习中组合约束与可辨识性挑战的最新进展，强调了噪声自适应技术在处理复杂系统因果推断中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的DAG结构识别面临两大挑战：一是强制无环约束带来的组合优化复杂性，限制了在大规模数据上的可扩展性；二是潜在混杂因素和异方差噪声带来的可辨识性难题。

Method: 总结并分类了DAG结构学习的方法论：从早期的组合搜索和贪心启发式算法，过渡到利用无环性平滑特征的现代连续优化框架；同时探讨了联合推断稀疏因果结构与外源噪声水平的稳健性估计方法。

Result: 提供了一套关于因果图恢复的全面教程，涵盖了从结构方程模型基础到前沿的噪声自适应估计技术（应对异方差和分布偏移），并展望了在线、非线性及神经因果发现的前景。

Conclusion: 本文通过阐述将DAG结构学习重构为连续、基于分数的优化问题的新兴方法，为信号处理领域在因果推断、高维统计和可扩展图学习的交叉领域提供了研究视角和未来方向。

Abstract: Directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) constitute a central modeling tool to enable principled reasoning about cause-effect interactions in complex systems. However, since the causal structure underlying a group of variables is often unknown and interventions may be infeasible or ethically challenging to implement, there is a need to address the task of inferring DAGs from observational data. However, most classical structure identification approaches face two key obstacles: the combinatorial challenge of enforcing acyclicity, which severely limits scalability, and identifiability challenges arising from latent confounding or heterogeneous noise. This tutorial offers an overview of recent signal processing and optimization advances that address these issues by recasting DAG structure learning as a continuous, score-based estimation problem over adjacency matrices. We begin with a didactic introduction to structural equation models and the formulation of causal graph recovery, followed by a historical survey of score-based methods ranging from early combinatorial search schemes and greedy heuristics to modern continuous frameworks that leverage smooth characterizations of acyclicity. Building on this foundation, we describe concomitant DAG estimation methods that jointly infer sparse causal structure and exogenous noise levels, improving robustness under heteroscedasticity and distribution shifts by rendering the estimator noise adaptive. All in all, the tutorial introduces readers to challenges and opportunities for signal processing research at the crossroads of causal inference, high-dimensional statistics, and scalable graph learning, while outlining emerging directions including online, nonlinear, and neural causal discovery.

</details>


### [181] [Learning Kernel-Based MDPs from Episodic Preferential Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23650)
*Nikola Pavlovic,Sattar Vakili,Qing Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对核MDP模型，提出了基于偏好的强化学习（RLHF）框架，并通过理论证明该方法能实现策略的最优收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类反馈通常以偏好形式（A比B好）而非数值奖励形式出现，旨在解决强化学习中仅基于偏好进行有效学习的理论基础缺失问题。

Method: 基于核MDP模型，利用Bradley-Terry-Luce模型建模偏好反馈，开发了针对回合终点比较的偏好值估计与置信集构建方法。

Result: 证明了所提出的算法具有高概率的次线性遗憾界，且学到的策略值收敛于最优策略。

Conclusion: 本文通过理论证明了在核MDP环境下，仅通过成对偏好反馈可以实现最优策略学习，且算法具备次线性的遗憾界，为RLHF提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Human feedback often arrives as preferences rather than calibrated numeric rewards, motivating reinforcement learning from preferential feedback, also referred to as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). We present a rigorous theoretical study of preference-only learning in episodic kernel MDPs. In each episode, the learner deploys two policies from a common start state and receives a single binary label indicating which trajectory is preferred, modeled by a Bradley--Terry--Luce link on the difference of cumulative (unobserved) rewards. Under kernel-based assumptions on the reward and transition functions (one of the most general models amenable to theoretical analysis) we develop preference-based value estimation and confidence sets tailored to end-of-episode comparisons.We prove high-probability regret bounds that scale sublinearly in the number of episodes, implying that the value of the learned policy converges to that of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [182] [On the Stability of Spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich Flows and Their Implications for Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23879)
*Aratrika Mustafi,Soumya Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文构建了球形Hellinger-Kantorovich (SHK) 梯度流的扰动分析框架，定量描述了势函数扰动对采样过程的影响，并将其创新性地应用于差分隐私采样，提供了明确的隐私与效用保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在弥补现有SHK梯度流在处理势函数扰动与误差传播方面理论分析的不足，并探索其在差分隐私采样中的实际应用潜力。

Method: 基于球形Hellinger-Kantorovich (SHK) 几何开发了梯度流的扰动理论；利用对数似然比、Rényi 散度和 KL 散度进行误差分析；将该理论应用于差分隐私中指数机制的近似采样。

Result: 推导出了维度无关的对数似然比和Rényi散度的点对点控制边界；建立了指数机制下SHK采样器的Pure-DP和Approximate-DP理论保证，并成功分离了算法的本征次优性与有限时间采样误差。

Conclusion: 基于SHK几何的梯度流具有良好的扰动稳定性，并为差分隐私中的指数机制采样提供了统一的理论框架，能够定量评估采样误差对隐私保护和效用的影响。

Abstract: Gradient-flow sampling interprets a Gibbs distribution as the minimizer of an energy functional over probability measures and generates dynamics converging to this target. Under spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich (SHK) geometry, the flow couples transport and reaction and coincides with birth-death Langevin dynamics. In this work, we develop a perturbation theory for SHK gradient flows. For two potentials $V$ and $V^{\prime}$, we compare the associated flows from a common initialization and quantify how potential discrepancies propagate over time. A uniform perturbation bound yields dimension-free, pointwise control of the log-likelihood ratio and Rényi divergence, while additional structure allows us to derive bounds for the KL divergence as well. We apply these results to approximate sampling for the exponential mechanism in differential privacy. The likelihood-ratio control provides explicit time-dependent Pure-DP guarantees for SHK-based samplers, while the KL bound yields Approximate-DP certificates via hockey-stick divergence. We also derive a utility bound separating intrinsic exponential-mechanism suboptimality from finite-time sampling error.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Fairness in Aggregation: Optimal Top-$k$ and Improved Full Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23265)
*Diptarka Chakraborty,Arya Mazumdar,Barna Saha,Alvin Hong Yao Yan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了受公平性约束的排序聚合问题，提出了公平 top-k 排序的最优算法，并将全排序公平聚合的近似比改进至 2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的排序聚合方法往往会固化或放大对少数群体的代表性不足，需在算法排序中引入公平性约束，以确保少数群体在 top-k 结果及整体排序中的体现。

Method: 使用了基于 Spearman footrule（L1 距离）的数学框架，利用组合优化算法，针对 top-k 排序设计了最优解算法，并针对全排序变体设计了一种新的 2-近似算法。

Result: 提出了公平 top-k 排序的最优算法，并实现了更优的 2-近似算法用于全排序聚合；真实数据集实验验证了该方法相比现有基准具有更强的性能。

Conclusion: 本文在公平排序问题上取得了显著的理论与实践进展：针对公平 top-k 排序问题提出了首个最优算法，并将完整公平排序的近似比从 3 提升至 2，有效缩短了公平排序与非公平排序算法之间的计算差距。

Abstract: Ensuring fairness in algorithmic ranking systems is a critical challenge with significant societal implications for hiring, recommendations, web search, and data management. Standard methods for aggregating multiple preference orders into a consensus ranking may perpetuate and even amplify the lack of representation of underrepresented groups. To address this, recent research has focused on incorporating fairness constraints to ensure the presence of different groups in the top-$k$ positions of the final aggregate ranking.
  We study two fairness-aware variants under the well-known Spearman footrule, which corresponds to the $L_1$ distance between rankings. First, we address the practically salient task of computing a fair aggregate top-$k$ ranking -- crucial in settings like recommendations and hiring where selection is primarily based on the top-$k$ results -- and present the first optimal algorithm for this problem. Second, we consider fair (full) rank aggregation over all candidates (not specifically on top-$k$). We already know of a $3$-approximation for this fair rank aggregation variant (Wei et al., SIGMOD'22; Chakraborty et al., NeurIPS'22), whereas an exact algorithm exists for the corresponding unconstrained (unfair) version (Dwork et al., WWW'01). Closing the computational gap between fair and unconstrained rank aggregation has remained a tantalizing open problem. We make significant progress by giving a $2$-approximation algorithm for fair (full) rank aggregation, improving substantially over the previous $3$-approximation. Further, we complement our theoretical contributions with experiments on different real-world datasets, which corroborate our theoretical results and demonstrate strong empirical performance relative to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [184] [Reducing the Randomness in Partition Oracles for Bounded Degree Minor-Free Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23509)
*Akash Kumar,Abhiruk Lahiri,C. Seshadhri*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了分区预言机中随机种子消耗过大的问题，证明了可以在 $poly(d\varepsilon^{-1}) \cdot \log n$ 的随机种子下实现高效的图划分，优化了该领域的空间/时间效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分区预言机虽然实现了查询复杂度与图规模 $n$ 无关，但仍然依赖 $\Omega(n)$ 的大规模随机种子，这导致了实际应用中的线性预处理瓶颈。作者旨在探究随机种子长度是否也能缩减至独立于 $n$（或接近独立）的程度。

Method: 通过对 Kumar-Seshadhri-Stolman 的分区预言机进行优化改进，实现了基于小规模随机种子的分布式协调；并利用信息论方法证明了在标签空间 $[N]$ 下，即使是对于环图（cycles），分区预言机也需要 $\omega_N(1)$ 的随机位。

Result: 证明了对于有界度超有限图族，存在使用 $poly(d\varepsilon^{-1}) \cdot \log n$ 长度随机种子的分区预言机；同时证明了在顶点标签空间为 $[N]$ 的一般设置下，分区预言机存在随机位需求的下界。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将分区预言机（Partition Oracles）的随机种子长度从 $\Omega(n)$ 降低到了 $poly(d\varepsilon^{-1}) \cdot \log n$，并证明了对于更一般化标签模型的下界，开创了对分区预言机随机性复杂度研究的先河。

Abstract: Consider a bounded-degree graph $G$ that belongs to a minor-closed family (such as planar graphs). Such a graph has a hyperfinite decomposition, wherein, for a sufficiently small $\varepsilon > 0$, one can remove $\varepsilon dn$ edges to obtain connected components of size independent of $n$. (As usual, $n$ is the number of vertices and $d$ is the degree bound.) In a seminal result, Hassidim-Kelner-Nguyen-Onak (FOCS 2009) introduced the partition oracle, a procedure that provides local access to a hyperfinite decomposition. The partition oracle computes the component containing an input vertex $v$ with query complexity (to $G$) independent of $n$. Remarkably, this is done without any preprocessing on $G$. The coordination is done purely through a shared random seed.
  Despite a line of work on optimizing the query complexity of partition oracles, there were no attempts to bound the size of the random seed. All existing partition oracles use a random seed of size $Ω(n)$, which technically implies a linear setup time. Any blackbox derandomization would likely need $Ω(\log^2n)$ uniform random bits. A natural question is whether the random seed can also have length independent of $n$.
  We prove the $poly(d\varepsilon^{-1})$-query partition oracles of Kumar-Seshadhri-Stolman can be implemented with a random seed of $poly(d\varepsilon^{-1}) \cdot \log n$ length. To get a deeper understanding on the randomness complexity, we consider a more general model where the vertex labels come from the universe $[N]$, where $N \geq n$. In this setting, we prove that any partition oracle even for cycles requires $ω_N(1)$ random bits.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [185] [Energy per Successful Goal: Goal-Level Energy Accounting for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22883)
*Deepak Panigrahy,Aakash Tyagi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对智能体复杂的运行机制，本文提出了A-LEMS系统，通过从“单次推理能耗”转向“成功任务目标能耗（EpG）”及“编排开销指数（OOI）”来精准评估智能体系统的真实能耗代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI能耗基准仅衡量单次推理或训练，无法准确反映智能体（Agent）系统中多步编排、工具调用及重试机制带来的复杂能耗成本，导致任务真实耗能被掩盖。

Method: 提出了A-LEMS（智能体大模型能耗测量系统），包含基于时间边界模型的能源归因框架、五层观测管道（将RAPL信号映射到工作流）以及可复现性协议；引入了EpG（Energy per Successful Goal）和OOI（Orchestration Overhead Index）评估指标。

Result: 实验显示，智能体工作流在推理任务中的平均EpG比线性基线高出4.33倍；但在工具调用任务中，智能体的OOI可降至1.0以下，证明该指标能客观反映编排结构的能效特征而非固定的性能惩罚。

Conclusion: 现有基于单次推理的AI能耗基准不适用于智能体系统；提出了EpG（任务目标能耗）和OOI（编排开销指数），证明了智能体工作的能耗核心在于编排结构而非单纯的推理计算，为智能体AI建立了新的能效评估标准。

Abstract: Current AI energy benchmarks measure consumption at the granularity of a single model invocation or training run. For classical single-turn workloads this unit remains coherent. For agentic systems - where a single user goal may trigger multi-step orchestration, tool calls, retries, and failure-recovery cycles - the invocation count is an implementation artifact rather than a task property, and inference-level normalization misrepresents the energy cost of goal completion. We present A-LEMS (Agentic LLM Energy Measurement System), a cross-layer measurement framework that redefines the unit of AI energy accounting from energy per inference to Energy per Successful Goal (EpG). EpG aggregates total workflow energy across all execution attempts, including failures and retries, normalized by successfully completed goals. A-LEMS formalizes energy attribution through a temporal boundary model, a five-layer observation pipeline mapping RAPL signals to workflow-level energy, and a reproducibility protocol binding every measurement to hardware and runtime configuration. Building on EpG, we define the Orchestration Overhead Index (OOI), isolating the energy cost of orchestration relative to linear execution under identical task criteria.
  Across five reasoning and three tool-augmented task families, agentic workflows consume 4.33x higher mean energy per successful goal than linear baselines (888.1 J vs 205.3 J). This overhead is driven by orchestration structure, not inference compute. For tool-augmented tasks, OOI inverts below 1.0x: agentic execution is cheaper than linear, confirming the metric captures orchestration structure rather than a fixed upward bias.
  These findings establish that energy-per-inference is insufficient for agentic AI. EpG and OOI provide the measurement foundation for accurate benchmarking, where orchestration structure is the primary determinant of energy cost.

</details>


### [186] [RMA: an Agentic System for Research-Level Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22875)
*Zelin Zhao,Bo Yuan,Jaemoo Choi,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RMA 是一个专门用于处理研究级数学问题的多智能体框架，它通过模块化分工、文献检索和多轮迭代推理，成功解决了复杂的开放性数学问题，在基准测试中显著优于现有主流大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型在数学领域的应用多局限于竞赛数学或形式化证明，缺乏对研究级数学问题所需的长期推理、文献检索能力及迭代精修能力的支持。因此，作者旨在构建一个专门针对研究难度数学问题的自动化推理框架。

Method: 提出了一种基于多智能体（初始化器、提案者、验证者）的推理框架 RMA。该框架将任务分解为问题分析、文献检索与理解、公平比较、知识库构建及证明验证等专项模块，并通过共享的结构化内存进行协同，执行多轮次、迭代式的证明生成与精修。

Result: 在 First Proof 基准测试（包含 10 个跨领域的顶级研究课题）上，RMA 成功解决了 8 个问题，表现优于强基准模型（包括 GPT-5.2R 和 Aletheia），且所生成的证明在逻辑严密性与可读性方面表现更佳。

Conclusion: RMA 提供了一个有效的多智能体分布式框架，通过模块化协作和迭代反馈，显著提升了处理研究级数学问题的能力。研究表明，结构化推理模块、迭代式精修与验证反馈的协同作用是其性能超越现有模型（如 GPT-5.2R 和 Aletheia）的关键。

Abstract: We present $\textbf{Research Math Agents (RMA)}$, an agentic framework for automated reasoning on research-level mathematical problems. Unlike prior studies centered on competition mathematics or formal theorem proving, RMA targets research-level mathematical problems that require long-horizon reasoning, literature grounding, and iterative proof refinement. RMA decomposes research-level proof solving into specialized modules for problem analysis, literature search and understanding, fair comparison, knowledge-bank construction, and proof verification, all coordinated by initializer, proposer, and verifier agents through a shared structured memory. Within this unified framework, these agents operate in a multi-role, multi-round workflow, collaboratively generating, refining, and verifying candidate proofs through iterative feedback. We evaluate RMA on the First Proof benchmark, which consists of ten research-level problems contributed by expert mathematicians across diverse domains. Through comprehensive expert evaluation, RMA outperforms strong baselines on the First Proof benchmark, including GPT-5.2R and Aletheia, solving eight out of ten research problems and producing more logically sound and readable proofs. Our comprehensive ablation studies further show that performance gains arise from the interaction of structured reasoning modules, iterative refinement, and verifier-based feedback, rather than any single component. Our solutions and implementations will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [187] [ImProver 2: Iteratively Self-Improving LMs for Neurosymbolic Proof Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22885)
*Riyaz Ahuja,Tate Rowney,Jeremy Avigad,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ImProver 2 神经符号框架，通过结合专家迭代与形式化结构脚手架，使得参数量较小的模型也能在证明优化任务中达到与大模型相当的性能，有效解决了证明重构中的效率与成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库的快速扩展导致对证明重构及高质量训练数据的需求增加，但现有的证明优化面临目标异构、数据稀缺以及训练与推理成本高等挑战。

Method: 提出 ImProver 2 神经符号框架；采用数据高效的专家迭代流水线（expert-iteration pipeline）；构建能够结合形式化结构与轻量级非形式化抽象的“脚手架”（scaffold）；引入评估结构证明属性的指标体系。

Result: 训练出一个 7B 参数的模型，其性能超越了同系列中量级大得多的模型，并与中等规模的前沿模型相当；证明了神经符号框架能显著提升小型和大型模型的性能。

Conclusion: 通过合适的框架支撑与训练策略，小型模型能够有效重构复杂的研究级证明，证明了证明优化是一项可扩展且可学习的任务。

Abstract: Formal mathematics libraries are rapidly expanding, creating a growing need to refactor verified proofs for maintainability and to improve training data quality for neural provers. However, scalable proof optimization is hindered by heterogeneous and heuristically specified objectives, scarce data, and high training and inference costs. To overcome these challenges, we introduce ImProver 2, a neurosymbolic framework for automated proof optimization in Lean 4. ImProver 2 combines a data-efficient expert-iteration pipeline with a scaffold that exposes formal structure alongside lightweight informal abstractions. We further introduce a suite of metrics capturing structural proof properties. Using ImProver 2, we train a 7B-parameter model that outperforms orders-of-magnitude larger models within the same model family, and is competitive with mid-tier frontier models across metrics. We additionally demonstrate that our neurosymbolic scaffold significantly improves performance across both small and frontier models. We show that with proper scaffolding and training, small models can effectively restructure research-level proofs over complex and varied metrics, matching substantially larger systems and establishing proof optimization as a scalable, learnable task.

</details>


### [188] [Mediative Fuzzy Logic: From Type-1 Foundations to Type-2, Type-3 and Quantum Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22900)
*Oscar Montiel Ross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了中介模糊逻辑的统一形式化框架，通过引入双格结构和多元扩展，解决了复杂环境中犹豫与冲突信息的决策难题，并验证了其在安全攸关系统中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的中介模糊逻辑缺乏完善的逻辑与语义基础，特别是在处理超出一型（Type-1）之外的复杂模糊设置时存在理论缺口。

Method: 提出了一种基于双格（bilattice）结构的真值建模方法，引入了受犹豫度与矛盾度控制的凸聚合算子；构建了基于标准 t-模的命题系统；并将该框架扩展至区间二型（IT2）、粒度三型（GT3）及量子密度算子等多个层级。

Result: 建立了中介模糊逻辑的健全性、次协调性（paraconsistency）及保守性；展示了该框架在自动驾驶传感器融合场景中处理异构、冲突证据的有效性；实现了多层级形式化表达的逻辑相干性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建中介模糊逻辑（Mediative Fuzzy Logic）的统一理论框架，证明了其在处理矛盾、犹豫及不确定证据时的逻辑严谨性与安全性，为智能决策系统的跨层级应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Mediative Fuzzy Logic was conceived as a practical scheme for reconciling hesitant or conflicting assessments in fuzzy control and decision-making. However, its logical and semantic foundations remain underdeveloped, especially beyond operational type-1 settings. This article develops a unified account of the type-1 core together with interval type-2, granular type-3, and quantum extensions. We characterize the mediative operator as a convex aggregation controlled by hesitation and contradiction, model mediative truth values as independent truth-falsity pairs in a continuous bilattice-like structure, and introduce a propositional system extending a standard t-norm-based fuzzy logic with a mediative connective. We establish soundness, paraconsistency, and conservativity over the underlying fuzzy base for formulas without mediation, and formulate coherent semantic extensions to interval type-2 truth values, granule-indexed local evaluations, and effects and density operators on Hilbert spaces. An autonomous-braking sensor-fusion example illustrates how the framework supports transparent, conservative, and safety-first decisions under incomplete, heterogeneous, and mildly contradictory evidence. Under suitable assumptions, the higher-level formulations reduce to the type-1 case, clarifying coherence across levels and reliably supporting future work in intelligent decision systems.

</details>


### [189] [EVE-Agent: Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22905)
*Yamato Arai,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自演进搜索智能体易产生不可控内容的弊端，本文提出 EVE-Agent，通过引入基于边际准确率增益的证据验证机制，实现了无需人工参与的自监督、可信纠错与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动型自演进智能体缺乏验证机制，易产生“流利但缺乏依据/不可靠”的内容，导致模型产生幻觉且无法溯源。因此，研究者提出将“证据可验证性”作为自演进过程的先决条件。

Method: 提出 EVE-Agent 框架，改进了 Proposer-Solver 架构：Proposer 同步生成问题、答案及证据段落；引入“证据验证器（Evidence Verifier）”，通过衡量引入证据后模型答案的边际准确率增益（Marginal Accuracy Gain）来计算奖励，从而筛选高质量、具备溯源性的训练样本。

Result: 实验表明，EVE-Agent 在保证模型后端、检索器和优化器不变的情况下，显著提升了模型生成的证据支撑正确性。生成的训练集具备可解释性和可审计性。

Conclusion: EVE-Agent 通过引入证据验证机制，成功构建了一个无需人类标注、自演进且具备可审计性的搜索智能体框架，证明了“证据可验证性”是实现可信自演进的关键。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents should not train on examples they cannot justify. Data-free self-evolving search agents offer a scalable route to systems that generate their own questions, answer them, and improve from their own feedback without human annotations. Yet, without verifiable evidence, this loop can reward fluent but unsupported examples, turning the self-generated curriculum into an opaque and potentially unreliable training signal. We argue that evidence verifiability is a prerequisite for trustworthy self-evolution in search agents: each generated instance should include not only an answer but also a source-grounded span whose contribution to that answer can be measured. We introduce EVE-Agent, an Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agent that operationalizes this principle through a modification to the proposer--solver framework. The proposer generates a question, an answer, and a verbatim evidence span. An evidence verifier then rewards the span according to the marginal accuracy gain when the evidence is provided. This produces a training signal that favors evidence that genuinely helps answer the question, without requiring oracle answers, human labels, or external annotations. EVE-Agent leaves the backbone model, retriever, search tool, and optimization framework unchanged. Experiments show that EVE-Agent substantially improves evidence-grounded correctness over prior self-evolving search agents. The resulting curriculum is not merely self-generated but auditable by construction: each training example carries an inspectable source span that explains why it should be trusted.

</details>


### [190] [The Deterministic Horizon: Impossibility Results as Design Specifications for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23024)
*Dongxin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将大模型面临的不可避免的数学和理论限制（“不可能性结果”）转化为了一套系统的设计规则，揭示了模型推理深度存在架构决定的性能天花板，并为构建可信AI提供了一套可计算的评估与构建框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大模型能力强大，但受限于计算本质的根本性限制。作者旨在将AI研究中常见的“不可能性结果”从单纯的理论局限转化为指导模型架构设计和部署的可操作性规则。

Method: 1. 提供了一个理论框架，通过计算复杂性、信息论和电路复杂性理论，推导大语言模型体系结构和算法的根本边界；2. 针对特定任务（逻辑推理、偏好学习、检索、机制设计等）构建了16条不可能性规格（specifications）；3. 进行实证测量（如测量“确定性地平线”和验证零知识证明的计算开销）。

Result: 1. 提出“确定性地平线”（Deterministic Horizon）：发现LLM存在推理深度的准确性上限（19-31层），且该上限与训练规模无关；2. 证明了在模型架构、偏好学习、检索系统、拍卖机制和零知识证明等领域的算术/复杂度下界；3. 建立了包含16项规格的不可能性目录，量化了违背这些限制的成本。

Conclusion: 利用不可能性结果（如Turing、Arrow、No Free Lunch等）来定义AI的“设计规则”。论文主张将这些根本限制视为构建可信AI的框架，通过识别 computable 边界和量化违反成本，为生成式AI的研究提供系统性的指导方针。

Abstract: Large language models now write software, draft legal documents, and produce clinical notes, yet fundamental limits, from Turing and Arrow to the No Free Lunch theorems, shape what computation can do. This thesis turns such impossibility results from curiosities into design rules. Its flagship result proves an accuracy ceiling set by architecture alone: past a critical reasoning depth, no amount of training moves it, at any adapter rank, sample size, or loss function. Computable before deployment from layer count and embedding width, this Deterministic Horizon is measured between nineteen and thirty-one across twelve transformer architectures, and fine-tuning on optimal-length traces recovers under four percentage points. The mechanism is a capacity invariant of the residual stream, and an information-theoretic conversion yields super-exponential accuracy decay past the horizon. An unconditional circuit-complexity lower bound for modular exponentiation against constant-depth prime-modulus circuits complements this result.
