The same argument recasts across subfields: preference learning under any misspecified model jumps discontinuously in sample complexity; multi-stage retrieval pipelines require at least as many independent metrics as stages; standard truthful auctions fail for agents with prompt-dependent valuations; and zero-knowledge verification of neural inference pays a measured overhead of one hundred ten to one hundred ninety times per non-linear activation. Together these form a catalogue of sixteen specifications, each pairing a computable boundary, a quantified violation cost, and a constructive design rule: two compositions are proved, one pairing is an honest obstruction, and four remain open. The impossibility-specification methodology is offered for the generative research programme that trustworthy AI may need. Every fundamental limit of AI is also a design rule.

</details>


### [191] [AutoResearch AI: Towards AI-Powered Research Automation for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23204)
*Guiyao Tie,Jiawen Shi,Dingjie Song,Yixiao Huang,Ziji Sheng,Xueyang Zhou,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Yongchao Chen,Ran Xu,Lifang He,Qingsong Wen,Manling Li,Cong Lu,Shuai Li,Pengtao Xie,Yixuan Yuan,Rui Meng,Lei Xing,Lichao Sun,Caiming Xiong,Philip S. Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地综述了AI科研自动化（AutoResearch）的现状，提出了评价模型与分析框架，分析了从辅助式AI到自主性科研系统的演进，并指出了其在不同科研场景下的适用性边界与核心难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对科学研究从“AI辅助任务式”向“全流程自动化工作流”的范式转移，针对当前研究系统存在的碎片化、缺乏可复现性、证据追踪不足及责任归属模糊等问题进行系统性总结与分析。

Method: 提出了“AutoResearch”概念框架，将AI科学研究自动化划分为五个流程阶段（文献调研、假设规划、实验工具使用、反馈验证、报告沟通），并构建了五个评价维度（新颖性、有效性、影响力、可靠性、溯源性）进行综合评估。

Result: 通过对现有的AI科学家系统、人机协作框架及基准测试的综合梳理，界定并对比了“Vibe Research”（人控辅助）与“AI主导研究系统”的不同空间，明确了当前科研自动化的技术边界及挑战。

Conclusion: AutoResearch的自主性存在领域局限性：在结构化、可执行且验证快速的场景中表现可靠，但在涉及物理实操、反馈滞后、多源异构、伦理约束或需机构问责的复杂环境中仍面临局限。

Abstract: Scientific research is being reshaped by AI systems that move beyond isolated assistance toward longer-horizon workflows spanning literature grounding, hypothesis generation, experimentation, validation, reporting, and revision. This shift marks a transition from task-level AI for science to workflow-level research automation. Yet current systems remain fragmented, differing in autonomy, domain scope, execution environment, validation mechanism, and human oversight, while still struggling with evidence preservation, reproducibility, weak-direction rejection, provenance tracking, cross-domain robustness, and accountable scientific closure. This survey examines these developments through AutoResearch, defined as the developmental spectrum of AI-powered scientific workflow automation. Within it, Vibe Research denotes the human-steered region of prompt-based assistance and human-verified execution, whereas emerging AI-led systems coordinate larger portions of the discovery loop without achieving robust autonomy. We analyze how research systems redistribute control, evidence, execution, validation, and accountability across workflows and organize the field around five workflow conditions: literature and research grounding; hypothesis formation and planning; experimentation and tool use; feedback, validation, and review; and reporting and knowledge communication. We further synthesize AI scientist systems, mixed-initiative co-research frameworks, benchmarks, domain deployments, and open-source infrastructures. Finally, we propose five evaluation dimensions--novelty, validity, impact, reliability, and provenance--and show that AutoResearch autonomy is domain-conditioned, being more credible in structured, executable, and rapidly verifiable settings but limited in embodied, delayed, heterogeneous, ethical, or institutionally accountable contexts.

</details>


### [192] [Foundation Protocol: A Coordination Layer for Agentic Society](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23218)
*Bang Liu,Yongfeng Gu,Jiayi Zhang,Zhaoyang Yu,Sirui Hong,Maojia Song,Xiaoqiang Wang,Mingyi Deng,Zijie Zhuang,Ronghao Wang,Mingzhe Cao,Yutong Zhu,Xingjian Li,Yifan Wu,Jianhao Ruan,Yiran Peng,Shuangrui Chen,Jinlin Wang,Yizhang Lin,Dongjie Zhang,Dekun Wu,Chen Ma,Lizi Liao,Han Yu,Jian Pei,Heng Ji,Qiang Yang,Yuyu Luo,Chenglin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 Foundation Protocol (FP)，这是一个以图为核心的社会基础设施层，专门用于协调异构的人类与AI智能体系统，通过集成经济原语和治理机制，在保障智能体自主性和可组合性的同时，实现安全、可追溯和可审计的协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体在社会基础设施中的作用日益凸显，系统的瓶颈已从单纯的模型能力转向智能体间的协调、多智能体协作、经济价值交换，以及在现实世界中实现安全管理与责任归属的需求。

Method: 提出 Foundation Protocol (FP)：一种以图（graph-first）为核心的协调协议层；设计了支持异构实体（人、AI、工具、组织）统一管理的架构；引入了用于计量、接收和结算的经济原语；将策略、溯源和审计作为系统核心设计要素；采用封装现有协议而非替代的策略。

Result: 设计并构建了一个能够桥接现有协议、支持多方协作与经济活动的协调层，实现了智能体间的高效互动、透明的审计与治理，降低了系统间的集成成本。

Conclusion: Foundation Protocol (FP) 旨在通过作为共享协调层，在保持自主智能体可组合性的同时，确保 accountability（责任制）不可妥协，从而构建一个开放、多元且可治理的人机社会。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are moving from tools into a layer of social infrastructure: they browse, purchase, deploy software, manage systems, and increasingly interact with one another. As these systems scale, the bottleneck shifts away from raw model capability toward coordination. Agents need to form reliable relationships, organize multi-agent work, exchange value, support an AI economy, and stay safe and accountable under real-world oversight. This paper introduces the Foundation Protocol (FP), a graph-first coordination layer for an emerging human-AI society. FP unifies heterogeneous entities, including agents, tools, resources, humans, institutions, and organizations, and supports native multi-party organization and event-based collaboration. It also provides economic primitives for metering, receipts, and settlement, and treats policy, provenance, and audit as first-class concerns. FP is designed to wrap and bridge existing protocols rather than replace them, enabling incremental adoption while reducing integration and governance overhead. The aim is to keep autonomous agency composable while keeping accountability non-negotiable, so that coordination itself can become shared infrastructure for a human-AI society that is open, pluralistic, and governable.

</details>


### [193] [GENSTRAT: Toward a Science of Strategic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23238)
*Vartan Shadarevian,Kia Ghods,Alex Kenich,Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型战略推理评估存在局限和污染的问题，本论文提出了 GENSTRAT 基准测试，通过程序化生成的动态游戏、多维度能力剖析及模型表现波动量化，实现了对 LLM 战略竞争力和部署可靠性的深度诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有战略推理基准测试多基于固定游戏，存在易饱和、易污染的问题，且无法准确预测 LLM 在复杂、混乱的真实经济环境（如市场、拍卖）中的部署表现。

Method: 1. 构建程序化生成战略环境（GENSTRAT）：生成零和不完美信息扑克游戏，实现免污染、可扩展的评估。2. 能力剖面建模：从状态空间、时间深度、信息敏感性、对手建模、风险、脆弱性六个维度评估。3. 参差度量化（Jaggedness Measure）：度量模型在相似游戏间的表现波动。4. 实验设计：对 9 个 frontier/open-weight 模型进行超过 36,000 场次的对抗赛。

Result: 1. 新型前沿模型在平均表现上占优。2. 总分相近的模型在能力剖面上表现出显著的定性差异。3. 顶级模型在“参差度”上存在显著差异，例如主流模型中存在表现不稳定（波动较大）的现象。

Conclusion: GENSTRAT 提供了一种比单一基准测试更深刻的评估方法。研究表明，仅看平均胜率不足以全面评估 LLM 的战略能力，通过能力剖面（Capability Profiles）和“参差度”（Jaggedness）指标，能够更精准地诊断模型在复杂动态战略环境中的稳定性与差异性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as economic agents in marketplaces, auctions, and bidding settings. Anticipating their behavior in any specific deployment is hard. Existing strategic-reasoning benchmarks evaluate models on fixed canonical games. These benchmarks may saturate as the frontier improves, and they do not allow evaluators to generalize with confidence from benchmark performance to the varied and messy strategic environments that actual deployments involve. We introduce GENSTRAT, which uses procedurally generated strategic environments to address these challenges. Concretely, we generate a distribution of two-player zero-sum imperfect-information card games. The generator can draw fresh games on demand, allowing for evergreen evaluation and resistance to contamination. We pair the game distribution with a capability-profile methodology that decomposes model competence across six axes (state space, temporal depth, information sensitivity, opponent modeling, risk, and brittleness). We also introduce a jaggedness measure of within-distribution smoothness that detects when a model's advantage jumps unpredictably between strategically similar games. We sample 50 benchmark games from a 2,000-game generated pool and evaluate nine frontier and open-weight LLMs in a head-to-head tournament with over 36,000 matches. Newer frontier-tier models score higher on average. Beyond that average, models with near-identical overall strength show qualitatively different capability profiles, and two of the top three leaderboard models (gpt-5 and claude) are noticeably more locally volatile than the third (gemini-3.1-pro), despite being close in overall strength. Together, the capability profile and the jaggedness measure give a deployment-relevant diagnostic that the overall ranking alone cannot provide.

</details>


### [194] [Design and Report Benchmarks for Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23262)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批评了现有知识工作AI评测脱离实际的现状，提出了一套基于O*NET任务分类、关注真实工作产出与环境的三步评估框架，以确保AI评测分数能更准确地反映真实工作表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的知识工作AI基准测试仍沿用传统NLP的评测逻辑，导致基准分数高并不能代表AI系统在真实工作环境中的胜任力。亟需一套能够将任务表现与真实工作产出建立明确联系的评测范式。

Method: 1. 提出三步评估框架：定义评估的工作活动、明确测试场景（包括角色、工具、约束条件）、评估最终工作产物。 2. 引入 O*NET 职业任务数据库，归纳出 18 项具体工作活动。 3. 通过三个案例（GDPval、OfficeQA Pro、APEX-SWE）进行实证分析与设计反思。

Result: 研究揭示了基准设计选择如何影响其最终的“工作主张”（work claim）效力。通过分析发现，当前基准测试在评估任务时，往往在测试环境、产物得分与实际工作表现之间存在严重的脱节与缺口。

Conclusion: 该研究认为目前的基准测试设计脱离了实际知识工作的范畴。通过提出一套三步评估框架，研究者明确了基准评分与实际工作成效之间的逻辑联系，强调了基准设计必须考虑到实际工作中的角色、工具、物料及产物可用性，从而缩小当前AI评测与现实部署之间的差距。

Abstract: The development of LLM agents has led to a growing body of work on knowledge-work AI, including coding, research, and healthcare. However, current knowledge-work evaluation and benchmark design still largely follow the logic of traditional NLP tasks. As a result, higher benchmark performance does not reliably show that a system can carry out knowledge work in real-world deployment settings. This paper contributes a three-step approach for making explicit how benchmarked tasks represent the work claims attached to their scores: defining the work activity under evaluation, specifying the tested setting, and scoring the appropriate work product. We review work studies showing that knowledge work is organized through roles and responsibilities, local materials and tools, and artifacts that must remain usable in downstream workflows. We then translate these concerns into benchmark design and reporting guidance, covering how tasks should be mapped to work activities, how tested settings should specify materials, tools, roles, and constraints, and how scoring should focus on the work product left by the system. To name the work activity being evaluated and distinguish it from common benchmark tasks, we derive an inventory of 18 work activities from the O{*}NET occupational task database. We demonstrate the approach through three benchmark case analyses: GDPval, a non-code occupational deliverable benchmark; OfficeQA Pro, a grounded document-analysis benchmark scored by final answers; and APEX-SWE, a software-engineering benchmark with executable scored products. These cases show how benchmark design choices shape the strongest work claim a score can support, and where gaps arise between the benchmarked task, tested setting, scored product, and broader work claim.

</details>


### [195] [Ontological Knowledge Blocks: Executable Compliance and Profile-Based Validation for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23297)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 OKB 的可编程治理基础设施，通过将法规要求转化为机器可验证的约束（SHACL 和 RDF/OWL），实现了 AI 系统合规性审查的自动化，解决了传统离线、人工审计手段无法规模化应用的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前关键数字基础设施中的 AI 治理仍处于“文档中心化”阶段，依赖手动审查和静态清单，无法应对自动化 AI 系统带来的规模化合规挑战（如透明度、公平性、可追溯性等）。

Method: 提出了 OKB（Ontological Knowledge Blocks）概念，将其形式化为包含规范义务、RDF/OWL 模式、SHACL 验证规则、证据要求及 PROV-O 溯源链接的 5 元组。开发了一个确定性的合规性编译器，通过中间表示（IR）将义务转换为可执行的知识库模块。

Result: 通过两个 AI 辅助高性能计算（HPC）原型进行验证，测试显示 SHACL 验证延迟在 12.6ms 至 100.3ms 之间，实现了基于不同治理配置文件的灵活验证，并证明了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs) 提供了一种可扩展、可编程且可重构的自动化治理框架，能够有效解决当前 AI 系统治理中依赖人工文档审计的局限性，实现合规性检查的自动化与实时化。

Abstract: AI-enabled services deployed in critical digital infrastructure are subject to governance obligations spanning transparency, accountability, fairness, and traceability. Compliance today remains documentation-centric: obligations are described in prose, audits rely on static checklists, and verification depends on manual review. Such approaches do not scale to automated AI systems. This paper introduces Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs), a programmable governance infrastructure that compiles regulatory obligations into machine-checkable constraints over structured evidence graphs. We formalize an OKB as a 5-tuple that binds normative obligations to an RDF/OWL concept schema, executable SHACL validation rules, explicit evidence requirements, and PROV-O provenance links. A deterministic regulatory compiler translates structured Intermediate Representation (IR) records into composable KB modules, enabling profile-based governance reconfiguration without modifying service code. We implement two prototypes and evaluate them in an AI-assisted HPC resource allocation scenario across 24 validation runs and four governance profiles. Results demonstrate profile-sensitive validation, strictly additive violation accumulation, SHACL validation latency between 12.6 ms and 100.3 ms, and profile equivalence testing confirming Combined as the strictly most comprehensive profile. All artefacts are released as open source.

</details>


### [196] [Human-in-the-Loop Multi-Agent Ventilator Decision Support with Contextual Bandit Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23320)
*Sijia Li,Xiaoyu Tan,Qixing Wang,Weiyi Zhao,Chen Zhan,Teqi Hao,Xuemin Wang,Lei Gu,Roland Eils,Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VDSS系统，通过模块化多智能体架构和在线学习机制，实现了呼吸机决策的个性化、可控与可审核，显著提升了临床决策支持的人机协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有呼吸机决策支持系统难以平衡临床安全性、个体化治疗风格及可解释性/可审核性的问题。传统的规则法缺乏灵活性，而端到端强化学习或单一大型语言模型在临床控制和审计方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出了一种人机交互（human-in-the-loop）的多智能体框架，利用合同驱动的结构化接口协调模块化组件；引入基于上下文赌博机（contextual bandit）的在线偏好适应机制；并设计了结构化拒绝反馈机制以优化规划过程，提高交互稳定性。

Result: ICU回溯轨迹重放实验显示，VDSS的推荐方案接受度更高，且达到可接受方案所需的交互轮次更少。

Conclusion: VDSS通过多智能体协作与结构化反馈，成功实现了既满足临床安全约束，又能适应个体医生偏好的呼吸机决策支持，验证了人机协作在ICU临床部署的可行性。

Abstract: Ventilator decision support requires sequential decisions that track evolving physiology and disease trajectories while respecting safety boundaries and clinician specific tuning styles. Rule based approaches rarely generalize personalization, and end to end reinforcement learning or single large language model systems remain difficult to control and audit. We propose the Ventilator Decision Support System (VDSS), a human in the loop multi agent framework that coordinates modular decision components through contract driven structured interfaces and produces traceable evidence for review. VDSS performs online preference adaptation with a contextual bandit, updating clinician specific preferences from the final accepted decision at each adjustment cycle and using them to guide subsequent recommendations. Structured rejection feedback triggers targeted replanning to reduce unproductive iterations and improve interaction stability. Retrospective ICU trajectory replay with expert review indicates higher recommendation acceptability and fewer interaction rounds to reach an acceptable plan, supporting clinically deployable human AI collaboration.

</details>


### [197] [EDGE-OPD: Internalizing Privileged Context with Evidence Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23493)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Dylan Bates,Aman Sharma,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）容易被特权上下文带偏的问题，本文提出了 EDGE-OPD 方法，通过引导采样和证据掩码机制，确保模型精确学习目标行为，同时避免通用能力的退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）虽能利用特权上下文训练模型，但特权信息往往会导致模型产生偏移（如推理退化、风格改变），导致模型学习到副作用而非理想的可迁移行为。

Method: 提出了一种名为 EDGE-OPD 的方法，包含两项关键改进：1. 引导式推演（guided rollouts），确保稀有目标行为在在线数据中出现；2. 证据掩码（evidence mask），仅在特权上下文支持所采样 token 的位置对模型进行梯度更新。

Result: 实验表明，原始 OPSD 及 RLSD 变体完全无法学习到目标特征，而 EDGE-OPD 成功实现了目标特征的学习；掩码消融实验揭示了特权信号在“积极证据尾部”的局限性，有助于在迁移知识的同时保持通用能力。

Conclusion: EDGE-OPD 通过引导式采样和证据掩码策略，解决了在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中特权信息导致模型行为偏移或通用能力退化的难题，实现了高效且精准的知识迁移。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has gained wide attraction as an LLM post-training paradigm due to its effectiveness in improving capabilities without introducing model distribution drift, and consequently, regression in general tasks. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is an efficient use-case of OPD, which is appealing as it requires only a single model as a student and teacher, and it also has the benefit of providing privileged context that is a absent at inference time (e.g. a persona, a private fact, or a worked solution) to the teacher during the training process. The challenge in this approach is that the privileged information can change model behavior more than intended: it can modify reasoning, degrade general capabilities, and affect performance indicators like response length, style, or local token preferences. Consequently, OPSD may train the student on side effects rather than a desired, transferable behavior. In this paper, we study this problem in a rare-token/identity setting and propose EviDence GuidEd On-Policy Distillation (EDGE-OPD), a modification of OPSD with two distinct characteristics: a) it uses guided rollouts to inject privileged-context behavior to the student at sampling time, so that the rare target behavior is actually present in the on-policy data, and b) it applies an evidence mask: the student is updated only at token positions where the privileged context supports the sampled token, rather than on every token in the rollout. We empirically show that OPSD (and its variant RLSD, with and without a verifier) completely fail to learn a target identity, while the integration of guided rollouts allows them to succeed. Additionally, mask-region ablations show that the persona signal is localized to the positive-evidence tail, allows us to draw valuable insights about efficient knowledge transfer and preservation of general purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [198] [CP or DP? Why Not Both: A Case Study in the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23569)
*Emma Legrand,Roger Kameugne,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将动态规划与约束规划相结合的混合优化框架，通过将CP作为DP搜索过程中的子程序，提升了解决部分车间调度问题（PSSP）的灵活性和扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上DP和CP通常被分别使用，本文旨在探索将两者结合的有效性，并解决部分车间调度问题（PSSP）中传统DP在灵活性和约束处理上的局限性。

Method: 提出一种混合算法框架：以DP作为主要的搜索框架，将CP集成作为子程序，利用其全局约束传播技术；并引入了任意时间（anytime）DP策略以及基于该模型的局部搜索（LNS）方案。

Result: 该方法成功应用于PSSP，不仅增强了处理任意优先级约束的能力，还支持了更灵活的搜索策略（如anytime column search）和LNS机制。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了动态规划（DP）与约束规划（CP）混合方法的有效性，为解决组合优化问题提供了一种灵活且可扩展的新框架，尽管在特定问题上暂未超越最先进的纯CP求解器。

Abstract: Dynamic Programming (DP) and Constraint Programming (CP) are well-established paradigms for solving combinatorial optimization problems. Usually, these two approaches are used separately. This paper aims to show that the two can be combined effectively and elegantly, with DP serving as the primary search framework and CP used as a subroutine to leverage global constraint propagation. This paper presents such an approach for the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem (PSSP), for which a pure DP method has previously been proposed, and efficient CP filtering algorithms are available. The PSSP is a general scheduling problem where each job consists of a set of operations with arbitrary precedence constraints. The approach is flexible enough to accommodate anytime DP strategies, such as anytime column search, whereas the original DP algorithm operated in a strictly layer-wise manner. Moreover, the flexibility of the CP modeling makes it straightforward to incorporate arbitrary precedence constraints. As a result, the model naturally handles any precedence graph and even enables the design of a Large Neighborhood Search (LNS) scheme, in which the DP model is reused, and partial-order schedules are imposed across restarts to improve the incumbent solution. While not competitive with state-of-the-art pure CP solvers for this specific problem, our primary contribution is demonstrating the viability of this hybrid integration.

</details>


### [199] [Co-ReAct: Rubrics as Step-Level Collaborators for ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23590)
*Jiazheng Kang,Bowen Zhang,Zixin Song,Jiangwang Chen,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co-ReAct 引入了一种准则引导的智能体决策框架，通过在推理的每一步注入经 GRPO 优化的判别式准则，有效解决了 ReAct 智能体在复杂推理任务中的盲目性，提升了任务执行质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 ReAct 智能体在执行搜索密集型、多步推理任务时，过度依赖自身判断，常导致轨迹浅显、冗余或目标不明确；现有准则应用多局限于评估或反馈，缺乏针对单步决策的动态引导。

Method: 提出 Co-ReAct 框架，在推理期间向智能体注入步骤级准则作为引导；训练了一个专门的准则生成器（Rubric Generator），并采用 GRPO（组相对策略优化）进行训练，其奖励函数基于专家共识排名的列表式 Spearman 等级相关性进行优化，以增强准则的判别力。

Result: 在 DeepResearchBench 和 SQA-CS-V2 基准测试中，Co-ReAct 在 8B/14B 开源模型及领先闭源模型上均显著超越了基准模型，且生成的准则生成器可作为即插即用组件提升现有智能体性能。

Conclusion: Co-ReAct 证明了通过将质量准则（Rubrics）作为推理过程中的步骤级引导，可以显著克服传统 ReAct 智能体在多步搜索推理任务中的局限性，实现更精准的任务执行。

Abstract: ReAct-style agents for search-intensive, multi-step reasoning tasks rely largely on their own internal judgment to decide what evidence to seek, which reasoning or action step to take next, and when to stop, often producing shallow, redundant, or poorly targeted trajectories. Prior work has explored rubrics as external quality signals, but existing uses are mostly evaluative rather than action-guiding: rubrics typically serve as training-time rewards or post-hoc evaluators of completed outputs, and in deep-research settings they are often coarse-grained and report-level rather than step-level. We introduce Co-ReAct, a rubric-guided action-selection framework that uses rubrics as step-level guidance during inference. At each decision step, Co-ReAct injects a rubric into the agent's context to guide the next Reason-or-Act decision, specifying what the agent should target in evidence seeking, search, reasoning, or self-evaluation. To make this guidance reliable, we train a dedicated rubric generator with GRPO. Unlike prior pairwise or binary preference formulations, our objective optimizes a list-wise Spearman rank-correlation reward against multi-judge expert consensus rankings, encouraging rubrics that are discriminative rather than merely plausible. On DeepResearchBench and SQA-CS-V2, Co-ReAct consistently improves over ReAct and representative test-time compute baselines across search agents built on both 8B/14B open-source and frontier closed-source base models. The trained rubric generator can also serve as a drop-in component that improves these baselines without changing their underlying decision mechanisms. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZBWpro/Co-ReAct.

</details>


### [200] [Solving the Aircraft Disassembly Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23592)
*Charles Thomas,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究针对飞机报废拆解的复杂调度问题，提出并对比了约束规划（CP）和混合整数规划（MIP）两种优化模型，旨在通过高效的调度安排平衡多重物理与人员约束，从而提升拆解过程的经济可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞机报废拆解虽对可持续发展至关重要，但利润空间微薄。为提升盈利能力和推广该业务，必须高效安排拆解任务以应对包括人员资质限制、工序先后顺序、飞机重心平衡以及作业空间受限等多重复杂约束。

Method: 针对大规模飞机拆解调度问题，构建了约束规划（Constraint Programming, CP）模型和混合整数规划（Mixed-Integer Programming, MIP）模型。基于工业伙伴提供的实际数据，设计了不同规模的算例（最高包含1450项任务）来评估模型性能。

Result: 该研究成功建立了针对该复杂问题的数学模型架构，并在包含多达1450项任务的真实案例数据上进行了测试，验证了模型在处理实际飞机拆解调度需求时的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出并评估了两种数学优化模型（CP和MIP），证明了其在解决大规模飞机拆解调度问题方面的可行性，为提高拆解过程的经济效益和可持续性提供了决策支持。

Abstract: Dismantling aircrafts reaching their end of life is a complex endeavour that is necessary in terms of sustainability but yields small income margins for air transport companies. An efficient scheduling of the disassembly procedure is thus crucial to ensure the profitability of the process and incentivize practice. This is a large scheduling problem that involves thousands of tasks and many different constraints: Extracting parts that are destined to be reused requires technicians with specific certifications and equipment. Extraction operations might be subject to precedence relations. Furthermore, the aircraft must be kept balanced during the whole process. Finally, some of the locations of the aircraft have a limited space that caps the number of technicians able to work there concurrently. This article presents the problem in details and proposes two approaches to solve the problem: a Constraint Programming model and a MIP model. The models are tested on instances of varying sizes involving up to 1450 tasks, which are based on real operational data provided by an industrial partner.

</details>


### [201] [One Policy, Infinite NPCs: Persona-Traceable Shared RL Policies for Scalable Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23652)
*Yoosung Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PCSP 是一种高效的 NPC 控制框架，利用共享强化学习策略和人格嵌入，实现了在大规模游戏场景中高性能、高一致性且可控的人格化 NPC 行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NPC 控制方法难以在保持人格一致性、可控性和实时推理性能三者之间取得权衡，无法满足大规模游戏（数百至数千 NPC）的需求。

Method: 提出 Persona Conditioned Shared Policy (PCSP)，采用单一强化学习策略，结合冻结的 LLM 嵌入表示；具体技术包括一次性 NPC 人格编码、低秩人格投影、神经人格条件化，以及结合 PPO、InfoNCE 一致性损失和 KL 散度多样性损失的训练目标。

Result: 在 300 人格基准测试中，PCSP 实现比随机高出 17 倍的零样本识别率，语义-行为相关性达到 0.73，推理速度比 LLM 策略基线快 22 倍；在 UE5 中可支持 64 名 NPC 的实时运行。

Conclusion: PCSP 证明了共享强化学习策略能够有效支撑大规模、实时且具备人格特征的 NPC 控制，解决了传统方法在一致性、可控性与推理速度方面的局限性。

Abstract: On a 300-persona life-simulation benchmark, pcsp achieves compositional zero-shot persona identification up to 17x above chance, Spearman rho approx 0.73 semantic-behavioral alignment, and 22x faster inference than an LLM-as-policy baseline. Life simulation games require hundreds to thousands of non-player characters (NPCs) that behave consistently with distinct personalities while remaining controllable through designer-authored natural language. Existing methods fail on constraints like persona consistency, controllability, or real-time inference. We introduce pcsp (Persona Conditioned Shared Policy), a single reinforcement learning policy conditioned on frozen LLM embeddings of free-form persona descriptions. pcsp combines once-per-NPC persona encoding, low-rank persona projection, neural persona conditioning, and a PPO + InfoNCE consistency + KL diversity training objective. Across three experimental settings, ablations show that the InfoNCE trajectory-consistency objective is load bearing: removing it collapses zero-shot persona identification to chance. External validation on Melting Pot 2.4.0 substrates confirms that our method produces persona-conditioned behavioral divergence in multi-agent strategic environments. We distinguish two senses of held-out evaluation: compositional zero-shot and vocabulary-expansion held-out. Finally, a UE5 deployment reproduces the in-engine persona-conditioning ablation at 64 agents with a low failure rate, showing that the sub-frame inference profile survives in a commercial game engine. These results prove that shared RL policies can support scalable, real-time, persona-conditioned NPC control.

</details>


### [202] [Agentic Proving for Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23772)
*Alessandro Sosso,Akhil Arora,Bas Spitters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，Claude Code 利用智能体证明框架在程序验证任务中表现卓越，但也揭示了现有评估基准已不足以测试当前最先进模型的性能，亟需更严格、更具鲁棒性的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探讨当前智能体系统在自动定理证明领域的成功经验，是否能有效迁移至程序验证任务，并评估其在生成可验证代码方面的性能。

Method: 在 CLEVER（一个基于 Lean 4 的程序验证基准测试）上评估 Claude Code。该评估框架采用智能体证明范式，通过“编译器在循环中（compiler-in-the-loop）”的方式进行代码自动生成、验证，并利用智能体自我反思机制识别错误。

Result: Claude 在 CLEVER 基准上表现优异：规格说明生成准确率高达98.8%（81.3%通过同构评分），实现验证成功率为87.5%，端到端管线成功率达98.1%。此外，Claude 在自我反馈和错误诊断方面也展现出极高水平。

Conclusion: 当前的程序验证基准测试难以衡量现代智能体（Agentic）证明器的能力；现有的基于同构（isomorphism-based）的评分机制存在局限，需要开发更严谨的评估方法；“紧密循环的编译器反馈”范式是目前进行基础程序验证最有效的方法。

Abstract: Agentic systems have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for automated theorem proving in formal mathematics. To assess how far these capabilities extend to program verification, we evaluate Claude Code in an agentic proving framework on CLEVER, a Lean 4 benchmark for verifiable code generation. Our results show that Claude generates arguably valid specifications for 98.8% of problems (with 81.3% also accepted by CLEVER's isomorphism-based scoring on the correct portion of the benchmark), certifies implementations against correct ground-truth specifications for 87.5% of problems, and reaches a 98.1% success rate on the end-to-end program generation and verification pipeline over entries with self-consistent premises. Across all stages, Claude further provides high-quality feedback on its own attempts (as confirmed under manual review), identifying underlying causes of failure and lingering bugs in the dataset. These findings highlight a growing mismatch between the difficulty of existing program verification benchmarks and the capabilities of modern agentic provers, and point to the need for more rigorous, bug-resilient evaluation methodologies, and in particular for alternatives to isomorphism-based scoring of generated specifications. More broadly, our results provide empirical evidence that tight compiler-in-the-loop agentic paradigms are currently the most effective approach for foundational program verification.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Binary Edits Robust Multimodal Knowledge Editing with Adversarial Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23780)
*Haoyuan Wang,Xiaohao Liu,Jiajie Su,Jianmao Xiao,Chaochao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态大模型知识编辑泛化能力弱的问题，本文提出ASAM框架，通过知识单元建模、潜在对抗鲁棒化（LAR）和秩约束子空间学习（RCSL），实现了在语义等价多模态输入下稳定、鲁棒的知识更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态知识编辑方法虽然具备良好的可靠性和局部性，但在泛化能力上存在不足，难以将编辑效果传播至具有不同视觉和语言表现形式的语义等价样本中。其根本原因在于缺乏明确的语义监督、编辑范围过于僵化以及在高维空间中过度锚定于单一特定样本。

Method: 1. 建立知识单元（Knowledge Units）：对语义等价的多模态输入进行分组；2. 潜在对抗鲁棒化技术（LAR）：在联合潜空间中生成语义一致但具有对抗性的变体，以暴露模型脆弱的语义区域；3. 秩约束子空间学习（RCSL）：通过基于奇异值的目标函数，在编辑层强制执行对抗表征的低秩对齐。

Result: 实验结果表明，ASAM相比现有基线方法，在保持模型原有能力不退化的前提下，显著提升了知识编辑在多模态变体间的泛化一致性。

Conclusion: 通过引入知识单元建模和协同对抗优化，ASAM有效地提高了多模态大模型在知识编辑中的泛化能力，确保编辑效果能在语义一致的变体中稳定传播，同时保持了良好的局部性和可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need efficient mechanisms to update knowledge without degrading existing capabilities. While intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing achieves strong reliability and locality, it often exhibits limited generality, failing to propagate edits across semantically equivalent visual and linguistic variations. This issue arises from the lack of explicit semantic supervision, rigid editing scopes, and biased anchoring to individual samples in high-dimensional multimodal spaces. We address robust intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing by explicitly targeting generalization. We formalize robustness through knowledge units that group semantically equivalent multimodal inputs and define generality as consistent predictions within each unit. To expose fragile semantic regions, we introduce Latent Adversarial Robustification (LAR), which generates adversarial yet semantically coherent variants in the joint latent space. We further propose Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning (RCSL), enforcing low-rank alignment of adversarial representations at the edit layer via a singular value-based objective. Extensive analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of ASAM empirically.

</details>


### [204] [SPACENUM: Revisiting Spatial Numerical Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23898)
*Jianshu Zhang,Yijiang Li,Huifeixin Chen,Haoran Lu,Letian Xue,Bingyang Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过 SpaceNum 框架评估发现，目前的 VLMs 在处理空间数值任务时本质上缺乏真正的空间理解，主要依赖浅层 cues，在涉及动态和静态空间推理时表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在具身环境（如动作幅度、空间坐标）中输出数值时，其背后的空间感知底层逻辑尚不明晰，需重新审视这些模型是否真正具备空间数值理解能力。

Method: 提出了一个统一框架 SpaceNum，包含 Num2Space 和 Space2Num 两个双向任务，通过错误分析、推理轨迹分析及受控干预等方法，深入评估模型对空间数值的理解能力。

Result: 实验表明，当前主流模型在空间数值任务上表现接近随机猜测；它们难以构建稳定的坐标感知表示，且缺乏从视觉观察中抽象出结构化空间布局的能力；显式推理带来的性能增益有限。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型（VLMs）在空间数值理解方面能力较弱，往往依赖浅层视觉暗示而非真正的空间推理。尽管微调可以带来一定程度的改善，但现有模型在处理动态转换和静态布局时仍面临严峻挑战。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in embodied environments, where they need produce numerical outputs such as action magnitudes and spatial coordinates. Although these numbers appear meaningful, it remains unclear whether these numerical outputs are genuinely grounded in spatial perception. Therefore, in this work, we revisit spatial numerical understanding through SpaceNum, a unified framework that captures two complementary settings: numbers as dynamic transitions during spatial exploration, and numbers as static layouts in spatial reasoning. We formulate two bidirectional tasks, Num2Space and Space2Num, to evaluate how well VLMs map between vision-side spatial structure and language-side numerical representations. We systematically study whether current VLMs truly understand numerical values in spatial settings. Across dynamic transitions and static layouts, we find that models largely fail to ground numbers in spatial meaning and often perform close to random guess. Through error analysis, reasoning trace analysis, and controlled interventions, we show that current VLMs rely heavily on shallow spatial cues, struggle to build stable coordinate-aware representations, and fail to abstract structured spatial layouts from visual observations. We further show that explicit reasoning provides only marginal gains, while tuning can partially improve spatial numerical understanding and transfer to external spatial reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [205] [From Raw Experience to Skill Consumption: A Systematic Study of Model-Generated Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23899)
*Zisu Huang,Jingwen Xu,Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Qihao Yang,Muzhao Tian,Xiaohua Wang,Changze Lv,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Xue Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个针对语言智能体技能全生命周期的效用评估框架，揭示了模型生成技能的迁移特性，并提出了能有效提升技能质量的“元技能”提取方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言智能体通过重用“技能”来提升能力，但缺乏对技能全生命周期的全面研究，导致对技能为何有效、何时有效以及成败因素缺乏理解。

Method: 构建了一个基于效用的评估框架，涵盖5个不同的智能体任务领域，对技能生命周期的三个阶段（经验生成、技能提取、技能消耗）进行了系统性实验与深度剖析。

Result: 模型生成的技能在平均水平上是有益的，但也存在明显的负迁移现象；提取器与消费者之间的表现不一致，即模型作为提取器强大并不代表其作为消费者同样强大。

Conclusion: 研究发现技能效用并不单纯取决于模型规模或基线任务表现。作者通过分析技能生命周期，提出了“元技能（meta-skill）”以指导技能提取，从而显著提高了技能质量并降低了负迁移。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly improve by reusing \emph{skills} -- structured procedural artifacts distilled from past experience. In particular, \emph{domain-level} and \emph{model-generated} skills are especially promising. They offer fast adaptation within a domain by encoding domain-specific recurring procedures, and they scale beyond labor-intensive hand-crafting. However, while extraction methods continue to proliferate, understanding remains limited, with no comprehensive study spanning the full skill lifecycle -- \textbf{experience generation}, \textbf{skill extraction}, and \textbf{skill consumption} -- to ask whether such skills actually work, when they work, and what makes them succeed or fail. To close this gap, we build a utility-grounded evaluation framework that provides systematic experimental results across extractors and target agents, covering five diverse agentic task domains. We find that model-generated skills are beneficial on average but exhibit non-trivial negative transfer, and that neither extractors nor targets behave uniformly. A model can be a strong extractor yet a weak consumer, or vice versa, with skill utility independent of model scale or baseline task strength. To explain these patterns, we then dissect each lifecycle stage in depth, analyzing how experience composition shapes skill quality, what properties characterize useful skills, and how the same skill transfers across different consumers. Finally, we translate these findings into a concrete \emph{meta-skill} that guides skill extraction toward the features tied to actual utility, which consistently improves skill quality across domains and substantially reduces negative transfer.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [ACALSim: A Scalable Parallel Simulation Framework for High-Performance System Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22936)
*Wei-Fen Lin,Jen-Chien Chang,Yen-Po Chen,Zi-Yi Tai,Yu-Cheng Chang,Chia-Pao Chiang,Yu-Yang Lee,Yu-Jie Wan*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ACALSim 是一款新型高性能并行仿真框架，通过可定制的线程调度和高效的内存同步机制，解决了 GPU 等复杂加速器仿真过程中存在的扩展性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构仿真已成为高性能计算系统设计空间探索的瓶颈；现有框架（如 SST、GPGPU-Sim）在单节点内部并行扩展能力不足，且缺乏针对特定工作负载优化线程调度的机制。

Method: 提出 ACALSim 框架，引入：1) 可插拔线程管理架构；2) 带快速前跳机制的事件驱动执行；3) 实现零拷贝通信的共享内存数据模型；4) 确定性执行的两阶段并行模型。

Result: 在 HPCSim 中应用后，相较于 SST 实现了超过 14 倍的加速，内存占用降低 41%，并在处理大规模 LLaMA 模型仿真时表现出良好的扩展性和实用性，周期计数与 A100 硬件验证吻合度高。

Conclusion: ACALSim 通过提供可插拔的线程管理架构和高效的执行模型，显著提升了大规模并行处理器的仿真效率，填补了现有仿真框架在架构设计空间探索方面的空白。

Abstract: Architectural simulation has become the critical bottleneck limiting design space exploration for high-performance computing systems. Modern GPUs and AI accelerators -- with hundreds to thousands of tightly-coupled components -- demand simulation frameworks that deliver efficient parallelism and scalable single-node execution. Existing frameworks fall short: SST focuses on multi-node MPI scalability but struggles with intra-node scaling, while GPGPU-Sim remains largely single-threaded. Critically, none expose a mechanism for users to optimize threading for their specific workloads. We introduce ACALSim, a scalable parallel simulation framework providing infrastructure and APIs for building high-performance simulators -- timing-model accuracy remains the responsibility of simulator developers. Its key innovation is a pluggable thread-management architecture that lets developers implement custom scheduling strategies tailored to specific simulation patterns, absent in existing frameworks. Complementing it are (1) event-driven execution with fast-forward to eliminate idle-cycle overhead, (2) a shared-memory data model enabling zero-copy communication, and (3) a two-phase parallel execution model for deterministic thread scaling. We demonstrate ACALSim through HPCSim, a GPU simulator targeting A100-class architectures. Against an SST implementation using identical shared timing cores to isolate framework overhead, ACALSim achieves over 14x speedup with 41% lower memory footprint; hardware validation confirms 0.72--1.22x cycle-count correlation with A100 measurements. While SST fails to complete 256+ thread-block workloads within practical time limits, ACALSim simulates full LLaMA transformer layers (single block) in 17.7 minutes for LLaMA-7B and 30.4 minutes for LLaMA-13B -- enabling design space exploration that SST cannot achieve.

</details>


### [207] [MASQ: Accelerating Masked Diffusion via Stage-Wise Multi-Precision Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23226)
*Seeyeon Kim,Jaehun Lee,Sungyeob Yoo,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MASQ 是一种专为掩码扩散模型设计的软硬件协同加速器，通过动态多精度分配和计算调度，有效解决了处理过程中的计算冗余，显著提升了生成速度与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型（Masked Diffusion）在进行区域特定图像合成时，尽管只需要生成掩码区域，但现有方法在每个时间步仍处理整张图像，导致严重的计算冗余。

Method: 提出了一种硬件-软件协同设计方案，包括：1. 阶段性的 MXINT8/4/2 精度分配以反映空间和语义重要性；2. 时间步感知调度；3. 优化非矩阵运算；4. 定制化硬件架构，包含区块级多精度计算引擎和掩码管理单元。

Result: 与 A100 和 Orin NX 相比，MASQ 分别实现了高达 16.06 倍和 5.39 倍的加速，以及 4.18 倍和 4.93 倍的能效增益，且未牺牲生成质量。

Conclusion: MASQ 是一个软硬件协同设计的加速器，通过在掩码扩散模型中实现动态精度分配和高效的任务调度，在保持图像生成质量的同时，相较于 A100 和 Orin NX 实现了显著的性能提升和能效改善。

Abstract: Masked diffusion enables region-specific image synthesis but suffers from computational redundancy, since the entire image is processed each timestep even though only the masked region requires generation. To address this, we introduce MASQ, a hardware-software co-designed accelerator for masked diffusion. Our approach performs stage-wise MXINT8/4/2 precision assignment that dynamically reflects spatial and semantic importance, complemented by timestep-aware scheduling and optimized non-matrix operations. MASQ features a block-wise multi-precision compute engine and mask management unit, efficiently handling our approach. It achieves up to 16.06x and 5.39x speedup and 4.18x and 4.93x energy-efficiency gain over A100 and Orin NX, respectively, while preserving quality.

</details>


### [208] [NASiC: 3D NAND-based CAM-Selected Multibit CIM Architecture for Efficient On-Device Mixture-of-Experts LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23294)
*Weikai Xu,Meng Li,Shuzhang Zhong,Tianyang Luo,Dongxue Zhao,Ling Liang,Zongwei Wang,Qianqian Huang,Yimao Cai,Ru Huang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了NASiC架构，将CAM引导的专家选择与CIM专家计算融合，优化了3D NAND在MoE模型推理中的并行计算能力与能效，实现了大幅度的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘端部署时面临存储空间占用大、3D NAND计算存内（CIM）架构对动态稀疏激活支持不佳导致并行度下降，以及Flash存储单元多位能力利用率不足的问题。

Method: 提出NASiC（3D NAND-based content addressable-selected CIM）架构，利用3D NAND的串联结构，通过CAM（内容可寻址存储器）掩码机制实现动态专家选择与CIM专家计算在单周期内的融合；并结合电路级优化和多位（multibit）CIM单元协同设计，实现块级并行计算与原位有符号多位运算。

Result: 相比现有技术方案，NASiC在性能上实现了4-114.8倍的提升，能效比提升了3.9-70倍，并保持了高质量的模型推理精度。

Conclusion: NASiC架构通过硬件级别的协同设计，有效解决了MoE模型在3D NAND CIM中部署的计算并行度低和存储空间浪费问题，为边缘端运行大语言模型提供了高效、高能效的解决方案。

Abstract: The Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have emerged as the state-of-the-art paradigm for scaling up large language models (LLMs) without proportionally increased computational cost. However, its on-device deployment faces a critical challenge due to the large memory requirement for storing all expert parameters. 3D NAND-based computing-in-memory (CIM) architectures uniquely offer high storage capacity and reduced data movement, while they are ill-suited for MoE models with dynamically sparse expert activation, leading to a degradation of effective computational parallelism, along with underutilization of multibit storage capability of Flash cells. In this work, we proposed a 3D NAND-based content addressable-selected CIM architecture, dubbed as NASiC, which is tailored to MoE models. By leveraging the intrinsic string structure of 3D NAND technology, NASiC fuses the dynamical expert selection through CAM-based masking mechanism and activated expert computation through CIM into a single computation cycle, eradicating redundant computation and enhancing computational parallelism. Moreover, circuit-level optimizations and multibit CIM cell are co-designed with proposed NASiC architecture, featuring block-wise parallel computation with in-situ signed multibit input and weight expansion, substantially improving the throughput and energy-efficiency of NAND CIM array, as well as the utilization of high-density 3D NAND technology for MoE models. With extensive experimental results, we demonstrate NASiC achieves 4-114.8x improved performance and 3.9-70x improved energy efficiency over state-of-the-art designs, along with high accuracy, showing its great potential for efficient on-device MoE LLM inference.

</details>


### [209] [DAE4HLS: Exposing Memory-Level Parallelism for High-Level Synthesis using Explicit Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23549)
*David Metz,Magnus Själander*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: DAE4HLS 提出了一种解耦访问-执行编程模型，通过显式管理内存访问请求与响应，解决了传统 HLS 在复杂内存模式下的性能瓶颈，实现了显著的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 HLS 工具难以自动优化复杂的内存访问模式（尤其是针对大规模数据集的不规则访问），导致其应用局限于简单的内存模式，未能充分发挥硬件加速的潜力。

Method: 提出了一种解耦访问-执行（DAE）范式，通过自定义编程模型明确分离内存请求与响应，并将其集成到 AMD Vitis HLS 工具链中，同时引入一种更适合处理不规则负载的动态 HLS 框架。

Result: 在 AMD Vitis HLS 工具链上实现了该范式，通过复用 AXI Stream 和 AXI Burst 接口，在处理不规则负载时获得了 10-79 倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入显式解耦的编程模型（DAE），DAE4HLS 有效克服了传统 HLS 在处理复杂内存访问模式时的局限性，展示了在保持 HLS 高开发效率的同时显著提升性能的潜力。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) performs well for simple memory access patterns, such as for sequential accesses that can be turned into bursts, or for memory accesses into small datasets that can be stored in scratchpads. This limits HLS to accelerating only the low-hanging fruit, where memory-level parallelism is either trivially abundant, due to simple access patterns, or latency is low, due to the small dataset. Applications with more complex access patterns on large datasets would also benefit from acceleration, and would especially benefit from the reduction in design and verification effort that HLS promises.
  In this paper, we present DAE4HLS, a decoupled access-execute (DAE) paradigm for HLS. We propose a new programming model for explicitly decoupling requests and responses, which unlocks memory-level parallelism that otherwise cannot be automatically provided by a compiler. We apply the DAE4HLS paradigm to the commercial AMD Vitis HLS toolchain and show that the existing AXI stream and AXI burst interfaces can be repurposed for explicit decoupling. We further apply the paradigm to a dynamic-HLS framework, which is better suited for handling irregular workloads as compared to statically scheduled HLS. We show that support for explicit decoupling improves the performance and achieves a total speedup of 10-79$\times$.

</details>


### [210] [To Overlay or to Customize? Revisiting Architectural Choices in Heterogeneous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23630)
*Xingzhen Chen,Shixin Ji,Zheng Dong,Peipei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统性对比自动驾驶场景下覆盖架构与定制化加速方案，指出两者的最优选择取决于模型切换频率、重配置开销及架构设计趋势，并为未来的架构部署提供了策略导向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶场景下，缺乏对于“覆盖架构（Overlay）”与“定制化加速（Customized Acceleration）”之间权衡关系的系统性部署准则。

Method: 以自动驾驶为应用场景，从部署中心视角进行系统性对比分析。评估维度包括工作负载变化、架构设计、重配置延迟及切换频率。

Result: 覆盖架构在频繁模型切换场景下表现更佳；随着位流重载开销的降低，定制化架构在能效要求高的场景中优势更突出；若覆盖架构灵活性进一步增强，其对比定制架构的优势将继续扩大。

Conclusion: 部署策略并非一成不变，而是随着技术演进（如位流重载开销降低或覆盖架构灵活性提升）而动态变化的。未来设计需综合考量工作负载特性与技术演进趋势，以平衡覆盖架构与定制加速器。

Abstract: In this work, we present a systematic study of this trade-off from a deployment-centric perspective, focusing on an autonomous driving scenario. Instead of treating overlay and customized acceleration as isolated design points, we analyze when each approach is preferable under practical conditions, including workload variation, architectural design, reconfiguration latency, and switching frequency. Our analysis shows that overlay-based architecture is more suitable for highly frequent model switching under the state-of-the-art architecture. However, as bitstream reload overhead continues to reduce, customized architectures may become increasingly attractive, especially for workloads with efficiency requirements. Conversely, if overlay architectures become more capable and flexible, they may further expand their advantage over customized architectures. These observations provide design insights for future architectural design, and the optimal deployment strategy will be flipped according to the technique development.

</details>
